# BREAKLINE — Game Design Document
*Working title. Roblox competitive arcade FPS. Last updated: Nov 10, 2026 (v5.19: METEOR SOUL DROP + FIXES - the Warp is no longer the watercolour painting + soul fly-through + white flash: your soul starts in SPACE straight above your spawn, burns down through the atmosphere like a re-entering rocket (cel-shaded fire with ink outlines, an anime layer drawn 12x a second: speed lines, inked flame tongues, ink scratches), slams into your spawn (impact frames, shockwave, dust, scorch, a big DON!), then the normal respawn reveal builds your body and the camera wooshes into your head exactly as the card roll starts (`WARP_TIME` 5.6 -> 6.0); the 20 s loadout screen is black space with stars again (the blueprint is gone) and the loadout dock is bigger (tiles 92 -> 118 px); sentries / bubble shields / prox mines can be PLACED again (the v5.18 frag-cook change had broken the click) and the place + preview reach is 20 studs (was 60); the crosshair no longer stays on screen after leaving the training zone; walking / running keeps the body stable (no bounce, side tilt or twist); new players get 3,000 souls with the starter pick; player card: LV sits LEFT of the name so the name is right next to the avatar, bigger title; the lobby `ThirdPersonClient` in the repo was still the pre-v5.18 copy and is replaced; NO card rebake (no card changed); v5.18: TRAINING ZONE + EMOTE FIXES + HANDS + GADGETS + MOVEMENT - walking out of the training zone no longer spams "Unable to assign property C0" (the places run with StarterPlayer.AvatarJointUpgrade, so the body joints are AnimationConstraints with a read-only C0 - every joint write now goes through the attachment), no longer leaves you in first person with a locked mouse and no longer leaves a floating copy of your hands + gun; lobby emotes work (PoseClient now drives AnimationConstraint joints too - that is why it printed "0 joints found"); the training zone gives you 250 health (260 with THICK SKIN), the card you pick is EQUIPPED on the HUD like in a match and gives the gun a fresh life, a new CHANGE LOADOUT button sits next to CHANGE CARD; the settings gear sits lower + further right and the panel is a tall single column; a gun is EITHER your primary OR in the reserve; first-person hands are ONE block each, both the same size, in your costume's colours, with thicker forearms, and the gadget arm is a square block with the hand at its end; sentries, bubble shields and mines go on floors / walls / ceilings / ramps and can be placed as far as the preview reaches (60 studs); the FRAG can be COOKED (the press pulls the pin, the FUSE counts down, release throws, too long = it goes off in your hand); walk 17 / sprint 32, jumps carry further, slides gain speed; a calmer run arm swing and a new athletic jump pose; NO card rebake (no card changed); v5.17: SETTINGS + TRAINING ZONE + BLUEPRINT + EMOTE FIXES + UI POLISH - a round SETTINGS gear next to the Roblox chat / mic buttons (long panel in the middle: mouse + aim sensitivity, FOV, master volume, screen shake, damage numbers, FPS + ping); a TRAINING ZONE in the lobby (any player inside the Workspace part "TrainingZone" gets first person, the in-round HUD, guns / gadgets / movement / cards exactly like a match, nobody is ever hurt, only effects + numbers; LEAVE button -> part "EntranceTrainingZone"; CHOOSE YOUR CARD = every card you own); the Breakline loadout background is a BLUEPRINT (white lines on navy, site plan of the real map on the right, briefing on the left, the middle free for the squad); poses: the lobby cursor no longer sticks in the middle, in a match your body no longer turns its back to the camera and your hands are no longer dragged to the gun grips; bigger + longer TITLE over the name in the lobby; card index / shop / cosmetics text polish; SMOKE is now SMOKE GRENADE; NO card rebake (no card changed); v5.16: POSES + TITLES + UI POLISH - new POSES (emotes): a POSES tab in the cosmetics locker with a 6-slot POSE WHEEL, T opens the wheel in a match and in the lobby (mouse direction / click / 1-6), a pose holds until you move or pull something out (it puts your item away), 10 test poses (still + animated, 3 free), sold in the shop; a costume on your player card can hold a pose (ADD POSE under REPOSITION); new TITLES above your name in the lobby + on your player card (shop + locker tab); PLAYER SKINS are called COSTUMES; gun skins say "<GUN> SKIN"; locker gun skins = one tab per gun; shop costume preview says COLORWAY FOR <base>; card shop: FIRST STEPS cover is a seedling (no more feet), "12 CARDS" + BREAKLINE readable on the packs, NEW CARDS IN next to DAILY SHOP, no light pool above SKIP ROLL, a thin anime slash, a polished CLAIM button; home CARD INDEX button fan centred + text moved right; Breakline: the loadout screen background is a MISSION BRIEFING of the map (live recon picture + briefing panel), the match starts 10 s after everybody loaded (was 15), nobody can move / shoot before the match starts, lower mouse sensitivity (`MatchConfig.MOUSE_SENS` 0.55), a gadget can no longer be in a gadget slot AND the reserve; NO card rebake (no card changed); v5.15: LOADING SCREEN FIX - the Breakline map loading screen never went away (it stayed over the whole match after the 15 s and after everybody had loaded): `LoadingScreen` used an undefined `t0` the moment the phase left Waiting, errored and stopped before it could fade out; fixed + a watchdog so it can never stay over a running match again; NO card rebake (no card changed); v5.14: CARD SHOP + STARTER CARDS + 8 WORKING CARDS - everybody (new AND existing players) now owns 4 copies each of EMBER HEART, THICK SKIN, DEEP POCKETS, HOT BARREL, GRAVE CANDLE, SCOUT'S EYE, HOLLOW POINT and GLASS CANNON (= one full 32-card deck), and those 8 cards really work in a match; the shop has a new CARDS tab with its own layout: a DAILY SHOP (3 big cards, re-picked every day at 00:00 UTC by rarity weight from the cards inside the packs on sale) and CARD PACKS (FIRST STEPS 300, SOUL PACK 750, LEVEL UP 1,500 souls, 3 per row, each with its own cover art + an EYE button that shows the 12 cards inside and every chance); buying a pack plays the opening (dark room, spotlight, anime slash rips the top off, the cards spin round in a circle and land on one, CLAIM; SKIP ROLL while it spins); the pack + daily cards are all the new PLACEHOLDER card for now (shown in each slot's rarity); bigger DECK title in the Card Index; the play-screen CARD INDEX button's card fan moved right + up, its text moved over and the DECK line is bigger; the pre-game loadout menu no longer closes on every countdown second; NO card rebake (the 32 baked cards did not change, the placeholder is drawn live); v5.13: SHOWCASE REPOSITION + EDITOR HANDLE FIX + CLIMB + ICONS - a REPOSITION button under every showcase slot opens a big view where a skin / gun skin is turned any way (drag, like the shop preview, just the model, no pad) and a power-up card leans a little left / right / up / down (up to 15 degrees, a real perspective picture from the baked card sheet); DONE (bottom middle) saves the angle and the item shows at that angle on the player card; the card editor's corner squares (resize) and round handle (turn) work now (they used to just move the item); the map loading picture is a still image at the smallest size that still fills the screen (no slow zoom / drift, no push-in on exit); LV + level number on the player card a bit bigger; every movement ability icon has a solid figure doing the move on top of the old line drawing; wall climb stays flat on its stomach through inside corners (wall -> ceiling, wall -> wall at an angle), round outside edges and ceiling -> wall (no more sideways hanging), and the body sits closer to the surface; NO card rebake (the cards did not change); v5.12: PLAYER CARD EDITOR + LOBBY POLISH - the player card has a new layout (small avatar top right, display name with a small LV right next to it, the real @username under it, tags top left, no EDIT CARD button, no dark boxes behind the showcase items) and the 3 showcase items can be MOVED, RESIZED and TURNED on the card in the cosmetics PLAYER CARD tab (the info always sits on top); picking a showcase item has three tabs (PLAYER SKINS / GUN SKINS / CARDS); the cosmetics shop shows ONE shop full screen with the other as a slim side tab (essence first, always); the preview no longer closes after you turn a model; nothing blinks when you click in the shop / locker any more; styled currency plate on the home screen; COSMETICS / INVITE labels under the home buttons; the small line under PLAY is back (home: BREAKLINE // 3V3, play screen: SELECT GAMEMODE); READY UP centred + bigger and it closes the game modes screen; CARD INDEX moved next to the RESERVE tiles; the card index (?) is just a ring with a bigger centred ?; dash pips no longer cover the word DASH; the lobby crown sits right above the head with the xN outside it; v5.11: LOBBY PLAY SCREEN REBUILT, PLAYER CARDS, READY UP, INVITE SCREEN, COLOURWAYS IN THE LOCKER, BUG FIXES - the play screen has PLAYER CARDS on the left (yours on top + 2 party slots: banner background, avatar, name, level, PARTY LEADER / READY tags, 3 showcase slots to flex a skin / gun skin / card), the podiums moved right (the right one above PLAY / INVITE) with PROMOTE / KICK on the front of a teammate's podium, the GAME MODES tab + the squad slots are gone, PLAY opens a big GAME MODES screen with READY UP on every mode (solo = searches at once, a party searches once everybody is ready for the leader's mode), the + button opens its own INVITE screen, the locker lists only the 3 base skins with a colourway swatch row, new PLAYER CARD locker tab + PLAYER CARDS shop tab (10 code-drawn backgrounds, 7 animated), drag-to-turn in the cosmetics viewers really works, the home-screen souls icon is the ghost + the currencies sit on a dark plate with bigger names, less filler text; FIXES: prox mine never went off (height test bug), the pre-game reserve could hold one item in every slot, the map loading picture was never found for "testing grounds_map", the wall run only starts while SPACE is held next to a wall; v5.10: COSMETICS SHOP + LOCKER, GUN SKINS, REBUILT SKINS, WIN-STREAK CROWN, WIN LINE-UP, NEW CURRENCY ICONS, LOADING FIXES - the three player skins are rebuilt with real gear detail (OPERATOR 160 parts, SOULBOUND 154, CHASSIS 118) + eight shop colourways; a COSMETICS SHOP button under SHOP (PLAYER SKINS / GUN SKINS tabs, an ESSENCE half and a SOULS half, drag-to-turn preview before buying) and a COSMETICS LOCKER (hanger button next to PLAY: own menu, PLAYER SKINS + GUN SKINS tabs, says which gun a skin goes on); 40 gun skins (4 per gun); a gold crown with xN floats over players on a win streak in the lobby; the win screen is a line-up of the winning team holding their guns with crowns (no thrones); souls icon = a little ghost, essence icon = a faceted gem; the loading screen picture now loads for real (thumbnail fallback) and stays 15 s; the Warp camera trails the soul; the lobby loading bar is centred; v5.9: MAP SPAWNS, WATERCOLOUR WARP, ESSENCE, HUD + LOBBY POLISH - every map now has its own RedSpawn / BlueSpawn markers (one or many per team), the Warp is a soul flying INTO the map's watercolour painting and through a 3D watercolour version of the real map (no run-in, slow white flash, card roll straight after), new TESTING GROUNDS painting drawn from the real map, smoother soul trail, second currency ESSENCE (premium, purple glimmers) next to the souls, HUD without dark plates + weapon name under the ammo + reload ring at the crosshair + fitted slot pictures, bold loadout captions, card hover no longer covers the rarity name, wall climb onto a ceiling faces the ceiling, clear BACK / CLOSE buttons, shop cart icon, fixed hanger icon, filler text removed; v5.8: MAP LOADING SCREEN, BOT DAMAGE FIX, WALL RUN UP + DIAGONALS, CLEAN CARD ROLL, SQUAD RAISED - the loading screen is now the picked map's own watercolour + pencil sketch, full screen, with the map name big bottom right and the progress along the bottom (`MatchConfig.MAPS.Info`, first picture: TESTING GROUNDS); bots were unhittable for ~3 s after a 1v1 round reset while the shooter still saw damage numbers (fixed + the server now only reports damage that really came off); wall run UP keeps a steady speed until the stamina is empty (no 1.15 s fade-out) and the camera pitch angles any wall run up / across / down; the black reel window, black card corners and grey boxes behind the picked / unpicked cards are gone; the pre-game squad sits ~70 px higher; v5.7: HIT FLASH, SOUND BANK v2, BIG LOADOUT MENU, RESERVE (J), WALL RUN + CLIMB, AI BOTS - enemies pop white then wash red for a split second when shot, every gun has its own SoundBank block + a slot for every sound-worthy action, the pre-game loadout opens a big lobby-style menu (dock + squad moved lower, the faint CHOOSE YOUR LOADOUT line removed), the RESERVE really works (press J while dead), wall run latches on from the side / head-on without jumping and keeps your direction, wall climb goes wall <-> ceiling, and the queue fills with bots after 60 s; v5.6: RELOADS, ADS, HANDS, MAP FOLDERS - per-gun reloads redone (the hand really holds the mag, round-fed guns load only the missing rounds one at a time, mag guns are slower), pistols sit lower + further out only while aiming, plain block hands, M249 cover no longer opens through the sight, one folder of maps per game mode picked at random, slick loadout clock + map name, card roll cards fill their frames; v5.5: GUN FEEL + SKINS v10 + THE CHASSIS ROBOT SKIN - Rivals-style gunplay (only the AK-47 has true recoil, every other gun is "Kick": the bullet goes exactly where the crosshair was while the gun + camera still kick), stylish draw / ADS / sprint / reload-snap animations, softer hitmarker, OPERATOR + SOULBOUND simplified (about 90 parts each, no gear clutter), new CHASSIS robot skin (73 parts); v5.4: SKIN POLISH + GUNS VISIBLE IN THE MENUS - Skins v9: slim glove block hands, a new showcase gun hold across the chest so the gun is no longer buried under the hands (lobby skin catalog, squad / party stage, match cutscenes), chamfered body blocks, cleaner more coherent OPERATOR + SOULBOUND (tighter palettes, no random tilts, no noise bits), new SKIN QUALITY STANDARD (5.4d) that every sellable skin must pass; v5.3: BACK TO BLOCKY - Skins v8 = pure Studio code again: classic blocky body, refined block outfits (OPERATOR helmet + goggles + plate carrier, SOULBOUND hood + bone mask + coat), BLOCK HANDS on the character, the lobby figure and in first person (GunModels / GadgetModels), joint fillers; the mesh-skin loader + Blender salvager are dropped; v5.2: MESH SKINS - Skins v7 wears an authored mesh from ReplicatedStorage.SkinModels.<id> (Blender / Meshy), first OPERATOR salvager mesh built in Blender (59 pieces, ~37k tris), old code outfit = fallback; v5.1: SKINS GO AUTHORED - every skin becomes a skinned mesh rigged to R15 (artist / Blender, imported with the 3D Importer), new OPERATOR concept = armored salvager (hood, goggles + respirator, salvaged left shoulder plate with the red slash, scrap-frame backpack), production brief + AI prompt in 5.4, no code changed; v5.0: SKIN ANATOMY KIT - one shared body kit for every skin (lofted tubes, ball joints on the REAL R15 pivots, shoulder yoke), OPERATOR + SOULBOUND rebuilt as outfits on it, Skin Style Bible + offline test rig (tools/skintest); v4.9: PLAYER MODEL OVERHAUL - sculpted bodies from stretched ellipsoids, real faces + hair, new OPERATOR / SOULBOUND outfits, no team lights on skins, lobby figure = exactly the in-game model; TEAM + PARTY OUTLINES through walls (TeamOutline); EPIC rarity (purple) + DIVINE = watercolour rainbow; STRONGER THROWS (fast, light in-flight gravity, underhand on right click) + new hold / throw animations; CARD INDEX lag fixed for real (the grid was building animated cards) + baked card sprite sheets (10 GUI objects a card instead of ~600); v4.8: FULL ART PASS - every gun + every gadget remodelled (real hand rests, no glowing team parts, nothing floating), new rounded player body with fingered gloves, ONE base skin (OPERATOR) + ONE soul skin (SOULBOUND), card page no longer lags (only the cards on screen exist, shared card drawing cache); v4.7 shop / dash / 250 health; v4.4 card index deck grid; v4.3: POWER-UP CARD SYSTEM - CARD INDEX page in the lobby (collection + 32-card deck, max 4 copies, rules popup), 32 placeholder cards in 7 rarities with hand-inked art drawn from GUI frames (CardArt), slot-machine card roll + 10 s pick after the run-in (new phase Cards), picked card on the HUD; v4.2: smaller gadgets in hand, cleaner warp flight (no balls), tiny ghost souls, 1v1 selectable in the lobby with a scrolling GAME MODES list (small name cards, one big card); v4.1: equip-then-throw/place gadgets with throw line + blue/red placement preview, real gadget models + pictures, wall-run spin fix, walk/run/crouch/slide animations, richer warp flight + no flash before the countdown, 1v1 mode, new respawn reveal (camera behind + above, rapid dive back in), death screen (15 s spectate / 10 s team wipe free roam), souls you can carry / throw / revive (8 s); v4.0: wall run + wall climb rebuilt (auto-start, body animation, vault), 6 real gadgets (frag, smoke, flashbang, sentry, bubble shield, prox mine), lobby = full movement kit and NO weapons / third person; v3.8 / v3.9: 4 real movement abilities (double jump, mega jump, wall run, wall climb), player levels + XP bar, respawn soul-reveal, lobby gun + nameplates, queue indicator on the lobby PLAY block, cursor fix on the loadout / summary screens, smoother logo + loading exit, calmer figure bob, static skin poses; v3.7: tighter ADS, smaller + fully connected gun models, idle moves only on the lobby squad stage, clean inspect / low-ready poses, first-person run-in, louder 5-second countdown, new slim logo with flowing red slash, lobby loading screen, invite popup fix; v3.6: pre-game dock fix, parallel run-in, slow-motion round end, winner thrones, bigger galaxy + new idle moves; v3.5: loading screen + pre-game loadout screen + warp-in cutscene + countdown + winner cutscene + souls summary screen; v3.4: lobby layout pass - GAME MODES tab on the left, loadout dock back at the bottom, round skins + invite buttons around PLAY, hover grow, galaxy + ringed planet behind the squad; v3.3: CLEAN lobby UI - flat dark on-brand look, squad on three pillars in starry space, loadout column on the stage, party row back at the bottom, static logo, new soul icon; v3.2: POP lobby UI (rounder, bouncier, flowy), taller + skinnier player body with a bigger world gun, polished match HUD; v3.1: ARC Raiders-style lobby, skins, FINALS-style match HUD, lobby gun only, no sensitivity changes; v3.0: new lobby UI + loadout, souls currency, sprint / damage / death-soul fixes; v2.9: left hand lowered onto the gun; v2.8: lobby + party + matchmaking + shared profile, two new script-placement rules; v2.7: guaranteed Red/Blue spawn, reload arm stays off screen; v2.6: reload tail fix, iron sights fixed on WASP / V9 / .44, full per-gun SoundBank; v2.5: reloads no longer move the gun across the screen, ANVIL cover fix, Finals-style sights, bullets fire through the reticle; v2.4: custom reload per gun, gun names back to M4A1 / AK-47 / FAMAS / WASP / ANVIL / LONGSHOT / HALO-50 / BREACHER / V9 / .44 Magnum).*

> **Hand-over tool (v5.0 note):** the owner asked for **the "paste dot rs" site**. Tried again on Nov 4 (v5.0): `paste.rs` still answers every upload from the AI sandbox with **400 Bad Request**. Working fallbacks tested from the sandbox: **dpaste.org** (`curl -d "content=..." https://dpaste.org/api/` -> a URL, add `/raw`), **bpa.st** (`curl -X POST -d "code=...&expiry=1day&lexer=text" https://bpa.st/` follows a 302 to the paste; add `/raw/<id>`). Images: **tmpfiles.org** (`curl -F file=@x.png https://tmpfiles.org/api/v1/upload`, expires in about an hour) and **uguu.se** (`-F files[]=@x.png https://uguu.se/upload`, expires in a few hours). Down / unusable on Nov 4: catbox, litterbox, 0x0.st, hastebin (needs a token), freeimage.host (the old key is rejected). Try paste.rs first next time; if it works again, use it. **Nov 4 (v5.3):** paste.rs still answers 400 to every upload; bpa.st used (`--data-urlencode "code@file"`, expiry 1week), every upload checked byte for byte.
>
> **Nov 5 (v5.5) re-test:** `paste.rs` answers every upload with **400 Bad Request** again (tried plain POST, `Content-Type: text/plain`, `-F file=@`, `-T`); **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw text at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`) and **dpaste.org** (`--data-urlencode "content@file" https://dpaste.org/api/` -> a URL, add `/raw`) both work. A sandbox without `git` / `unzip` can still read the repo: `curl -L https://codeload.github.com/BlubberKing/breakline/zip/refs/heads/main` + Python `zipfile`. `luau`, `luau-compile` and `luau-analyze` come from the Luau GitHub release (`luau-ubuntu.zip`, extract with Python `zipfile`); the offline skin check lives in `tools/skinqc/` (see 5.4).
>
> **Nov 6 (v5.8) re-test:** `paste.rs` still answers **400 Bad Request** (`--data-binary`, `Content-Type: text/plain`). **bpa.st** used again (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. The sandbox runs **Python 3.14 + NumPy 2.2**: NumPy's temporary elision can overwrite a big local array used as `a - b` inside a function (the art script keeps an extra reference / uses `np.subtract`). Map art is rendered by `tools/art/testing_grounds.py` (procedural, Python + Pillow + SciPy).
>
> **Nov 6 (v5.9) re-test:** `paste.rs` still answers **400 Bad Request** (`--data-binary`). Code went to **bpa.st** again (raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`, every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte); the two pictures went to an image host (links expire within hours - download them right away).
>
> **Nov 7 (v5.10) re-test:** `paste.rs` still answers **400 Bad Request** (`--data-binary`, `Content-Type: text/plain`). **bpa.st** used again (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release; the repo was read with `curl -L https://codeload.github.com/BlubberKing/breakline/tar.gz/refs/heads/main` + `tar` (no `git`). The offline skin check now also lives in a small Luau stand-in + Python renderer (see 5.4 v11).
>
> **Nov 7 (v5.12) re-test: paste.rs is UNRELIABLE.** For a few minutes it accepted uploads sent with an explicit text header (`curl -H "Content-Type: text/plain" --data-binary @file https://paste.rs/` -> `201` + URL; without the header: 400), but only up to ~80 KB (88 KB+ -> `500`), and minutes later it answered **400 / 503 even for a one-line test**. So every file went to **bpa.st** again (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. Next time: try paste.rs WITH the `Content-Type: text/plain` header first (files under ~80 KB), fall back to bpa.st. Repo read with `git clone` (+ `git fetch --unshallow` to read old versions of a file).
>
> **Nov 7 (v5.13) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** to every upload again (a one-line test with `-H "Content-Type: text/plain" --data-binary`, the same without the header, and `-F file=@`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release (`luau-ubuntu.zip`). The new ability icons were checked with a small Python + Pillow renderer of the same bar geometry; the climb corner maths with a NumPy stand-in (wall -> ceiling and ceiling -> wall).
>
> **Nov 7 (v5.14) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** again (a one-line test, with and without `-H "Content-Type: text/plain"`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release; the card shop screen + pack opening were run end to end in a small offline Roblox GUI stand-in (Luau), the pack odds / daily picks / starter deck in plain Luau.
>
> **Nov 8 (v5.15) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** again (a one-line test, with and without `-H "Content-Type: text/plain"`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release; the loading screen was run end to end (old + new file) in a small offline Roblox stand-in with a virtual clock and the server's phase timeline (Waiting -> hold -> Pregame -> Warp -> Playing).

> **Nov 8 (v5.16) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** again (a one-line test, with and without `-H "Content-Type: text/plain"`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte. Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release (no unknown non-Roblox names in any changed file); the Poses module was run in plain Luau (every pose sampled 0 - 3 s, wheel cleaning, ownership); the pose angles were checked with a small NumPy forward-kinematics script (hand / elbow positions against the head).
>
> **Nov 8 (v5.17) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** again (a one-line test, with and without `-H "Content-Type: text/plain"`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte (**do not upload in parallel with ONE shared temp file for the check - that made half of the checks fail once; use a temp file per upload**). Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release (no unknown non-Roblox names in any changed file); the `Settings` module was run in plain Luau (defaults, clamping, untouched `Sens`). The training zone, the blueprint, the settings panel and the pose fixes could NOT be run (they need Roblox) - they are checked for syntax and names only.
>
> **Nov 9 (v5.18) re-test:** `paste.rs` answered **400 Bad Request** again (a one-line test, with and without `-H "Content-Type: text/plain"`). Every file went to **bpa.st** (`curl -i --data-urlencode "code@file" -d expiry=1week -d lexer=text https://bpa.st/` -> `302 Location: /<id>`, raw at `https://bpa.st/raw/<id>`), every upload downloaded again and compared byte for byte (one temp file per upload). Repo read with `git clone --depth 1`; `luau-compile` / `luau-analyze` from the Luau GitHub release (no unknown non-Roblox names in any changed file). The C0 / emote bug was traced with the Roblox API reference: `AnimationConstraint.C0` / `C1` are read-only aliases of `Attachment0.CFrame` / `Attachment1.CFrame`, and `AnimationConstraint.Transform` exists like `Motor6D.Transform`. Nothing here could be run in Roblox.

> **Note to any AI reading this:** This file plus the `src/` folder is the source of truth.
> Items marked **PROPOSAL** are ideas, not approved — ask the owner before building on them.
> Items marked **TBD** are intentionally undecided and will be hashed out during development.

> update this file (including section 11 Current Status + the date at the top) and send the owner the
> **complete new GDD.md** in your reply, so they can replace the file in the repo. No exceptions.
>
> and reply with just the links, one per file, labelled with the repo path it replaces/creates, plus a short summary.
>
> **RULE — SAY WHICH GAME + WHICH SCRIPT TYPE:** There are now TWO games (Roblox places in one experience): **Breakline** (the gamemode) and the **Lobby**. Every time you hand over a file, say **which game** it goes in (Breakline / Lobby / both), the **full Studio location** (e.g. ServerScriptService), and, for every NEW script, the **type to create** (Script / LocalScript / ModuleScript, from the file ending: `.server.lua` = Script, `.client.lua` = LocalScript, `.lua` = ModuleScript). Repo folders: `src/` = Breakline, `lobby/src/` = Lobby; files that exist in both (PlaceConfig, ProfileStore) must stay identical.

---

## 1. Vision
- Arcade "supreme" gunplay in the spirit of **The Finals** and **Rivals** — fast, readable, not realistic.
- Hits must *feel* great: damage numbers popping to the side, hitmarkers, kill confirms, elimination banner.
- High polish bar on gun feel, movement and UI.
- **Roles:** AI = coder + UI designer. Owner = Studio work, map building, playtesting, publishing.

## 2. Game Modes

### 2.1 Main mode — Wall Push (locked design)
- **3v3.**
- A **middle wall** splits the map. Players can walk **and** shoot through it.
- Each team has a **spawn + finish line** at its end of the map.
- **3 holes per side** (6 total).
- **Boxes** spawn on the map. Players carry boxes and plug them into **their own side's** holes.
- The team with **more plugged boxes pushes the wall toward the enemy**. **Tie = wall stays still.**
- Enemies can **shoot plugged boxes to break them**.
- A team **loses when the wall reaches its finish line**.

**Built (v2, owner-specified, Oct 25):**
- **Layout:** 3v3 (Red vs Blue). Each team spawns on its own **finish line** at its end of the map (3 **invisible** spawn pads on the line). The wall starts in the middle.
- **The code only builds the gameplay pieces. You build and decorate the map.** `MatchServer` creates: invisible boundary walls + ceiling, the 2 finish lines, the 6 invisible team spawns, the middle wall with its holes, and the boxes. Nothing else (no floor, no cover, no lobby floor).
- **Maps (v5.6, replaces the single map in Workspace):** every game mode has its own folder of maps in **ServerStorage**: **`breaklineMaps`** and **`1v1Maps`** (names in `MatchConfig.MAPS.Folders`, case does not matter; a folder left in Workspace is moved to ServerStorage at start). Inside a mode folder, **every map is a Folder (or Model) named `<mapname>_map`**, e.g. `test_map`; the text before `_map` is the map's display name. The server picks **one map of the mode's folder at random** when the match server starts (one map = always that one, two maps = 50 / 50, three = a third each ...), **clones the whole thing into Workspace** (floor, parts, models, decoration, cover) and fits the match pieces inside **that map's own `MapBounds`** (found anywhere inside the folder). The mode comes from the lobby queue (`Roster.Mode`); in Studio it is `MatchConfig.MAPS.StudioMode` (default `Breakline`) or the Workspace attribute `TestMode` (`1v1` / `Breakline`). The old mode child on `MapBounds` is **no longer read**. If a mode has no maps, the old behaviour is the fallback (a `MapBounds` part directly in Workspace). The chosen map name is published as `MatchState.MapName` (shown on the loadout screen). **v5.8 map info:** `MatchConfig.MAPS.Info.<key>` (key = folder name without `_map`, lower case) holds `Name` (display name, e.g. `test` -> `TESTING GROUNDS`) and `Image` (the uploaded **loading picture**, `rbxassetid://<id>`; `rbxassetid://0` / empty = none yet). A map folder's own attributes **`DisplayName`** / **`LoadingImage`** (strings) win over the table. **v5.11: the key is matched loosely** - case, spaces, `_` and `-` are ignored, and an entry whose `Name` equals the map name counts too (the owner's folder `testing grounds_map` has the key `testing grounds`, the table only had `test`, so the picture was never found and the loading screen stayed dark). `MAPS.Info` now has a `["testing grounds"]` entry (and keeps `test`). Published as `MatchState.MapName` + `MatchState.MapImage`. **Delete any old loose map + `MapBounds` from Workspace once your maps are in the folders** (two maps would overlap). Example: `ServerStorage > breaklineMaps > test_map > { MapBounds, Floor, ... }`, `ServerStorage > 1v1Maps > arena_map > { MapBounds, ... }`.
- **MapBounds (how the code fits your map):** every `<name>_map` folder holds **an anchored, fully transparent Part named `MapBounds`**, sized to cover the playable area. Width = X size, Length = Z size, Height = Y size; the **bottom** of the part is the ground level. Red's end is the Front (-Z) end, Blue's the Back (+Z) end (`MAP.SwapEnds` flips this). Rotating MapBounds rotates everything. Finish lines/spawns sit `MAP.FinishInset` studs in from each end; the wall is as tall as MapBounds (max `WallHeightMax`); hole + spawn positions are fractions of the width. Resize MapBounds and everything re-fits on the next server start. If it is missing, the box around all parts of the map is used (or a 140x60x260 default at the origin) and a warning is printed.
- **Spawn markers (v5.9):** every `<name>_map` folder holds parts named **`RedSpawn`** and **`BlueSpawn`** (team name + `Spawn`). A team can have **one** marker or **several** (several parts with the same name anywhere in the folder, or a Model / Folder named `RedSpawn` holding parts). `MatchServer` hides them (transparent, no collision / touch / raycast, a `SpawnLocation` is disabled) and places every player and bot itself: **more markers than players** = spread evenly over them (1 player on 3 markers = the middle one, 2 on 5 = the 2nd and 4th), **fewer markers than players** = the extra players stand side by side on a shared marker, centred on it, `SPAWN_SPREAD` 3.6 studs apart. Order = across the map (left to right), then along it. Everyone faces the **other team's markers** and stands on the real floor under the marker (raycast). Used for the Warp spawn, mid-round respawns, 1v1 round resets and bots (`BotAPI.SpawnCF`). A map without markers for a team falls back to the old finish-line spawns (with a warning in the Output).
- **Lobby:** put a `SpawnLocation` named `LobbySpawn` in Workspace (anywhere); if none exists an invisible one is created inside MapBounds. (The Lobby is a separate place, so it never touches the map folders.)
- **Wall:** transparent (plain transparent plastic, NOT Glass material, so box faces show through); players **and bullets pass through** it. **3 indents (holes) per side, 6 total.**
- **Boxes:** look like little robots: dark steel crate, glowing edges in amber (turns **team-colored when plugged**), antenna bulb, soft light, and an **animated face on all 4 sides** (blinks; wide-eyed when carried; squeezed eyes + "O" mouth when thrown; happy when it goes in; worried when worn down or drained; panicking under 30% health). **1 box spawns on each side at round start, then 1 new box per side every 60s.** Loose boxes glow yellow. **No expiry timer.**
- **Pick up:** hold **E** (0.25s) on a loose box. **Plugging:** carry a box up to one of **your own side's** empty holes (within 7 studs) and it **floats into the hole** with a short animation + burst of sparks.
- **Stealing:** you may plug **any** box in **any** hole on your side - boxes don't belong to a specific hole. You can also **extract an enemy's plugged box**: stand on their side and **hold E (1.5s)** on it, then carry it to your side and plug it into one of your holes.
- **Plugged box health:** **5000 HP.** Enemies **shoot** plugged boxes to break them (your own team can't). A **health bar + number floats over each plugged box and is visible from either side of the wall (through the wall too).**
- **Push drain:** the team that is **currently pushing the wall** (more plugged boxes) has **all its plugged boxes lose 10 HP every second** (`BOX.PushDrainPerSec`). The bar outline turns orange while a box is draining. Tie = nobody drains.
- **Carrying:** holding a box = **no guns, no gadgets** (can't shoot/aim/reload), slightly slower. **Two-handed pose:** in **first person** a small box (smaller + lower than before so it doesn't block the screen, `CARRY_VM` in MatchConfig) is held in both hands with a bob and a throw thrust; in **third person** (what others see) the gun disappears and **both hands (IK) hold the box**, torso leans less. **Left click = throw** (decent distance), **Q = drop**.
- **Catching + throwing in:** a thrown box that gets within 6 studs of a **teammate** is caught mid-air. A thrown box that gets within 7 studs of an empty hole on its team's side floats in. Boxes pass through players so they never block anyone.
- **Pushing:** the team with **more plugged boxes pushes the wall toward the enemy**. Speed depends on the box lead (`MatchConfig.WALL_SPEED`): **1 box ahead 0.8 studs/s, 2 ahead 1.5, 3 ahead 2.1** (slowed from 1.5/3.0/4.5). **Tie = wall stays still.** The wall reaching a finish line wins the round for the other team.
- **Rounds (v3.5 flow, see 2.1b):** Waiting (loading screen) -> Pregame 20s -> Warp 6s (v5.19 meteor soul drop) -> Cards ~13s (v4.3) -> Countdown 3s -> Playing -> SlowMo 2.6s -> Result 5s -> Summary (souls screen). Teams are shuffled each round (or come from the lobby roster), extra players (7+) wait in the lobby. 5s respawn at your team's finish line.
- **Fixed:** respawning/teleporting at round start left a copy of your first-person arms + gun in the camera. Now the old viewmodel is destroyed when its character is gone and `MatchClient` sweeps any leftover every 0.5s.
- All numbers are in `MatchConfig` (ReplicatedStorage).

### 2.1b Match flow + screens (built v3.5, all in `MatchFlow` + `LoadingScreen`)
`MatchServer` drives `ReplicatedStorage.MatchState.Phase`; every screen is built on the client from that phase and its `PhaseEnds` time.
| Phase | Length | What the player sees |
|---|---|---|
| **Waiting** | until everyone loaded (max `READY_TIMEOUT` 40s, + `ROSTER_WAIT` in a real match) | **v5.16: the match starts 10 s after the LAST player has loaded (`MatchConfig.LOAD_HOLD` 15 -> 10, `LoadingScreen.MIN_SHOW` 15 -> 10); everybody is frozen (anchored) and cannot shoot until Playing (see INPUT LOCK below).** **v5.8 MAP LOADING SCREEN** (`LoadingScreen` v3, ReplicatedFirst): the picked map's **own picture fills the screen** (a watercolour + pencil sketch, `MatchState.MapImage`, Crop scaling, **v5.13: a STILL picture at exactly screen size = as zoomed out as it can be with no empty space (the slow drift / zoom from 106 % is gone)**, fades in once loaded), soft dark wash at the bottom + right so text reads. **Bottom right:** a mono mode line (`BREAKLINE  //  3V3` or `1V1  //  DUEL`) with a red tick, then the **map name in big Oswald letters** (`MapName`, max ~138 px at 1080p, scales down for long names) sliding in, a red slash drawing in under it. **Bottom left:** a big percentage + the status line (CONNECTING / LOADING MAP / LOADING PLAYER / WAITING FOR PLAYERS 3 / 6 / ALL PLAYERS LOADED). **Very bottom edge:** a full-width red progress line with a light glint. Small BREAKLINE logo top left. **v5.10: the screen stays at least 15 s (`MIN_SHOW`) AND the server holds the Waiting phase `MatchConfig.LOAD_HOLD` (15 s) after the LAST player has loaded (`MatchState.LoadHoldEnds`), so nobody misses the picture; the status line then reads `ALL PLAYERS LOADED // MATCH STARTS IN n`. The picture is loaded with `UIKit.LoadImage` (waits for `IsLoaded`, falls back to a `rbxthumb://type=Asset` link of the same id), and the link that worked is saved as Player attribute `MapImageSrc` for the Warp. A map with no picture configured prints a warning in the server Output.** **v5.15 FIX (`LoadingScreen` v3.3): the screen NEVER went away** - the end-of-loading loop tested `os.clock() - t0 >= MIN_SHOW`, but `t0` was only a local inside the logo fade task, so it was nil there; the test was skipped while the phase was Waiting and crashed the script the moment the server moved to Pregame, so the fade-out never ran and the screen (DisplayOrder 1000) sat over the whole match. Now `shownAt` (set when the screen appears) is used, the loop runs in `pcall` (an error keeps the picture up until the phase leaves Waiting, then closes it normally), a **watchdog** closes the screen at the latest `FAILSAFE` 3 s after the phase has left Waiting (once `MIN_SHOW` has passed) whatever happens, and waiting for `ClientReady` has a 30 s limit. Normal timing is unchanged: the screen fades ~1.2 s into Pregame. Exit: name slides out right, everything fades (v5.13: the picture no longer pushes in). No picture uploaded = the same layout on the dark background. *(Replaced v5.8:)* black, the **v3.7 logo** big in the middle: BREAK flies in from the left, LINE from the right, they meet and the slim red slash draws in with light flowing down it (no explosions); on exit the slash flares and the words fly apart. One line of text under it: LOADING / CONNECTING / LOADING MAP / LOADING PLAYER / WAITING FOR PLAYERS 3 / 6 / ALL PLAYERS LOADED, plus a thin progress line. The client tells the server it is loaded with `MatchRemotes.ClientReady`. |
| **Pregame** | `PREGAME_TIME` 20s | **v5.19 (owner: "make the background back to just a black background, like you are in space, like it was a while back; make the loadout at the bottom a bit bigger"): the background is BLACK SPACE again** - the dark gradient + 160 stars behind the squad exactly like v5.15 (`addStars` in `runPregame`); the v5.16 briefing / v5.17 blueprint code (`buildBriefing`) is removed. **The loadout dock is bigger:** tiles **92 -> 118 px** (`LoadoutPicker.Dock` new option `sq`; gaps, icons, tags, names and the MOVEMENT / WEAPON / GADGETS / RESERVE captions grow with it; the dead-screen RESERVE dock keeps 92), the row starts at y **936** (was 962) so it still ends at the same bottom edge, and the squad sits ~30 px higher (`PREGAME_LOOK_Y` 4.2 -> **3.8**) so the feet stay clear of it. *(Replaced v5.17:)* **v5.17 BLUEPRINT (`MatchFlow.buildBriefing` v2, owner: "try the blueprint style, line stuff up so the important stuff is on the sides, the middle is covered by the players"):** the background is a BLUEPRINT sheet: navy paper with a fine + major grid, a double border with tick marks, a soft light + two construction circles behind the squad (the only things in the middle, x 520 - 1400 of the 1920 canvas). **RIGHT column (x 1400 - 1872): SITE PLAN** = the real map (`MatchState.MapFolder`) seen straight from above as clean white outlines (every solid block = a pale fill + a white footprint outline, flat floors skipped, the play area border + a fine grid, THE LINE as a dashed cyan line, both insertion points as rings + cross tagged YOU / HOSTILE, drawn live in a ViewportFrame; the plan is turned so YOUR insertion is at the bottom), width / length dimension lines in the margins (real studs), a slow scan line, a legend under it. **LEFT column (x 56 - 466): MISSION BRIEFING** (operation, mode, squad, area, insertion + numbered NOTES that are true game facts, a REV A tag) and **OBJECTIVES** under it, every line typed in. The spy look (night vision, scan lines, REC, CLASSIFIED stamp, redacted lines) is gone. Other two directions the AI offered: DOSSIER (manila folder + stamped photo) - not built. (Older: **v5.16 MISSION BRIEFING BACKGROUND (`MatchFlow.buildBriefing`, replaces the black starfield; owner: "on brand, represents the map, not the same loading picture, like a stealth mission briefing"):** the REAL map (`MatchState.MapFolder`, the cloned `<name>_map`) is redrawn live as a **night-vision / thermal recon picture from high above** (floors dark, every block coloured by its height deep teal -> pale green, bright outlines on the tallest blocks, a faint grid, the play-area outline, **both insertion points** from the `RedSpawn` / `BlueSpawn` markers as glowing pillars + rings tagged YOUR INSERTION / HOSTILE INSERTION, **THE LINE** dashed across the middle in Breakline), the camera drifts slowly over it from your team's side; on top a **feed look** (scan lines, a slow scan band, vignette, corner brackets, blinking REC + `RECON FEED // <MAP>`, a running clock, `GRID w x l`), a dark pool behind the squad so the figures read; **MISSION BRIEFING panel (right):** OPERATION (map), MODE, SQUAD, AREA (real size in studs), INSERTION (local time), INTEL lines that are true game facts (box drop interval, 3 holes per side, boxes can be shot / stolen; 1v1: first to 5), two redacted lines, a red CLASSIFIED stamp, every line typed in; **OBJECTIVES panel (left)**. Nothing uploaded. You (middle) + teammates (left/right) stand side by side on glowing discs, names above. **Loadout dock at the bottom** (MOVE, PRIMARY, 3 GADGETS, 3 RESERVE; click a tile, pick an item; changes are validated and saved by the server via `PregameLoadout`, the gun you pick is the one you play with). **Clock at the top (v5.6: slick, NO box):** a big number, a thin team-coloured progress line under it, and **the map name right below** (`MAP` + `TEST` for `test_map`), (v5.7: the faint CHOOSE YOUR LOADOUT line behind the player names is gone). **v5.8:** the squad is raised again (~70 px at 1080p: look point 5.3 -> 4.2 `PREGAME_LOOK_Y`, camera height 4.9 -> 4.2) because the feet clipped into the loadout dock. **v5.7 layout:** the squad stood lower on the screen (camera look point 3.6 -> 5.3, camera height 4.2 -> 4.9) and the loadout dock sits lower (dock Y 905 -> 962 on the 1080 canvas, so there is room at the top). **v5.7 picker:** clicking MOVE / PRIMARY / a GADGET / a RESERVE tile opens the **BIG lobby-style menu** (`LoadoutPicker.Big`: dim backdrop, 1560 x 690 panel, grid of large cards with a picture, name, sub line and an EQUIPPED tag, ESC / click outside / CLOSE to leave, picking an item closes it) instead of the tiny horizontal scroll. **Last 5s (v3.7, louder):** the scene darkens and a **huge number slams in every second** over the squad (white 5-4, team color 3-2-1) with a shock ring, red pulses at the screen edges and the tick sound; the clock flashes red too. **v5.6:** the words MATCH STARTS IN are now the small caption above the clock number at the top of the screen (they used to sit in the middle, on the players' faces and names). The picker closes at 3. |
| **Warp** | `WARP_TIME` **6.0 s (v5.19)** | **v5.19 METEOR SOUL DROP (`MatchFlow.runWarp` v6, owner: "remove that whole sequence - no more screen flashing with the drawing of the map and the soul flying through; the soul spawns really high in the sky, like in space, directly above where the player will spawn, flies down really fast with fire on it like a rocket re-entering the atmosphere, lands on the spawn with a cool animation, then the respawn animation, and when the camera wooshes into the player the card roll starts; anime style, like an anime artist drew the stuff onto it").** Played in the REAL world with the real camera (no ViewportFrames, no painting, no white flash). Timeline (all in the `SD` table at the top of the WARP section): **0 - 0.55 s** the loadout screen fades to black. **SPACE (0.55 - 1.3 s):** your soul hangs **`SD.HEIGHT` 720 studs above your spawn**; the camera sits beside it looking down past it at the map far below; the world is darkened + cooled (a local `ColorCorrectionEffect`), a 2D star field + a black sky gradient over the top of the screen = space; the soul ignites. **RE-ENTRY (1.3 s - LAND):** the soul falls along a slightly slanted line (`SD.SLANT` 0.3, it comes in from behind your spawn's facing), accelerating (`u^2.6`, several hundred studs/s at the end); the camera CHASES it (behind + beside, pulled further back at speed, FOV 60 -> 82). The fire is **cel-shaded**: flat Neon layers (white core, yellow, orange, red; each a cylinder + a round tip, lengths follow the speed + flicker), a flattened yellow heat cap in front, 8 flame tongues that get new lengths 12x a second, a smoke ribbon (orange -> grey), embers, a PointLight - and **every fire / smoke part has a black INK OUTLINE** (a `Highlight` with no fill, `SD.INK`), which is what makes it read as a drawing. The screen edges glow orange with heat, the space look fades as it gets lower, a light blur at top speed. **ANIME LAYER (2D, on top):** redrawn **12 times a second (`SD.ANIME_FPS`, anime "on twos")** and held in between: 44 manga **speed lines** rushing out from the soul (white with an ink edge, every 4th black), 9 **inked flame tongues** (flat orange / yellow, 3 px ink stroke) trailing it on screen, 5 sharp **ink scratches**. The last 0.42 s the camera stops in the air exactly where the respawn reveal camera starts (behind + above your spawn) and the soul slams past it. **IMPACT (LAND = `WARP_TIME - REVEAL_TIME - 0.17` = 3.78 s):** two anime **IMPACT FRAMES** (0.07 s black with white burst lines from the landing point, 0.07 s white with black lines), a yellow + a white **shockwave ring** on the floor, a **fire pillar**, a white flash ball, **18 dust chunks with ink outlines** that arc out and fall, an **ember burst**, a **scorch mark** that fades, 0.35 s camera shake and a big brush-style **"ドンッ!" (DON!)** that slams in (`SD.IMPACT_TEXT`; set it to `"BOOM!"` if your font shows boxes). **RESPAWN REVEAL (0.12 s later):** MatchFlow sets `Revealing` / `RevealStart` / `RevealBuild` / `RevealFrom` **locally** on every dropping character (this client only - the server never sees them) and the normal **RespawnReveal** plays: souls build the body from the feet up while the camera hovers behind you, then the woosh into your head - it ends ~0.05 s before the phase switches to **Cards**, so the card roll starts the moment you are back in first person. Until their soul lands every dropping body is hidden locally. **Everybody's soul drops** (teammates and enemies at their own spawns, smaller impacts); only yours is followed by the camera. The HUD guis are switched off for the drop and come back at Cards (`hiddenGuis` / `restoreWorld`); GunClient already puts the first-person gun away while the camera is Scriptable. Optional sound slots `SoulIgnite`, `SoulReentry`, `SoulImpact` (silent until added to the SoundBank). Removed: the watercolour map builder, the painting, the white flash, `MAPS.WashTexture` is no longer read (the loading screen still uses `MAPS.Info` images). *(Replaced v5.9 / v5.10:)* **v5.9 WATERCOLOUR FLY-THROUGH (`MatchFlow.runWarp`), replaces the soul-to-the-white-door flight AND the first-person run-in.** Fade to black -> the map's **watercolour painting** (`MatchState.MapImage`, the loading picture) fills the screen -> **your soul lights up** in front of it (bright core, two soft halos, a **smooth tapered ribbon trail** of pooled thin cylinders + a few drifting motes; the old chunky block trail is gone) -> the painting slowly pushes in and dissolves into a **3D watercolour version of the real map**, built live from the loaded `<name>_map` folder (`MatchState.MapFolder`): every visible part is redrawn as a translucent blue-violet wash (floors cream, every third block a deeper wash), the biggest blocks get **ink edges that overshoot their corners** like the painting's construction lines, standing blocks drop **soft wash shadows**, a **pencil grid** lies on the floor, a warm paper vignette frames it; optional uploaded wash texture `MatchConfig.MAPS.WashTexture` -> the soul flies through it on a curve from **high above + behind your side of the map down onto your own spawn marker** (**v5.10: the camera TRAILS the soul - it rides the same path up to ~16 % of the flight behind it, 6 studs back along the path and 3.4 up, looks at the soul and only turns toward the spawn at the very end; FOV 60-68; before, it sat right on the soul and the soul filled the screen**, ~3.6 s) -> a **gradual white flash** (0.8 s) -> you are **standing on your spawn in first person** (the camera is handed over looking the way your spawn faces), the flash clears and the **card roll starts**. No run-in. Limits: `WARP_MAX_PARTS` 380 parts, `WARP_MAX_EDGED` 150 inked blocks, `WARP_MAX_SHADOWS` 70; parts bigger than 600 studs (a baseplate) are drawn but do not stretch the flight path. A map with no painting yet starts straight in the 3D scene. |
| **Cards** (v4.3) | `CARD_REVEAL` 2.8s + `CARD_PICK` 10s (+ `CARD_OUTRO` 1s) | **Power-up card roll** (section 4.2, `CardRoll`). Everybody still frozen on their spawn. Screen dims, CHOOSE YOUR CARD drops in (v5.9: no small explainer line under it any more), then 3 slot-machine reels pop up **one after another, 0.5 s apart, left first**, spin and land; the 10 s pick timer starts when the last one has landed. Click / tap / keys 1-3 to pick, no pick = the middle card. Ends early once everybody has picked. The picked card flies to the HUD. |
| **Countdown** | `COUNTDOWN_TIME` 3s | Big 3 - 2 - 1 on the real map, everybody frozen. Then **GO**. |
| **Playing** | until the wall reaches a finish line | The normal match. Guns are server-blocked in every phase except Playing / Result. **v4.1 respawn reveal (replaces v3.8):** see section 2.3 (death + souls + respawn). Short version: your **camera hovers right behind and above your own body** while 18 blue souls with glowing tails spiral in and build it from the feet up (light pillar, two turning rune rings, sparkles, blue outline that fades as it is finished), then **a fast rapid rush (FOV kick, blur, speed streaks) flies the camera back into your head** and you can move and shoot instantly. Build `REVEAL_BUILD` 1.75 s + dive `REVEAL_DIVE` 0.3 s (`MatchConfig`). |
| **SlowMo** | `SLOWMO_TIME` 2.6s | The moment the wall reaches a finish line everything slows down to `SLOWMO_SCALE` 0.1 (eased in over `SLOWMO_RAMP` 0.5s): walking, sliding, jumping and falling (gravity x scale squared), and shooting (fire rate). The screen desaturates, cinematic bars slide in, short white hit. Implemented by `TimeScale` (shared module) read by `Movement`, `GunClient`, `MatchServer` (gravity) and `MatchFlow` (look). |
| **Result** | `RESULT_TIME` 5s | **v5.10 LINE-UP:** the **winning team** (up to 3, lowest user id in the middle, stepping a little forward) **stands side by side on a dark team-rimmed floor, each holding their own gun (and equipped gun skin) in the `aim` stance** (`Skins.NewFigure` option `stance`), **a gold crown floating, bobbing and turning above every head** (`Crown`, tier by win streak) with the streak as `xN` beside it from 2 up; slow push-in, light columns behind them, "RED TEAM WINS" + VICTORY / DEFEAT (+ `n WINS IN A ROW` for you). The thrones are gone. |
| **Summary** | each player until CONTINUE (client auto-presses 15s after the soul count; server gives up after `SUMMARY_MAX` 60s) | **Souls screen.** Your character stands still on the **left** (v3.7: no hopping, no idle moves); the stuff happens in the **middle**: MATCH BASE, then one row at a time pops in (small text): VICTORY/DEFEAT/DRAW, KILLS, BOXES PLUGGED, BOXES DESTROYED, DAMAGE (each with its count and its multiplier), then TOTAL MULTIPLIER, then the **soul number counts up rapidly from 1** to what you earned, then **CONTINUE** appears next to it and a **15s timer** starts (RETURNING TO LOBBY IN n). **v3.8: an XP bar sits at the very bottom** (LV n, +xp, bar): it fills a bit with every stat row that pops in and tops up at the total; level-ups flash gold. **v3.8: the mouse cursor is visible on the Pregame + Summary screens and clicks no longer fire your gun** (`MatchFlow` sets `GunMenuOpen`, `GunClient` shows the cursor and ignores input). Pressing it (or the timer ending) saves your profile and teleports you to the lobby (`Continue` remote -> `MatchRoster.SendOne`). |

**v5.16 INPUT LOCK + SENSITIVITY:** (1) **Nobody can move, shoot, use a gadget or pose before the match really starts** (owner: "while loading in I click and hear shooting"): `MatchConfig.LOCKED_PHASES` = Waiting / Pregame / Warp / Cards / Countdown. `MatchServer.setPhase` anchors every character in those phases (and `onCharacter` anchors a character that spawns in one); `GunClient` ignores the mouse / R / aiming and fires nothing (no gun sound) while the phase is locked (gadgets were already blocked). (2) **Lower mouse sensitivity** (owner: "same Roblox sensitivity as other gun games, here the guns move way too easily"): `MatchConfig.MOUSE_SENS` = **0.55** multiplies the player's own Roblox sensitivity (`UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity`), set by `GunClient` every frame (hip + aiming the same) and by `MatchFlow` when it hands the camera back. Raise it toward 1 for faster, lower for slower.

**Souls formula** (`MatchConfig.REWARD`, computed on the server in `MatchServer.buildReward`, the client only displays it): `Base 50` x set multiplier (**Win 1.5 / Loss 0.75 / Draw 1.0**) x kills (`1 + 0.10 per kill`) x boxes plugged (`1 + 0.05 each`) x boxes destroyed (`1 + 0.08 each`) x damage (`1 + 0.02 per full 1000 damage` dealt to players). Each multiplier is rounded to 2 decimals before it is multiplied in, so what is shown multiplies to exactly the total. Souls go into the profile (`Souls`), Wins/Losses are counted as before. `PlaceConfig.SOULS_WIN / SOULS_LOSS` are no longer used.
**Stats** are Player attributes, reset every round by `MatchServer`: `StatKills` + `StatDamage` (added by `GunServer`, players only, damage capped at the target's remaining health), `StatPlugged` (whoever's carry/throw plugged the box) and `StatDestroyed` (last shooter when a plugged box is shot to 0; drained boxes give nobody credit).
**Mouse:** the game locks first person, so the interactive screens (Pregame, Summary) contain an invisible `Modal` button, which frees the cursor while they are open.

**Still PROPOSALS (not approved):**
- Wall speed per box lead is now a 3-step table (`WALL_SPEED`, see above) - still needs playtesting.
- ~8 min match + overtime (no timer yet; a round ends only when the wall reaches a finish line).
- The moving wall smashes cover it passes through.
- Name ideas: Breakline, Pushwall, Overload, Shift Line.

### 2.2 Standard modes
- **1v1** is built (section 2.2b, selectable in the lobby). **2v2** rules are still TBD.

### 2.2b 1v1 mode (v4.1)
- **How the mode is picked (v5.6):** by the lobby queue (`Roster.Mode`: `1v1` or `Breakline`), **not** by a child on `MapBounds` any more. The 1v1 maps live in `ServerStorage > 1v1Maps > <name>_map` (see 2.1 Maps). `MatchServer` prints `[MatchServer] mode: ... | map: ...` at start-up; the mode is published as `MatchState.Mode`, the map name as `MatchState.MapName`.
- **Rules:** one player per team (Red vs Blue; extra players wait in the lobby team), a pure gunfight round: **first to 5 kills wins the round** (the finish lines + spawns are still built so your map keeps working) (`MatchConfig.V1.KillsToWin`). Kills are read from the existing `StatKills` (guns, frag, sentry...). `MinPlayers` 1 so you can test alone (set 2 for live). After a death you are out `V1.RespawnTime` 4 s and **spectate your opponent**. Same flow, cutscenes and souls screen as Breakline (the souls breakdown only lists the rows that apply to a 1v1). The top bar shows each side's kills and a bar that leans toward the leader; the timer label says `RED - FIRST TO 5`.
- **Lobby side (v4.2, selectable now):** the GAME MODES tab has a **1V1** entry next to BREAKLINE (see 5.5). The PLAY block follows the selected mode (`PLAY  //  1V1` or `3V3`). `LobbyServer` keeps **one queue per mode** (`MODE_SIZE = { breakline = PlaceConfig.TEAM_SIZE, ["1v1"] = 1 }`), so a 1v1 packs two solo players and a Breakline queue packs 3v3 as before. The queue call sends the mode id (`QueueR:FireServer(queued, modeId)`), the server validates it, and `MatchRoster` carries the mode in the roster so the Breakline place knows which mode was queued. **1v1 is solo only:** a party member who tries to queue it gets a notice to leave the party first. **Both places must be updated together** (the lobby sends the mode, Breakline reads it).
- **Lobby wording for 1v1** (card text in `MODES` in `LobbyClient`): tag `DUEL // FIRST TO 5`; description: one player against one player, win gunfights, use your gadgets, first to 5 kills, sit out a few seconds after a death and watch your opponent; facts: Red vs Blue one player each / First to 5 kills wins the round / Pure gunfights: aim, gadgets, positioning / Solo only: leave your party to queue. **Style rule from the owner:** do not describe 1v1 by what it lacks (no "no boxes / no souls" lines). Breakline and 1v1 are two separate modes; players can infer the differences.

### 2.3 Death, souls and respawn (v4.1, scripts `DeathServer`, `DeathScreen`, `SoulClient`, `RespawnReveal`, `RevealFX`)
- **Roblox auto respawn is off** (`Players.RespawnTime` 600). `DeathServer` owns the timers and calls `LoadCharacter`.
- **Dead for 15 s** (`DEAD_TIME`) and you **spectate a living teammate** (first person through their eyes, click / A / D to switch). **Team wipe:** if nobody on your team is alive, **everybody on the team is reset to 10 s** (`WIPE_TIME`, e.g. you had 7 s left and spectated the last mate, he dies: everyone is now at 10) and you **free roam** (WASD fly, SPACE / C up / down, SHIFT fast) - walls stop you (sphere cast that slides along surfaces) and the camera is clamped inside `MapBounds` (the invisible boundary walls cannot be ray-cast, so MapBounds is used). Spectating your last teammate and he dies = straight into free roam.
- **Death bar (bottom of the screen):** one solid team-coloured (darkened) bar, 78 px tall: **big countdown on the left**, **name of who you spectate (or FREE ROAM) in the middle**, and the **very bottom edge is a line that shrinks** to match the time left. While a teammate revives you the middle says `BEING REVIVED BY <name> 54%`.
- **Souls:** when you die with a teammate alive a **soul drops on your body**: **v4.2: a tiny, ghostly, translucent soul** (small ghost body with two eyes and a fading wispy tail, team-coloured glow; the rings, orbiting sparks and light beam were removed) hovering low over the body (hover height 1.5, was 2.5) with a **name tag** (`NAME` / `HOLD F REVIVE   E CARRY`). Teammates: **hold E (0.3 s) = pick it up** and carry it like a box (**left click = throw, Q = drop**, guns away); **v4.2: the carried soul is small and sits by your shoulder** (`SoulClient`, `DeathServer`), or **hold F for 8 s = revive** (`REZ_TIME`). Your own soul and enemies cannot use it; it disappears the moment you respawn (timer or revive). Souls are not created on a team wipe, and all souls vanish when the round ends.
- **Revive animation:** the reviver **kneels and is rooted** (arms pressed down) next to the soul; **the revived player's body is built out of souls right there, slowly over the 8 s** (their skin, the same soul-build effect as a respawn) with a progress bar + percent on the soul's tag. **Cancel (let go / walk away / die) and the half-built body fades away**. When it completes you appear **at the soul** with only the last ~14% of the reveal left (`REZ_FINISH`), then dive into your body.
- Gadgets / guns are blocked for the carrier and the reviver. Attributes: Player `IsDead`, `DeathEnds`, `DeathTotal`, `DeathMode` (`spectate` / `roam`), `CarryingSoul`; Character `Reviving`; soul part `OwnerId`, `OwnerName`, `TeamName`, `State`, `RezBy`, `RezStart`, `RezTime`, `Ground`, `Carrier`.

### 2.4 AI bots in the queue (v5.7: `BotServer`, `BotAPI`, lobby `LobbyServer`, `MatchRoster`, `MatchConfig.BOTS`, `PlaceConfig.BOT_WAIT`)
- **When:** the lobby matchmaker first tries a normal full match (as before). If that does not work and the **oldest waiting entry of a mode has been in the queue `PlaceConfig.BOT_WAIT` (60 s)**, nobody else is queueing with them: `LobbyServer.matchMode(items, mode, true)` packs everyone who is waiting into Red / Blue as usual and **the empty slots become bots** (3v3: whatever is missing up to 3 per team; 1v1: one bot). It reserves a Breakline server and saves the roster as `{ Red, Blue, Mode, Bots = { Red = n, Blue = n } }`. `BOT_WAIT = 0` turns bots off. Only real players are teleported, `MatchRoster.Total` counts real players only, `MatchRoster.Bots` carries the counts.
- **Studio test:** set `MatchConfig.BOTS.StudioRed / StudioBlue` (bots per team) and press Play; live servers read the roster.
- **What a bot is:** a normal R15 character (default skin, a world gun on the chest, random gun from `BOTS.Weapons`), a Humanoid with `BOTS.Health` 250, Character attributes `IsBot = true` and `OwnerTeam = "Red" / "Blue"` (`GunServer` already refuses friendly fire for any model with `OwnerTeam`, players shoot bots like sentries). Spawned at the Cards / Countdown phase on the team's finish line (`BotAPI.SpawnCF`), frozen until Playing, removed when the round is decided (SlowMo and later). A dead bot respawns after `BOTS.RespawnTime` 14 s (1v1: the round reset, `MatchState.Round`, brings everybody back).
- **Brain (`BotServer`, one 0.1 s think per bot):** looks for the nearest enemy (player or enemy bot) it can SEE (ray, `BOTS.Sight` 150); strafes + keeps distance 16 - 55 studs, turns toward the target every frame, waits `ReactTime` 0.45 s, then fires in bursts (`BurstTime` / `PauseTime`) with hitscan, `Spread` 3.2 deg aim error, `FireRateMult` 0.7, `DamageMult` 0.55, the gun's own falloff + head multiplier, its own reload. Damage goes through `Vitals.Damage` (a respawn ForceField protects), players get the usual `Damaged` remote. **v5.8 spawn shield fix:** the bot's ForceField used to be added only after `Skins.Dress` had finished (up to ~1.5 s) and then lasted 1.6 s + the whole freeze, so after a 1v1 round reset a bot stayed unkillable for ~3 s after the player could already shoot, and `Vitals.Damage` still reported the full hit (the owner saw ~800 damage go in without a kill). Now the shield end is fixed the moment the respawn starts: a 1v1 reset shield ends together with the player's reveal (`REVEAL_TIME`, before the bot's freeze ends), a normal respawn has `BOTS.SpawnShield` 1 s. `Vitals.Damage` returns what really came off (0 while a ForceField protects) and `GunServer` sends no damage number / stats for a hit that dealt 0. With nobody in sight it walks (PathfindingService) to the last place it saw somebody, hunts the nearest enemy (1v1) or roams random spots on either half. A stuck bot jumps and re-plans.
- **Breakline boxes:** `BuilderPerTeam` 1 bot per team fetches the nearest loose box (`BotAPI.GrabBox`), carries it to its own side's nearest empty hole and `MatchServer` plugs it exactly like a player's carried box (`botCarry` table + a loop next to the player carry loop). Bots do not shoot enemy boxes or throw boxes.
- **Credit:** `GunServer` gives kill + damage stats for shooting a bot (`IsBot` attribute); bot kills are counted per team in `MatchState.BotKills_<team>` (`BotAPI.AddBotKill`) and added into `teamKills`, so the 1v1 kill race works. Kill banner shows `BOT <team> <n>`.
- **Known limits (untested in Studio):** bots have no arm IK or aim pitch (the gun stays at the chest); no revive logic (a player whose whole human team is down is a team wipe, bots are not counted); bots ignore gadgets and shields; PathfindingService needs a walkable map.

## 3. Loadout

### 3.1 Active loadout (chosen before the match)
- **1 gun**
- **3 gadgets**
- **1 special movement ability**

- **v5.14 pre-game menu fix (`MatchFlow`):** during the last 5 seconds of the 20 s loadout screen the big loadout picker used to close on every countdown slam (3, 2, 1); it now stays open and only closes when the clock hits 0.

### 3.2 Reserve (v5.7: REAL - press J while dead)
- The loadout has **3 reserve slots** (R1 - R3). A reserve slot holds one extra gun **or** gadget (picked in the lobby loadout or the pre-game menu).
- **While you are dead in a Playing round, press J** (keyboard; closes with J / ESC again) and the **RESERVE screen** opens over the spectate view (`DeathScreen`, `LoadoutPicker.Dock`): your MOVE, PRIMARY, 3 GADGET and 3 RESERVE tiles. Only the **PRIMARY and GADGET tiles are clickable**. Click the gun or a gadget slot and the **small horizontal scroll** opens above it (`LoadoutPicker.Strip`) listing **only what is in your reserve** for that slot (guns for the gun slot, gadgets for a gadget slot; a gadget you already carry in another slot is not offered).
- Picking one is a real **swap**: the reserve item is equipped and the item it replaces goes into **that same reserve slot** (e.g. a flashbang in gadget slot 2, a sentry in R1: pick the sentry for slot 2 and the flashbang lands in R1). The client fires `MatchRemotes.ReserveSwap(kind, idx, id)`; `MatchServer` validates it (phase Playing, `IsDead`, item really in your reserve, 0.15 s rate limit, no duplicate gadget), rewrites `LoadoutJson` (and the `Weapon` / `Sight` attributes for a gun) and everything else follows from those attributes (GunServer, GadgetServer resets charges on the new loadout, MatchHUD repaints). **The swap is match-only: your saved lobby loadout is not changed.** The dock repaints as soon as the server answers.
- The screen **closes by itself** the moment you come back to life (revive, personal respawn or team respawn: `IsDead` goes false) or the phase leaves Playing. Movement ability is still not swappable.
- The death bar shows `J  RESERVE` in its hint line. While the screen is open the mouse is free, the spectate camera keeps following but does not turn, free roam keys are ignored.
- Sound slots: `ReserveOpen`, `ReserveClose`, `ReserveSwap` (SoundBank, Body).
- **v5.11 no duplicates:** the Breakline pre-game picker wrote the slot straight away, so all three reserve slots could hold the same item (the lobby already swapped). Now picking an item that sits in another slot of the same row **swaps the two slots** (same rule as the lobby, `MatchFlow.applyItem`), and `LoadoutRules.Clean` (both games) clears any duplicate gadget or duplicate reserve item on the server (the first one stays).

- **v5.16 GADGET OR RESERVE, NEVER BOTH (owner):** a gadget that sits in one of the 3 gadget slots can no longer also sit in a reserve slot. Picking an item for one row takes it out of the other row (lobby `LobbyClient.setSlot`, Breakline pre-game `MatchFlow.applyItem`), and `LoadoutRules.Clean` (both games) clears a reserve slot that holds a gadget that is already in a gadget slot (the gadget slot wins).

- **v5.18 GUN: PRIMARY OR RESERVE, NEVER BOTH (owner: "I should not be able to put a gun in my reserve and primary at the same time"):** picking a gun as the primary takes it out of the reserve, and the reserve picker no longer offers the gun you carry as your primary (lobby `LobbyClient.setSlot` + picker, Breakline pre-game `MatchFlow.applyItem` + picker, the training zone's CHANGE LOADOUT). `LoadoutRules.Clean` (both games) clears a reserve slot that holds the primary gun (the primary wins). A reserve SWAP while dead (J) still works: the old primary goes into the reserve slot the new gun came from.

### 3.3 Special movement abilities (v3.8: REAL, one per loadout, MOVE slot)
Everyone keeps the base kit (sprint, crouch, slide, slide-jump, mantle). The MOVE slot holds ONE of these (script: `AbilityClient`, a small icon + cooldown / bar HUD sits bottom middle; all numbers in its `CFG`):
- **Double Jump** (icon: two chevrons): press SPACE again in the air for a second jump (6.8 studs). **Cooldown 3.5s.**
- **Mega Jump** (icon: big arrow over a coil): HOLD SPACE on the ground, a **charge bar** shows bottom middle (1.3s to full), release = launch 16 to 54 studs depending on the charge. A quick tap is a normal jump. You walk at half speed while charging. **Cooldown 6s.**
- **Wall Run** (icon: wall + speed lines; **v4.0 rebuilt, no SPACE needed**): **sprint into a wall** (head-on, speed >= 18) and you run **UP** it (27 studs/s, the push fades out over ~1.15s, then you **vault over the top** if there is a ledge, otherwise you kick off). **Jump / sprint-jump next to a wall** (or hit it at an angle in the air) and you run **ALONG** it (32 studs/s, any direction, camera-relative). Walls only, never ceilings; low ledges (< 5.5 studs) are not walls (jump / mantle those). A **stamina bar** drains while running (3.4s from full, up-runs cost 1.4x) and refills on the ground; run dry and you drop and must regain 25% before the next run. SPACE = wall jump (flies off the wall in the direction you look). Letting go of the move keys drops you after 0.3s. You cannot re-attach to the wall you just left for 0.9s. **You can shoot while wall-running.** No cooldown. **Look (v4.0):** body stays upright and leans (15 deg along, 12 deg up), the horizon rolls 6 deg, dust kicks up off the wall, ring on the wall at the start, and **everybody sees a real run animation** (`AbilityPose`: stride legs, the arm next to the wall reaches out and touches it, the other arm pumps; up-run = fast knee-high stepping).
- **v4.1 wall spin fix:** while on a wall (run or climb) the character used to start spinning / wobbling in 360s. Cause: the body turn target was blended by lerping the *look vector* (two opposite vectors lerp through zero, so the direction flipped) into a springy `AlignOrientation`. Now the target is built once, the body **slerps** toward it (`CFrame:Lerp` on rotations), the constraint is **rigid** and any leftover angular velocity is zeroed every frame (`alignTo` in `AbilityClient`). Same smoothing on the stand-up after leaving a wall.
- **v5.11 WALL RUN = HOLD SPACE (owner: "in a tight space it just always activates"):** the wall run no longer latches on by itself. It only starts while **SPACE is held** (gamepad A held, or the touch jump button held) and a wall is in reach (same reach / speed rules as before). **Keep holding SPACE to keep running; let go = wall jump** (in the look direction), or just drop off if you grabbed the wall less than 0.15 s ago. HUD hint: `HOLD SPACE BY A WALL` / `RELEASE SPACE: WALL JUMP`. (`spaceHeld()` in `AbilityClient`, both games.)
- **Wall Climb** (icon: wall + ceiling corner + arrow; **v4.0: no SPACE needed**): **walk into a wall** (hold the move key at it for 0.14s) and you grab on and climb **for as long as you want**; SPACE also grabs walls / overhangs / the ceiling above you within 3 studs. WASD moves you along it (camera-relative, over edges, onto ceilings). **Climbing over the top edge = automatic vault** onto the floor up there. SPACE = jump off, C / Ctrl = let go. **Letting go starts a 3.5s cooldown. You CANNOT shoot while climbing** (`GunClient` hides the gun, `GunServer` refuses shots while `AbilityMode == "climb"`). **Look (v4.0):** you climb **upright** (only on overhangs / ceilings the body turns over), hand-over-hand arm animation, legs pushing, dust when moving; the gun hangs at the hip (`ThirdPersonClient` turns the IK hands off while climbing). **v5.9 ceiling pose:** while climbing, the body always **faces the surface** and the head points the way you climb along it; on an overhang / ceiling that heading (`ceilHead` in `AbilityClient`) is your direction along the ceiling, so the body pitches back over the inside corner and lies under the ceiling looking up at it (before, it stood upside down on the ceiling and looked like it hung from its head). Crawling backwards keeps the heading (feet first, no spin); sideways turns you smoothly.
- **v5.7 WALL RUN (rebuilt feel, `AbilityClient`):** you no longer have to be pressed against the wall or jump. **Run past a wall within 4.8 studs (`WR_SIDE_REACH`) at sprint speed, on the ground or in the air, and you latch on at once and run ALONG it** (the pull toward the wall is stronger, up to 14 studs/s, so you glide in). **Run at a wall (it must be within 3.6 studs ahead, `WR_REACH`) at a steep angle (steeper than `WR_ALONG_MAX` 0.62, 1 = head-on) and you run UP it**; a shallower angle runs along it. Both until the stamina bar is empty (3.4 s). **Direction control while running along:** the run stays horizontal and the move keys are read against the wall (`sideDir`): W with the camera pointing along the wall = that way, S = back, A / D follow the camera's right; if the camera points straight into the wall you keep your way, so looking at the wall can no longer turn the run into a climb. Look straight up (camera pitch above ~55 deg) and hold W to turn a side run into an up-run. While running UP the controls are camera-relative on the wall as before (A / D slide you sideways, which turns it into a side run). **The weird camera angle** came from the root hugging the wall (1.45 studs) with a 15 deg lean and 6 deg roll: now `HOLD_SIDE` 2.0, lean 8 / 8 deg, camera roll 3 deg. Walls must still be 5.5 studs tall (`WR_WALL_H`), 0.9 s lock on the wall you just left.
- **v5.8 WALL RUN UP + DIAGONALS (`AbilityClient`, both games):** (1) **running UP no longer fades out:** the old up-run push faded to 0 over `WR_UP_TIME` 1.15 s and then kicked you off, so you got ~2 s up the wall, slowed right down and fell. Now an up-run holds **`WR_UP_SPEED` 25 studs/s until the stamina bar is empty** (straight up costs `WR_UP_COST` 1.25x, so ~2.7 s / ~65 studs from full), then you drop; running off the top still vaults you over. (2) **One direction rule for every wall run (`wallDir`):** the horizontal part is the way along the wall (camera + A / D, as `sideDir` did), the vertical part comes from the **camera pitch while you hold W**: level = along, looking up past `WR_CLIMB_DEAD` 10 deg starts angling the run up, `WR_CLIMB_FULL` 62 deg = straight up (about 36 deg pitch = a 45 deg diagonal), looking down past `WR_DOWN_DEAD` 28 deg angles it down (max ~40 deg). So running up AND across is as easy as running along: angle the camera that way. Head-on up-runs stay straight up while the camera faces the wall. Speed blends from `WR_SPEED` 32 (along) to 25 (straight up); stamina cost blends the same way. `WR_UP_TIME` is gone, `sideDir` is kept but unused.
- **v5.7 WALL CLIMB wall <-> ceiling:** `climbHit` used to keep the wall under your hands as your surface for as long as it existed, so a ceiling in front of you was only noticed after the wall was gone (never). Now the **surface AHEAD of you in the direction you climb is checked first** (`WC_CORNER_REACH` 4.2 studs, it must face you and differ from the current surface), so climbing up a wall into a ceiling just carries on across the ceiling, and from the ceiling onto the next wall the same way. Outside corners (over an edge) are unchanged. Sound slots: `ClimbGrab`, `ClimbRelease`, `WallExhausted`, `DoubleJump`, `MegaCharge`, `MegaLaunch`.
- **v5.13 WALL CLIMB STAYS ON ITS STOMACH (`AbilityClient`, both games; owner: "switching from one wall to another at a different angle, or from a wall to a ceiling, I am no longer crawling directly on it, I hang off it sideways"):** (1) **Inside corners are blended:** the surface ahead (ceiling over the wall, the next wall at an angle, `WC_CORNER_REACH` 4.2) no longer takes over the moment it is found.
