It is tracked (`cornerN`), and as you get closer your body normal turns from the old surface (`bellyN`) to the new one in step with the distance (`WC_CORNER_BLEND` 2.6 studs), the path curves round the corner (the climb direction is taken on the blended surface, the hold pull is shared between both surfaces), and the new surface becomes yours when you are flat on it. Before, you turned to face the ceiling while still on the wall (4 studs away) and then flew across. (2) **No more flip:** the head direction (`headDir`) is carried from surface to surface (laid flat on each new one) and only ever turns AROUND the stomach: upright on a wall (`WC_TURN_WALL` 3.2 rad/s), the way you crawl on a ceiling (`WC_TURN_CEIL` 5 rad/s, backwards keeps it). Before, it was world-up on walls and the heading on ceilings, so ceiling -> wall flipped the body 180 degrees mid-corner through a sideways pose. Coming down from a ceiling you now arrive head first and roll upright on the wall. (3) **Outside edges:** going over the side edge of a wall (or a ceiling edge) carries you round onto the side face (`wrap`, up to 0.45 s) instead of letting go. (4) **Closer:** the body centre holds `WC_HOLD` 1.3 studs from the surface (was `HOLD_FACE` 1.75, still used by the up-run), so the stomach is on the surface. The body turn follows faster while climbing (slerp `dt * 16`, was 9). `ceilHead` / `climbHit` are gone. Checked with a NumPy stand-in of the corner maths: wall -> ceiling and ceiling -> wall arrive flat at 1.3 studs, normal + head turn together, no flip.
- **v5.13 ICONS (`UIKit.AbilityIcon`, both games):** every movement icon keeps its line drawing (speed lines, chevrons, wall / ceiling, arrows; now one shade darker) and gets a **solid figure doing the move**: round head, thick torso, pill-shaped two-segment arms + legs (the far limbs a little darker). DASH = a runner in a deep forward lunge behind the speed lines; DOUBLE JUMP = a tucked jumper over the two air chevrons; MEGA JUMP = a figure shooting up off the coil (one arm up) with the arrow beside it; WALL RUN = a runner leaning out from the wall with both feet on it, streaks behind; WALL CLIMB = a climber flat on the wall reaching for the ceiling, arrow going wall -> ceiling. Same call (`AbilityIcon(parent, id, size, color)`), so the lobby slots, pre-game dock, shop and HUD all get them.
- The old placeholders (Phase Shift, Glider, Wall Peek) are gone from `LobbyCatalog`. Old saves with one of them just show an empty MOVE slot.
- `AbilityClient` sets the character attributes `AbilityMode` (`wallrun` / `climb`) + `WallNormal`; `Movement` stands down while it is set; `AbilityServer` (v2) validates the mode against the saved loadout and relays both. `AbilityPose` (new LocalScript, StarterPlayerScripts) animates every character from those attributes. All three scripts are the same in both games, so the abilities also work in the lobby. Tuning: `CFG` at the top of `AbilityClient` (WR_*, WC_*), poses: `AbilityPose`. Optional sounds: SoundBank slots `WallStart`, `WallJump`, `Vault` (silent until added).

### 3.4 Gadgets (v4.0: REAL, Breakline matches only; v4.1: equip first)
**v4.9 throwing overhaul (owner: "it just gets lobbed from your player in a crappy not strong way"):** the old throw was ~70 studs/s against full Roblox gravity (196), which only carried ~13 studs. Now (`GadgetConfig.Throw`, server `GadgetServer.launch`, preview line `GadgetClient.simulate`, all the same maths):
- **Speed** frag 118 / smoke 110 / flash 114 studs/s, **upward angle** `Up` 0.16 -> 0.1 (flatter, harder).
- **Light gravity in flight:** a `VectorForce` on the thrown item cancels part of gravity so it flies with **`Gravity` = 78**; at its **first bounce** the force switches off and normal gravity takes over (it does not float around on the ground). Bounces keep `Elasticity` 0.3.
- **Run speed carries over** 60 % (`Inherit`, was 35 %): sprint-throws go much further.
- It leaves your **throwing hand** (`Side` 0.45 studs right of your head, `Start` 1.6 in front), not your eyes.
- **Release timing:** the click is sent at once; the server sets `GadgetThrowAt` (everybody's arm starts the swing) and launches the item **`Windup` 0.12 s later**, the moment the arm whips forward.
- **Right click (when you are not holding a throw) = underhand toss:** 42 % speed (`UnderMult`), +0.3 upward (`UnderUp`), its own arm swing. Right click while holding still cancels.
- **First person (`GadgetClient`):** new resting hold (grenade low in the right hand, a little inward), holding the click cocks the arm back beside your head, and the throw is keyframed in 3 beats: **cock** (0 - 0.12 s) -> **whip** forward and across (the item disappears from the hand at 0.12 s) -> **follow-through** down and out of view (to 0.46 s), then the next grenade rises (normal equip raise). Underhand has its own 3 beats (low back -> forward/up -> follow).
- **Third person (`MoveAnim`, both games):** grenade-ready hold (grenade at chest height, elbow tucked, left hand beside it on the pin; turrets / projectors / mines carried with both hands at the waist) and a real **3-beat throw** for everybody: arm cocked behind the head with the torso turned away and the left arm pointing at the target -> arm snaps over the top while the torso turns through -> finishes low across the body, eases back over 0.3 s. Underhand: arm low behind the hip, swings forward and up. The item in other players' hands is smaller (0.55) and sits in the palm.
**v5.18 (owner Nov 9):**
- **FRAG COOKING** (`GadgetConfig.FRAG.cook`, `GadgetServer`, `GadgetClient`): **pressing** the click pulls the pin (new remote `GadgetRemotes.Cook`) and the fuse starts right then (`fuse` 2.3 -> **3.0**, counted from the press). Hold = the throw line as before + a **FUSE 2.4** readout and bar under the crosshair (red under 1 s); **release = the same throw as before**, the frag flies with whatever fuse is left (at least `minFuse` 0.15). Hold it for the whole 3 s and it **goes off in your hand** (normal blast, you take `selfMult` 50 %). The pin is out, so **right click while cooking = an underhand toss** (it can no longer be cancelled); **putting it away (1 / Q / another gadget) drops it at your feet**. A press while the grenade is still coming up pulls the pin the moment it can be thrown. A right-click toss without holding first is an uncooked frag (full 3 s). The preview line ends where the fuse runs out. Smoke / flashbang are unchanged. Optional sound slot `GadCook` (silent until added).
- **PLACE ANYWHERE, AS FAR AS YOU CAN SEE IT** (`GadgetModels.Place`, `GadgetServer`): sentry, bubble shield and prox mine go on **floors, walls, ceilings and ramps** (`ground = false` for all; the 4-corner flat-surface + clearance checks stay). The place range is **`GM.REACH` = 60 studs for all three** (**v5.19: 20**), and the preview ray is exactly that long, so wherever the blue copy shows you can place it (before: the copy showed further out than the 13 - 15 stud place range). Server: the **sentry** stands on / hangs from / sticks out of the surface (base turned onto the normal; floor + ceiling sentries face the way you looked and sweep round the normal, wall sentries look straight out and sweep left / right; the head turns with the surface as its up), the **shield** dome grows out of the surface along its normal (projector + ring turned onto it), and the **mine** trigger is measured in the mine's own frame (8.5 studs across the surface, -2 .. +6 studs out from it), so wall / ceiling mines go off too.
- **Gadget arm** (`GadgetClient`, `GadgetModels.BuildHand`): the hand is **one block** (`GM.HAND_SIZE`) in your costume's glove colour, the forearm is a **square block** (was a round cylinder that looked like an oval) in your costume's sleeve colour, with a cuff, and it **starts at the back of the hand** (the hand used to sit on the side of the arm).

**v5.19 (owner Nov 10):**
- **PLACING WORKS AGAIN (`GadgetClient`, both games):** the sentry, bubble shield and prox mine could not be placed at all: the v5.18 frag-cook change put `doPlace()` INSIDE the throw branch of the click handler (`onPrimaryDown`), so a click with a placeable did nothing. The click places again (the server side was fine).
- **REACH 20 STUDS (`GadgetModels`, both games):** `GM.REACH` **60 -> 20** for all three placeables - the preview ray is exactly that long, so the blue / red copy follows your view up to 20 studs (then hangs red at the end of it) and that is also the farthest you can place it (the server allows the usual small tolerance).

**v4.2 size fix:** held gadgets were far too big in first person. Throwables are now scaled to **0.38** and placeables to **0.24** (`VM_SCALE_THROW`, `VM_SCALE_PLACE` in `GadgetClient`, about the size of the box you carry), held farther from the camera, with a smaller glove and sleeve.
**v4.1 controls:** the key (**2 / 3 / 4**, gamepad DPad left / up / right, mobile buttons) now **EQUIPS** the gadget: your gun is swapped for it (gun hidden, the item is in your right hand, first person + third person for everyone). Press the same key again, **1**, or **Q** to put it away and get the gun back (with the normal equip animation). Equipped item is outlined blue in the HUD.
- **Throwables (Frag / Smoke / Flashbang):** **left click = throw**. **Hold the click** = a see-through white line shows the whole arc (including a bounce) and a ring where it lands; release = throw; **right click** while holding cancels. The line uses the same speed / upward angle / gravity as the server (`GadgetConfig.Throw.Up`), so it matches.
- **Placeables (Sentry / Bubble Shield / Prox. Mine):** a **blue see-through copy** (with a blue outline) follows where you look - **move your view to drag it around** (it glides, faces your direction; the shield also shows its dome, the mine and sentry show their trigger / sight range as a faint disc). It turns **red when it cannot go there**: out of reach (sentry / shield 15 studs, mine 13), too steep (sentry / shield need floor), part of the base would hang over an edge, or something is in the way (clearance box). **Left click = place it exactly there.** The server repeats every check (same `GadgetModels.Check`) and rebuilds the surface itself.
- **v4.8 remodel (`GadgetModels` v2):** every gadget is rebuilt with real detail and is one connected piece (checked offline). **Frag** = M67 style: seamed olive body, yellow stencil marks, fuze with band + cap, a curved safety lever that hugs the body, safety pin with a pull ring. **Smoke** = M18 style canister: colour band, hazard stripe ring, label, vented top shoulder + bottom plate, seam rings, lever + pin ring. **Flashbang** = M84 style: aluminium body with two rings of vent slots, black end caps, yellow band. **Sentry** (about 107 parts): ground plate with bolts, column with corner plates + hydraulic rods + cable, tripod with knees / leg covers / foot pads, slew ring with teeth, armoured head (glacis, cheek plates, hatch, louvres), receiver + vented shroud + cooled barrel + slotted muzzle, sensor eye in a housing, ammo box with lid / handle / latch, exhaust slits, camera dome, antenna. **Shield projector**: bolted puck, vents, three pylons with emitter heads + status lights, glowing core inside a ring cage, finned cap. **Mine**: ribbed body, hazard-stripe ribs, bolts, label, three sensor stalks. Names other scripts rely on did not change (sentry `BaseDisc` / `Eye` / groups `base` + `head` / `HeadPivot` / `Muzzle`, mine `Body` / `Eye`). The team colour only shows on small status lights, rings and the sentry / shield trim.
- **Gloved hand for gadgets (v4.8):** the first-person hand is a plain block hand (v5.6: palm block, one block that curls up the front of the item, wrist block - no finger lines, no thumb; `GadgetModels.BuildHand`) with a block sleeve. **Sentry performance:** its head has about 75 parts, so only the head body is moved each frame and every other head part is welded to it (`GadgetServer`).
- **(v4.1 text:) Models are nicer + shared** (`GadgetModels`): frag (olive body with bands, lever + gold pin), smoke canister (grey with a glowing band + nozzle), flashbang (yellow band, vents), sentry (tripod legs, shroud, sensors, ammo box, antenna), shield projector (puck, pylons, glowing core), mine (rimmed puck, ring, eye, sensor prongs). **Lobby + pre-game pictures** now show the real model (`GadgetModels.Picture`, a ViewportFrame like the gun pictures) instead of the 2-letter tile; the in-match HUD slots use it too.
- Old behaviour kept: Pick up to 3 in the lobby loadout (GADGETS tiles); only while the round is **Playing**.
- (v4.0 text:) Pick up to 3 in the lobby loadout (GADGETS tiles) and throw them with **2 / 3 / 4** (gamepad DPad left / up / right, mobile: 3 buttons). Only while the round is **Playing**; not while carrying a box, wall-climbing or being revealed. Charges refill on every respawn; one used charge comes back after its recharge time. No friendly fire (your own frag / mine hits you at half / 40%). All numbers: `GadgetConfig` (ReplicatedStorage). Server rules: `GadgetServer`; keys + visuals: `GadgetClient`; HUD slots (icon, charges, cooldown, recharge number): `MatchHUD`.
| Gadget | Charges / recharge | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| **Frag Grenade** | 2 / 24s | Bouncing grenade, **v5.18: cookable, 3.0s fuse from the press** (was 2.3s from the throw), 17 stud blast, up to 120 dmg (falls off with distance, needs line of sight), 260 vs plugged enemy boxes, 220 vs shields. |
| **Smoke** | 2 / 30s | Pops after 1s, a thick cloud (15 studs) for 13s. Blocks the sentry's sight. |
| **Flashbang** | 2 / 22s | 1.25s fuse. Anyone who can SEE it (line of sight, 75 studs) is blinded: white-out up to 3.8s, stronger the more you look at it and the closer you are, then colour drain + blur fade. |
| **Sentry** | 1 / 80s | Throw it, it lands on flat ground and arms in 0.9s. Turret that tracks and shoots **enemies** in line of sight (62 studs, 540 rpm, 7 dmg), 170 HP (shootable), lasts 36s. One per player. Gives the kill / damage credit to its owner. |
| **Bubble Shield** | 1 / 50s | Throw it on the ground, it **expands into a dome** (9.5 stud radius, 750 HP, 16s). Enemy bullets and explosions hit the dome; bullets fired from INSIDE leave freely (so it is a real bubble to shoot out of). It cracks lighter as it takes damage and shatters when broken. |
| **Prox. Mine** | 2 / 34s | Sticks to any surface where it lands, arms in 1.3s (red blinking eye). An enemy within 8.5 studs makes it blink fast, hop up and blow after 0.35s (13 stud blast, 100 dmg, 200 vs boxes). Max 3 per player, 75s life. Other explosions can set it off. **v5.11 FIX:** it never went off - the height test was `d.Y - m.pos.Y` with `d` already relative to the mine, so on any map not sitting at Y = 0 nobody was ever close enough. Now: flat distance <= 8.5 and -2 .. +6 studs over the mine, against enemy players **and enemy bots**. Explosions (frag + mine) now also hurt enemy bots. Your own mine still ignores you and your team. |
Gadgets are NOT usable in the lobby. Optional sounds (silent until added to SoundBank): `Explosion`, `FlashBang`, `ShieldBreak`.

## 4. Power-Up Cards
*(v4.3: BUILT, not playtested. The 32 cards are PLACEHOLDERS: names, art and text are made up. **v5.14: 8 of them WORK** (4.4): Ember Heart, Thick Skin, Deep Pockets, Hot Barrel, Grave Candle, Scout's Eye, Hollow Point, Glass Cannon. The other 24 still do nothing. Cards are now sold in the shop (5.9).)*

### 4.1 Card collection + deck (lobby: CARD INDEX)
- **Collection** = every card you own and how many copies. **v5.14 STARTER COLLECTION (owner, Nov 7): every player, new AND existing, owns 4 copies each of EMBER HEART, THICK SKIN, DEEP POCKETS (common), HOT BARREL, GRAVE CANDLE, SCOUT'S EYE (uncommon), HOLLOW POINT (rare) and GLASS CANNON (epic)** = 32 copies = exactly one full deck (`Cards.STARTER`). The free "everyone owns every placeholder" collection (85 copies) is gone. The profile key `Cards` now holds only the **extra** copies won from packs / bought in the daily shop (`{ [cardId] = n }`, written by `CardShopServer`); `Cards.Collection(saved)` = starter + extra. The `CardsJson` attribute is the FULL collection: clients read it with `Cards.Owned` (v5.14 fix: `CardIndex` used to run `Collection` on it again, adding the starter copies twice). **Existing players:** a saved deck with cards they no longer own fails `Cards.Validate`, so the lobby and the match fall back to `Cards.DefaultDeck` = the 8 starter cards x 4 (nothing to do by hand). The owner listed Scout's Eye as a rare; it is **Uncommon** in `Cards.List` and was left there (moving it = a card rebake; say so if it should move).
- **Deck** ("card pool") = the cards you take into a match (profile key `Deck`, a list of 32 card ids).
  - Exactly **32 cards - no more, no less** (owner changed this from 36 on Nov 3). It only saves when it is exactly 32 (`DECK_SIZE` in `Cards`).
  - **Max 4 copies of the same card** (`MAX_COPIES`), and never more copies than you own.
  - The server re-checks every save (`Cards.Validate` in `LobbyServer`, remote `SetDeck`), so the saved deck is always playable. An empty / broken deck falls back to `Cards.DefaultDeck` (one of every card).
- **CARD INDEX page** (`CardIndex` LocalScript, lobby). Opened from two **CARD INDEX** buttons: on the home HUD right above the PLAY cluster, and in the PLAY screen top bar left of the souls (both show `DECK n / 32`). Full-screen, same CLEAN look as the lobby (near-black, hairlines, Breakline red, Oswald / Gotham / Roboto Mono, starfield). ESC / BACK closes it.
  - **Top bar:** logo / CARD INDEX, a round **(?) DECK RULES** button (v5.12: just a ring, no grey fill, bigger centred ?) (popup with the rules below + the 7 rarity swatches), ESC BACK.
  - **Left - COLLECTION:** rarity filter chips (ALL + 8 rarities), SORT // RARITY or NAME, a scrolling grid of cards (200 px wide). Under each card: [-] button, 4 pips (filled red = in your deck, outlined = owned, hidden = copies you do not own), [+] button, and `IN DECK n / max // OWNED n`. A red `xN` badge sits on cards that are in your deck. **Click a card = inspect** (big card, rarity, description, ADD TO DECK / TAKE ONE OUT, < > or arrow keys to browse). **(v5.17: right click no longer does anything.)**
  - **Right - DECK:** (v5.14: the **DECK** title above the counter is **46 px**, was 24) big `n / 32` counter (red until it is exactly 32), progress bar, status chip (`ADD 3 MORE CARDS` / `SAVING...` / `SAVED // READY`), a **rarity mix** bar with counts, and one row per card in the deck (rarity strip, name, rarity, `xN`, [-]). Buttons: **AUTO FILL** (rarest cards first, one copy per pass), **UNDO CHANGES** (back to the saved deck), **CLEAR**.
  - **v5.17 polish (owner Nov 8, `CardIndex` v1.7):** inspect view - the "PLACEHOLDER CARD // THE ABILITY DOES NOTHING YET" line is gone, the rarity above the name is a big **40 px** rarity word (no more "CARD 07 OF 33"), the stats line reads `IN DECK:  2 / 4        |        OWNED:  4`, the "RIGHT CLICK A CARD..." line is gone and **right click no longer takes a card out** (collection cards and deck minis; the [-] buttons and TAKE ONE OUT still do), BROWSE is a bright 24 px label. Deck panel - RARITY MIX is 18 px bright, the small `/ 32` sits right next to the big number (it follows the number's real width), the bottom hint is gone and **`-  CLICK A CARD TO INSPECT`** sits next to the COLLECTION title.
  - **Saving is automatic** the moment the deck is valid. Leaving with an unfinished deck warns once ("press BACK again to discard"), then drops the changes; the last saved deck stays.
  - The card art is built a couple of cards per frame when the page first opens (no hitch), then kept.

### 4.2 Match-start roll (Breakline: phase `Cards`)
- Right **after the run-in cutscene and before the 3-2-1**, with everybody still frozen: `MatchServer` draws **3 cards from that player's 32-card deck** (3 different deck slots, so duplicates in the deck can show twice) and sends them in the Player attribute `CardOffer` (JSON: `t` = phase start, `cards`, `deck` = filler cards for the spinning reels).
- **Roll animation** (`CardRoll` LocalScript): the screen dims, CHOOSE YOUR CARD drops in. **Reel 1 (left) pops up and spins, 0.5 s later reel 2 (middle), 0.5 s later reel 3** (`APPEAR` 0.15 / 0.65 / 1.15 s, each spins `ROLL` 1.5 s, landing at about 1.65 / 2.15 / 2.65 s). Each reel winds back a hair, launches into a blur (speed streaks, red pay line, ticking notches), slows card by card, overshoots and clunks back. The card shown just before the final one is the rarest other card in your deck (a near miss). Landing: white flash, rarity-coloured rotating burst + sparks, shock ring from Rare up, **screen shake + coloured edge pulse from Mythic up**, the rarity name pops above the reel.
- **v5.8 clean reels:** the reel housing has **no fill** any more (its dark square showed in the card's rounded corners while rolling and behind the landed card), only the thin rounded outline, red tab and notches; the reel window is a rounded **CanvasGroup** (`CARD_R` = the card's own 9 px corner at 250 wide, 11 px at 300), so the spinning cards, top / bottom shading, streaks and flash are clipped to the card shape (no black gaps between cards either); the shading fades away on landing; the dim over unpicked cards uses the card radius; and on a pick **every reel's frame fades out** (`fadeHousing`) instead of turning into an opaque box (picked) or a grey see-through box (the other two).
- **Pick:** when the last reel has landed, a **10 s timer** (`CARD_PICK`) starts (number + bar, red for the last 3 s). Hover = the card lifts, grows and glows, the others dim. **Click / tap or keys 1 / 2 / 3** to pick. The middle card has an **AUTO PICK** tag: **no pick in 10 s = the middle card**. The server only accepts a pick once the reels are done and assigns the middle card itself when time runs out. Then LOCKED IN (or AUTO PICKED) stamps over the card, the other two drop away, WAITING FOR THE OTHER PLAYERS. If everyone has picked the phase ends early.
- **Your card for the whole match:** Player attribute `MatchCard` (card id). When the countdown starts the card flies to the **HUD: bottom right, above the item row**, a small live card (112 px) with YOUR CARD / name / rarity next to it (`CardHUD` ScreenGui, made by `CardRoll`). Shown during Countdown, Playing and SlowMo.
- **v5.14: 8 cards read `MatchCard` for gameplay (4.4).** Making another card work = read `player:GetAttribute("MatchCard")` (or `CardEffects.Has(player, id)`) in the script that owns that mechanic, put its numbers in `CardEffects`, and add `works = true` to its line in `Cards.List`.
- **v5.9 hover fix:** a hovered card lifts 14 px and grows to 1.05 (was 16 px / 1.06); its rarity name (and the AUTO PICK tag over the middle card) now sit above the cards in the draw order and **ride up with the lifted / enlarged / picked card** (`liftLabels`), so the card's border and glow never cover them. The line "3 CARDS FROM YOUR DECK // ONE CARD FOR THE WHOLE MATCH" is removed.

### 4.3 Rarities + the card look (`Cards`, `CardArt`)
| Rarity | Colour | Frame extras |
|---|---|---|
| Common | grey | paper grain, simple frame |
| Uncommon | green | + vine curls in the corners, faint vein lines |
| Rare | blue | + double frame, blue gem, fine line texture, slow shine |
| **Epic (v4.9)** | **purple** | + double frame, violet gem, faceted amethyst light (long crossing facet lines + a violet glow), amethyst crystal clusters in the corners, a soft violet inner glow line, 3 twinkles |
| Legendary | yellow / gold | + sunburst, gold corner brackets, gem with glow, sparkles, faster shine |
| Mythic | red | + flames along the bottom, rising embers, pulsing glow, gold brackets |
| Divine | **watercolour rainbow (v4.9)** | pastel watercolour paper (rose / peach / butter / mint / sky / lilac, `R.wash` in `Cards`) with 14 overlapping colour blooms + lighter centres + pigment speckles, a pastel prism edge that turns, rainbow gem, white corner brackets, white star sparkles. Deliberately soft, not a hard full-saturation rainbow (owner: "watercolor of different colors"). Was plain purple. |
| Signature | white / black | black + white op-art stripes that drift, red Breakline slash, holo foil sweep, the art is split: dark ink on white left, white chalk on black right |
- **Every card:** picture on top (hand-inked illustration), **name** on a dark plate, **description under the name** (handwritten font on a parchment box), **rarity name at the bottom**, background = rarity colour.
- **Art style (owner: "do not make them look AI"):** everything is drawn from plain GUI frames by `CardArt`: wobbly pen strokes with a faint second pass, pen circles that overshoot, cross-hatching and short shading strokes, watercolour washes that sit slightly off the lines, paper speckles and burnt edges. Each card has its own drawing (`art` key -> `Painters` in `CardArt`), seeded by the card id so it never changes. No image uploads needed.
- **Rarity ranks (v4.9):** common 1, uncommon 2, rare 3, **epic 4**, legendary 5, mythic 6, divine 7, signature 8 (`Cards.Rarities`). `CardArt` keeps its old frame-style numbers internally (`CardArt.Style`, epic = 3.5). `CardRoll`: the "rare" landing sound now plays from Epic (softer) / Legendary (full), screen shake + edge pulse from Mythic up (thresholds moved with the ranks).
- **The 32 placeholder cards** (`Cards.List`): Common - Steady Hands, Light Feet, Thick Skin, Deep Pockets, Long Breath, Box Hugger, Ember Heart, Scrap Plating, Lucky Coin. Uncommon - Ghost Step, Mender's Thread, Hot Barrel, Magnet Palms, Grave Candle, Scout's Eye, Skeleton Key, Paper Crane. Rare - Tidebreaker, Moonlit Reload, Hollow Point. **Epic (v4.9) - Stormcaller, Lantern Keeper, Glass Cannon** (moved up from Rare). Legendary - Crown of the Line, Sun Eater, Golden Hour, Oathblade. Mythic - The Red Serpent, Bloodmoon Pact. Divine - Halo of Ash, Seraph's Eye. Signature - The Breakline.
- **Adding a card:** add a line to `Cards.List` (id, rarity, art, name, desc) and, for new art, a painter `P.<art> = function(d, R) ... end` in `CardArt` (222 x 168 canvas; `d:line / curve / circle / arc / wash / hatch / shade / leaf / flame / heart / cloud / star / twinkle`). Copy both modules to both games.
- **Card performance (v4.9 pass inside v4.8):** a card is ~400 GUI objects, so drawing every card in the collection made the CARD INDEX lag (worst on tablets). Now: (1) **`CardIndex` only keeps the cards near the screen alive** - each collection cell is an empty frame until it scrolls within one row of the window (`updateVisible`, 10 times a second, at most 4 cells filled per tick), and it is emptied again when it leaves; the deck minis do the same (`updateMiniVisible`); closing the page empties everything. (2) **`CardArt` keeps a template cache:** a static card (`anim = false`) is drawn once, then `Build` returns a *clone* (no re-drawing, no text measuring); the 36 most recently used templates are kept (`TEMPLATE_MAX`). (3) **Level of detail:** cards up to 260 px wide are drawn LITE (no faint second pen pass, fewer paper grains, sparser hatch), up to 130 px (deck minis) MICRO (also skips hair-thin strokes, about 55 % fewer objects). The random seed is unchanged, so every card looks the same as before, just with fewer invisible bits. Animated cards (inspect view, card roll winners, HUD card) are still drawn fresh. `CardArt.ClearCache()` drops the cache. The look of the cards did not change.
- **v4.9 CARD LAG - the real cause + the real fix:** (1) **Bug:** the collection grid called `CardArt.Build(slot, id, { width = CARD_W })` without `anim = false`, so every card that scrolled into view was drawn FRESH as an animated card (300-700 GUI frames + looping tweens) - the template cache was never used. Now `anim = false`. (2) Cells are only emptied once they are **3 rows** away (filled within 1.2 rows), so scrolling up and down does not rebuild the same cards; 6 fills per tick. (3) **Bug:** the deck panel called `updateMiniVisible` before it was defined (`task.defer(nil)` errored on every deck change) - forward-declared now. (4) **BAKED CARDS:** every card was rendered offline into **4 sprite sheets** (`BreaklineCards_1..4.png`, 1000 x 700, 8 cards each, 250 x 350 per card, transparent rounded corners; drawn from the exact `CardArt` frames by an offline Luau + Python rasteriser, text left out). Once the 4 image ids are pasted into **`CardArt.SHEETS`** (top of `CardArt`, both games), every STATIC card (collection grid, deck minis, roll reels) is **one ImageLabel + the live name / description / rarity text = ~10 GUI objects instead of ~600**. Animated cards (inspect view, roll winners, HUD card) are still drawn live. A card missing from `BAKED_MAP`, or whose rarity changed since the bake (`BAKED_RARITY`), falls back to the drawn version automatically. **Changing a card's art / rarity or adding a card = the sheets must be re-baked** (ask the AI to "re-bake the card sheets"; the bake scripts read `Cards` + `CardArt` directly). `opts.baked = false` skips it. `CardArt.IsBaked(id)`.
- **v5.14 PLACEHOLDER card** (`placeholder`, art `mystery` = a big inked question mark over two blank fanned cards, `Cards.List`, `placeholder = true`): the stand-in for every card-shop slot until the real pack cards exist. `CardArt.Build(..., { rarity = "epic" })` (new `opts.rarity`) draws ANY card in another rarity's frame, so the shop shows the placeholder as each slot's rarity. The placeholder is **not on the baked sheets on purpose**: it is drawn live (the automatic fallback), so **no rebake was needed**. Rebake once the real pack cards are made (ask the AI to "re-bake the card sheets").
- Card list, balance and the remaining 24 abilities are still **TBD**.

### 4.4 Working cards (v5.14: `CardEffects` module + the scripts below, Breakline)
All numbers live in `ReplicatedStorage.CardEffects` (Breakline). The card counts from the moment it is picked (`MatchCard`) to the end of the match, every life.
| Card | Rarity | What it does | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Ember Heart** | Common | Health starts regenerating **1 s sooner** (12 s -> 11 s without damage) | `Vitals.RegenDelayFor` |
| **Thick Skin** | Common | **+10 max health** (250 -> 260); you get the 10 the moment you pick it, then every respawn | `Vitals.MaxFor` + `MatchCard` listener |
| **Deep Pockets** | Common | **Every life starts with one extra magazine loaded** (e.g. M4A1 60 / 30 until the first reload; reloads are normal after that). The ammo system has no reserve ammo (reloads are free), so "spawn with one extra magazine" became this; the card text says so. Not given again after a reserve gun swap | `GunServer` + `GunClient` (both track it, the card picked after the spawn still loads it if the gun was not used yet) |
| **Hot Barrel** | Uncommon | **+10 % fire rate for 4 s after a kill** (gun or gadget kill, player or bot; each kill restarts it). Attribute `HotBarrelUntil` (server time) | `GunServer` (rate check), `GadgetServer`, `GunClient` (shot timing) |
| **Grave Candle** | Uncommon | **Teammates' souls show through walls**: a flickering candle-light ball with a Highlight drawn on top of everything + a flame + distance tag at any range | `CardEffectsClient` |
| **Scout's Eye** | Uncommon | **Enemies you hit are outlined red through walls for 2 s** (every hit restarts it; guns + gadgets) | `CardEffectsClient` (reads `HitConfirm`) |
| **Hollow Point** | Rare | **Headshots +12 % damage** | `GunServer` |
| **Glass Cannon** | Epic | **+15 % damage dealt** (guns + gadgets, not to yourself) **and +15 % damage taken** (everything through `Vitals.Damage`) | `GunServer`, `GadgetServer`, `Vitals.Damage` |

## 5. Economy & Shop

### 5.1 Currencies
- **Souls** (blue glowing ball; was "Coins") — earned by playing: missions, kills, wins, etc. **v5.19 STARTING SOULS (owner): a new player gets 3,000 souls the moment they make the starter pick** (1 weapon + 1 movement; `ShopServer` `STARTER_SOULS`, added with `Profiles.Increment` right after `StarterDone` is set, so it can only happen once; the pick message says `+3,000 souls to start`). Players who already made their pick get nothing extra. **v5.10 icon (`UIKit.SoulIcon`): a small glowing GHOST** - rounded head, scalloped hem, two eyes, soft halo, a glimmer; pale at the head to soul blue at the hem (the old diamond did not look like a soul).
- **Essence** (v5.9, the second currency, **premium**: meant to be bought with Robux) — purple, icon = **three small purple glimmers** (four-point sparkles, one big + two small; `UIKit.EssenceIcon`, colours `UIKit.C.essence` / `essencePale`). Saved in the profile as `Essence` (both games, `ProfileStore`), mirrored to the Player attribute `Essence`. **v5.10 icon (`UIKit.EssenceIcon`): a faceted purple GEM** - a crown + a pavilion (two clipped diamonds), girdle line, table highlight, centre facet line, glimmer.
  - **Safe by design for real money:** Essence is never changed with `Set` / `Increment`. `ProfileStore.GrantEssence(player, amount, receiptId)` and `ProfileStore.SpendEssence(player, amount)` write straight to the DataStore with `UpdateAsync` on the saved value (no lost or doubled Essence even if the other place saved in between) and only report success once it is saved. `GrantEssence` remembers the last 50 purchase ids (`EssenceReceipts`), so a repeated `ProcessReceipt` call never pays out twice: a future `ProcessReceipt` handler returns `PurchaseGranted` only when `GrantEssence` returned true, otherwise `NotProcessedYet` (Roblox retries).
  - **Lobby display:** right of the souls. **Home screen (no menu open):** both bigger, each with its **name** (SOULS / ESSENCE in its colour) above its balance; **v5.11: on a slight dark plate (hairline border) with bigger names (18 px Oswald, were 13 px mono), and the souls icon there is the ghost too (`LobbyClient` still drew the old diamond).** **Play screen / menus open:** compact, icon + balance only (top bar, left of BACK). The **shop** box shows both too.
  - **Not built yet:** buying Essence (Developer Products + a purchase menu + `ProcessReceipt`; the owner creates the products in the Creator Dashboard and sends their ids), and anything to spend it on. **TBD:** pack sizes + prices, what Essence buys.

### 5.2 Earning coins
- Completing **missions** (daily/weekly — TBD)
- **Kills**
- **Winning** a match
- Other sources TBD (match completion, objectives like plugging/breaking boxes, etc.)

### 5.3 Shop sells
- **Skins**
- **Guns**
- **Gadgets**
- **Movement abilities**
- **Cards:** roll **crates** (random cards) or buy **individual cards on sale** — **v5.14: built as the CARDS tab, souls only (5.9).**

### 5.9 Card shop (v5.14: `CardShop` + `CardShopUI` modules, `CardShopServer`, `ShopClient` CARDS tab - Lobby)
- **Where:** the SHOP (button on the left / shop NPC) has a 4th tab **CARDS** next to GUNS / GADGETS / MOVEMENT. It has its **own layout** (no item grid + detail panel): **everything costs SOULS, never Essence.**
- **DAILY SHOP (top):** title + `NEW CARDS IN hh:mm:ss`. **Three big cards side by side** (250 px wide, each in its own panel with a soft rarity glow, name, rarity, price, **BUY -> CONFIRM**). **Every day at 00:00 UTC** the game picks 3 different cards **only from the cards inside the packs that are on sale** (`CardShop.DailyPool`, not every card in the game), each pick weighted by its rarity (`CardShop.DailyWeight`: common 40, uncommon 26, rare 16, epic 9, legendary 5, mythic 2.5, divine 1, signature 0.5). The pick is seeded by the day number, so **every lobby server shows the same shop**. Prices (`DailyPrice`): common 150, uncommon 300, rare 600, epic 1,000, legendary 1,800, mythic 3,000, divine 4,500, signature 6,000 souls. **Each daily card can be bought once per day** (`BOUGHT - BACK TOMORROW`; profile key `CardShopDaily = { day, bought }`).
- **CARD PACKS (under it):** **3 per row**, each panel: the pack's **own cover art** (drawn in code, `CardShopUI.PackArt`: foil gradient body with a diagonal sheen, crimped ends with teeth, a dashed TEAR line, BREAKLINE, a ringed emblem, name + tag), name, tag, one line about it, the price, **BUY** (asks twice) and a **square EYE button** next to it. **One card per pack.**
  - **FIRST STEPS** (green, two footprints) - **300 souls** - beginner pack: common 60 %, uncommon 26 %, rare 10 %, epic 4 %.
  - **SOUL PACK** (soul blue, the soul ghost) - **750 souls** - the first "normal" pack, every rarity inside: common 42, uncommon 22, rare 16, epic 9, legendary 6, mythic 3, divine 1.5, signature 0.5 %.
  - **LEVEL UP** (gold, three stacked chevrons) - **1,500 souls** - advanced pack, rare and up: rare 28, epic 30, legendary 21, mythic 12, divine 6, signature 3 %.
  - **12 cards per pack** (`CardShop.Packs[...].entries`, weights add up to 100 so they read as percent). **All 36 slots are the PLACEHOLDER card for now**, each in its slot's rarity. To put a real card in: replace `id = "placeholder"` with the card id (a real card always uses its own rarity). `onSale = false` hides a pack (and takes its cards out of the daily shop).
- **EYE view:** a big panel: the pack (big), name, price, **chance per rarity** (bars), and **every card in the pack** (4 x 3: the card, its rarity, its **chance**, its name), sorted rarest first. CLOSE / ESC / click outside.
- **Pack opening (after the server rolled the card):** the screen goes dark and a **spotlight** (fanned soft light from above + a pool of light) falls on the pack, which drops in and trembles; an **anime-style slash** streaks across the tear line with a white flash and speed lines, the **torn top spins away** and light pours out, the pack drops away; then **the cards spin round on a circle** (front cards travel **left -> right**, back cards return, depth = size + shade) and slow down over ~5.4 s until the won card stops in front; the others fall away, a rarity-coloured flash + slow rotating rays, the card **grows big** (live animated card) with its rarity above it, and **CLAIM** is the only button. **SKIP ROLL** (bottom middle) jumps to the result while it spins. ESC does nothing while a pack opens (and the shop cannot close under it).
- **Server (`CardShopServer`, remotes `CardShopRemotes.GetState / BuyDaily / BuyPack`):** checks the price + souls, rolls with `CardShop.RollPack` (the same odds the EYE view shows), adds the copy to the profile key `Cards`, re-publishes `CardsJson` + `DeckJson` and saves at once; one purchase at a time, 0.35 s rate limit.
- New copies go straight into the collection (CARD INDEX). Owning more than 4 copies is fine (the deck still takes at most 4).
- **v5.16 POLISH (owner Nov 8, `CardShopUI` v1.1):** (1) **FIRST STEPS cover = a SEEDLING** (ground line + two seeds, a curved stem in three strokes, two leaves with a vein + highlight, a bud) - the two footprints looked AI-drawn and are gone. (2) The small "1 CARD // 12 POSSIBLE" line is replaced by **"12 CARDS"** (17 px Oswald, white, on a dark plate with an accent frame; the number comes from the pack's entries). (3) **BREAKLINE** at the top of every pack is **bigger (19 px Oswald) and white with an ink outline** (was a small pale mono line). (4) **NEW CARDS IN hh:mm:ss sits right next to the DAILY SHOP title** (a horizontal list: title, then the timer with the time in white) instead of at the far right edge. (5) Opening: the **stacked "pool of light" ellipses above SKIP ROLL are gone** and the spotlight beam fades out higher up. (6) **The slash is a thin anime crescent**: one tapered arc (sharp ends, ~7 px in the middle) built from 40 short segments, revealed tip-first in 0.085 s, with two thinner coloured afterimages trailing it, a glint at the tip, an **impact frame** (a dark blink, then the white flash), and it thins to a hairline and slides away; the speed lines are tapered (solid at the cut, fading at the tip). No more blocky bars. (7) **CLAIM is a polished button:** rarity-coloured glossy gradient (red for Signature), a light frame + a fine top highlight, a soft pulsing glow behind it, a shine that sweeps across every 2.4 s, diamond marks either side of the word, pops in (Back ease), grows on hover, squashes when pressed. Packs are drawn live (no images), so **nothing to re-bake**.

### 5.4 Skins (v5.10 / Skins v11: ALL THREE OUTFITS REBUILT with real gear detail + eight shop colourways; v5.5: Skins v10 - OPERATOR + SOULBOUND simplified, new CHASSIS robot skin; v5.4: Skins v9 polish - slim gloves, guns visible in the menus, chamfered blocks; v5.3: BACK TO BLOCKY - pure Studio code, refined block outfits + block hands; mesh skins + the Blender salvager are dropped)
- `ReplicatedStorage/Skins` **v9** (ModuleScript, **both games**, identical copies; v9 = v8 + the Nov 4 polish below). API unchanged (`Skins.List / Get / Is / Dress / NewFigure / Proportions / Prepare / ApplyProportions`).
- **v5.3 DECISION (owner, Nov 4):** the authored-mesh direction (v5.1 / v5.2: SkinModels folder, Meshy, the Blender salvager) and the smooth "anatomy kit" (v5.0) are both **dropped**. Skins go back to the **total blocky look with blocky hands**, like the early versions, but refined so they look as good as blocky can: clean big blocks, good detail. **Everything is built in code, nothing is imported.** The `ReplicatedStorage.SkinModels` folder is no longer read (delete it if you made one).
- **The block look (rules for every skin):**
  1. **Body = the classic blocky avatar.** Every R15 part is ONE clean block exactly the size of the real part (`K.core`; **v9: with chamfered vertical edges** - two crossing blocks, the same 7 % bevel `K.BEVEL` on every body part, matching the bevelled head, so the whole figure shares one edge language), so the silhouette is the stock blocky Roblox body (with `Skins.Proportions`: tall + lean). The hit boxes are still the invisible R15 parts (gameplay unchanged).
  2. **Detail = bigger blocks on top.** Plates, pouches, straps, pads, cuffs, collars, laces, helmet, hood, mask. Each layer is a few % bigger than the layer under it, so **two different colours never share a face** (no flicker / z-fighting). Few big readable blocks, 2-4 tones per material; nothing smaller than about 0.03 studs.
  3. **Block hands** (`K.hand`, **v9 = slim gloves**): a glove block **narrower and shallower than the sleeve** (`K.HAND` = 0.66 x hand width, 0.62 x depth, 1.4 x length; the v8 mitten was 1.02 x 0.94 x 1.7 and swallowed every gun), and a wrist cuff that bridges sleeve and glove. **v5.6 (owner: "literally just has to be a blocky hand"): the knuckle plate and the thumb block are gone** - a hand is one plain glove block. Same hands on the character and the menu figure. The **first-person hands are plain blocks too** (`GunModels.HandParts` v9: ONE mitten block per hand + a wrist block - no finger blocks, finger lines, thumb, index finger, web or back-pad; the first-person sleeves + cuffs are square blocks) and the **gadget hand** (`GadgetModels.BuildHand` v9: palm, one finger-side block, wrist).
  4. **Joint fillers** (`K.fillers`): a 45 degree turned block on every elbow and knee pivot, so a bent blocky limb never shows a gap or a hollow corner.
  5. **Head** (`K.head`): a cube with bevel blocks on the 4 vertical edges (reads as a head, not a crate) and a block face: brows, eyes with a small white highlight, nose, optional mouth / ears.
  6. **Materials:** `Fabric` cloth, `Leather` belts / holster / straps, `Metal` hardware, `SmoothPlastic` skin / plates / boots, `Glass` lenses + the lantern, `Neon` ONLY soul glow (SOULBOUND eyes + lantern core). **No team colours on skins** (team + party = outlines, 5.4c).
  7. Budget: about **130 parts** a skin (v9: OPERATOR 137, SOULBOUND 127 incl. hands). All rules for new skins: **5.4d**.
- **Builder:** `B.blk(base, size, pos, color, mat, rotDeg)` = block, `B.wdg(...)` = wedge (Roblox WedgePart: tall side at +Z, slope falls to the front), `B.box(base, size, cframe, ...)`. `base` = the R15 part it is welded to (`"UpperTorso"`, `"LeftLowerArm"` ...). Positions are from the CENTRE of that part, -Z = front, +X = the character's right (on both sides). Sizes are fractions of the real part size (`Z[part]`) or studs x `Z.u`, so the lobby figure (`FIG_SCALE`) matches the player exactly. `Z.h` = head size. Kit: `K.head`, `K.core`, `K.band` (belts / cuffs / straps round a part), `K.hand`, `K.boot` (shaft, collar, laces, foot block with a toe, toe slope wedge, sole, toe cap, heel), `K.fillers`.
- **SKINS v11 (v5.10, Nov 7) - owner: "make them look not like AI ... all three ... sometimes basic is better, and all you did was strip some items off, that is not what I meant: you can have detail and still look good and normal ... put everything into these skins ... redo them completely".** v10 removed clutter; v11 puts the detail back, but as DESIGNED kit instead of loose bits. What makes gear look designed, and what every outfit now follows: (1) **every strap runs to something** (chin strap -> chin band, holster hip strap -> platform, bandolier -> vial holders), (2) **every pouch is the same 3-part pattern** (body + flap + snap, `K.pouch` / `K.sidePouch`), (3) **paired pieces are symmetric**, only a few deliberate asymmetries remain, (4) **layering in steps**: a base layer, a gear layer 0.12 thick, trim / piping 0.06, snaps 0.034+, (5) **a seam / piping line where two materials meet**, (6) the **same 2-3 colours repeated** across head / torso / legs, (7) nothing sticks out more than 0.35 studs sideways, (8) **no random tilts** (45 degrees only; the only other angle is the 6 degree brow from `K.head`). New kit: `K.pouch`, `K.sidePouch`, `B.cyl` (cylinders for robot pistons / joints / pipes only).
  - **OPERATOR (160 parts)** - "a tactical operator, built like real kit". **Head:** ballistic helmet (shell, crown, brim band, elastic band, side rails, rear counterweight pouch), headset ear cups with arms + a left mic boom, goggles (strap, frame, two lenses, ONE red clip), gaiter (folded top edge + chin cinch band), chin straps. **Torso:** charcoal combat shirt with a collar, coyote plate carrier (front + back bag, top / bottom piping, shoulder straps with keepers, cummerbund with PALS webbing on both sides), admin panel + name tape, **three mag pouches** (body + flap + snap), back handle (two posts + bar) + two vertical straps. **Waist:** battle belt + buckle + two belt pouches (olive left, coyote right). **Arms:** sleeves with an outer seam, elbow pad + cap, velcro strap, cuff, plain glove; **left**: the red Breakline patch (two white bars) + a watch. **Legs:** cargo trousers; **left cargo pocket** (pouch pattern) / **right drop-leg holster** (hip strap, platform, pistol, grip, retention strap); thigh straps, hard-shell knee pads (under-layer + shell + two straps), blousing bands, laced boots with white soles. Palette: charcoal + coyote + olive (+ one darker each), red only on the arm patch + goggle clip.
  - **SOULBOUND (154 parts)** - "a pallbearer who carries the dead". **Head:** deep hood (crown, sides, back, peak, stitched rim under the peak, stitched front edges, back seam, cowl round the neck), **bone mask** (plate, brow ridge, nose bridge + hole, cheek carving, jaw plate, two sockets with a soul glow, mouth slit + five stitches, a crack over the left eye). **Torso:** navy coat with a bone shirt V, placket + four bone buttons, lapels, **high collar with a bone rim**, **two-layer mantle with a bone edge**, leather **bandolier with three soul vials** (holder + glass tube + bone cap), short back cape + bone spine stitch. **Waist:** belt, **ragged split tail** (front pairs with outer / inner strips, bone hem lines; back with three strips), **soul lantern** on the right, **key ring + two keys** on the left. **Arms:** coat sleeves, dark cuffs + a bone cuff line; **left** layered bone pauldron (two lames, spike, strap), **right** three bandage wraps; bone-wrapped hands. **Legs:** trousers with a front crease, bone knee plates, three bone shin wraps, tall leather boots with two buckled straps each. Glow: only the mask eyes + the lantern core (the vials are plain glass).
  - **CHASSIS (118 parts)** - "a worn wall-crew robot". **Head:** steel head, bezelled screen with two warm eyes + brow lids, speaker grille, jaw seam, ear modules with discs, vented cap plate, antenna (base, stalk, orange tip). **Torso:** steel chest, neck ring, **orange panel in a graphite frame**, **hazard band with five orange chevrons**, one status lamp, back pack + orange stripe + **two exhaust pipes**. **Waist:** hip band, groin plate, two hip pods with caps. **Arms:** shoulder caps (**orange left / steel-dark right**) on **hydraulic pistons**, elbow joints, slatted forearms, wrist rings, graphite gloves. **Legs:** thigh side plates, **thigh pistons with mounts**, knee joint + guard, orange shin stripe, ankle rings, heavy toe-capped boots. Glow: screen eyes + one status lamp.
  - **EDITIONS (the cosmetics shop, `Skins.List` entries with `base` + `price`; the three base skins stay FREE for everybody):** souls - **OPERATOR // ARCTIC** 1,400, **SOULBOUND // ASHEN** 1,600, **CHASSIS // RUSTBUCKET** 1,100, **CHASSIS // COBALT** 1,800; essence - **OPERATOR // NIGHTFALL** 300, **SOULBOUND // EMBERBOUND** 450, **SOULBOUND // GILDED** 650, **CHASSIS // CHROME** 500. An edition = the same `DRESS[base]` with changed colours (`edition(...)` in `Skins`). `Skins.Price(id)` / `Skins.IsFree(id)`; `S.Dress` / `NewFigure` build `DRESS[skin.base or skin.id]`. Prices live ONLY in `Skins` / `GunSkins` (the server reads them).
  - **Offline check (v11):** a Luau stand-in for Vector3 / CFrame / Color3 runs the real `DRESS` code, dumps every block, and a Python script checks it: **one connected group, no part under 0.03 studs, sideways overhang <= 0.35 studs, only 45 degree turns (+ the 6 degree brow)** - all three base skins and all eight editions pass (OPERATOR 160 / SOULBOUND 154 / CHASSIS 118 parts incl. hands; hard limit 180).
  - **Figure option `stance`:** `Skins.NewFigure(parent, skin, { weapon, gunSkin, stance = "aim" })` holds a gun pose forever (used by the win line-up). `gunSkin` paints the held gun (5.6).

- **(SUPERSEDED by v11 above - kept for history) THE THREE SKINS (v10, Nov 5):** owner: "they still kinda look like AI ... sometimes simple and basic is actually better". What reads as AI is **clutter**: dozens of tiny pouches, tabs, buckles, straps, antennas, hems and tilted bits that no designer would draw. v10 removes all of it and keeps only the shapes that carry the identity: one clear read on the head, one on the torso, one on the legs. **No random tilts at all** (the only angle left is the 45 degree bandolier on SOULBOUND), one darker shade per main colour, every detail flush. Parts: OPERATOR **91** (was 137), SOULBOUND **95** (was 127), CHASSIS **73**; all three are one connected group, nothing under 0.03 studs, nothing sticks out more than 0.35 studs (offline check, `tools/skinqc`).
  - **OPERATOR (base skin, everyone starts with it)** - "a clean tactical operator". **Head:** coyote helmet (shell, crown, dark brim band, ear skirts) with goggles pushed up on the front (strap, frame, two dark lenses, ONE red clip), olive gaiter up to the nose, block face. **Torso:** charcoal jacket, coyote plate carrier (front + back bag, dark cummerbund, two shoulder straps) with three mag pouches under ONE flap. **Legs:** olive cargos, belt + buckle, left cargo pocket, **drop-leg holster on the right thigh** (platform, grip, one strap), coyote knee pads, boots with white soles. **Left arm:** the red Breakline patch + white "break line" mark (two offset bars). **Palette:** 3 main colours (charcoal / coyote / olive) + one darker shade each; red only on the arm patch + the goggle clip. **Removed in v10:** admin panel + name tape, hydration pouch, radio + antenna, dump pouch, shoulder + cuff straps, chin straps, helmet back pouch + top patch, rails, zip / seams, laces, right-arm pad, second knee-pad plate, leg-pocket flap. Asymmetry: patch left, holster right, pocket left.
  - **SOULBOUND (soul skin)** - "a pallbearer". **Head:** deep navy hood (crown, two sides, back, peak) whose dark inside is the face, **bone mask** (plate, brow ridge, two sockets with a soul glow each, mouth bar with three stitches, a small stepped crack over the LEFT eye). **Torso:** navy coat, bone collar V, two lapels, mantle, **ONE bandolier** (front + back, both at 45 degrees) with three bone charms. **Waist:** belt, split coat tail (front pair + back, straight, no flare), **soul lantern on the front-right of the belt** (bracket, hanger, cap, base, glass, neon core). **Arms:** coat sleeves, dark cuffs, **bone pauldron on the LEFT shoulder** (two lames + a spike), bone-bandaged block hands. **Legs:** dark trousers, two bone shin wraps, tall near-black leather boots. **Palette:** navy + bone + near-black leather; the soul glow (mask eyes + lantern) is the only accent. **Removed in v10:** the hood tail + rim, mask cheeks / nose ridge / extra teeth, coat buttons, back cape, side coat panels, blue hems + cuffs + shirt (the V is bone now), thigh strap + pouch, right-arm strap + charm, lantern posts + finial, the third shin wrap, all flow tilts. Asymmetry: pauldron left, lantern right, crack left.
  - **CHASSIS (NEW, v10 - robot skin)** - "a worn wall-crew robot": the box robots of Wall Push, grown up. **Head:** boxy steel head (Metal), dark screen face with **two warm glowing eyes** (the only glow), graphite ear modules, steel-dark cap plate, one short antenna with an orange tip on the RIGHT. **Torso:** steel chest, graphite neck ring, **one safety-orange chest panel** over a graphite grille, graphite back pack with one orange stripe. **Waist:** graphite, steel-dark hip band, two hip pods. **Arms:** steel upper arms with shoulder caps (**orange on the LEFT, steel-dark on the right**), steel-dark forearms, graphite wrist bands + slim graphite gloves. **Legs:** graphite thighs, steel-dark shins with a steel knee guard + an orange stripe, graphite boots. **Palette:** steel + graphite + orange (one darker shade each), screen glow = accent. Menu card colour orange. Built from the same kit (`K.head` got an optional `mat` so the head can be Metal); same hands, same hit boxes, same bulk rules.
- **The menu figure IS the in-game model** (`Skins.NewFigure`): a virtual R15 rig dressed by the same `DRESS` code, scaled by `FIG_SCALE`, arms solved with 2-bone IK. **v8:** the block hands ride on the forearm (`base.<Side>Hand` = forearm x wrist, like the real rig), so the figure wears the same hands as the player; the gun no longer brings its own hands to the figure. Holding a gun the shoulders sit a little closer (`Z.shX` = torso half + 0.15 x arm width) so both hands reach every gun; empty-handed figures use the real rig width (arms hang straight down beside the body). **v9 SHOWCASE HOLD:** the gun is held **across the chest**, muzzle forward-left and a little down (`S.FigureTune` = gunScale 0.8 (= the in-game world gun), grip -0.4 / -0.05 / 0.6, dir 1.3 / -0.5, blade -0.25), so the camera sees the side of the gun instead of looking down the barrel; the gloves sit just **under** the gun (`dropR` 0.2 / `dropL` 0.18: fist round the pistol grip, palm cupping the handguard); `GunPose.aim` retuned (rot -0.15 / -0.75 / 0, pos 0.6 / 0.5 / -0.3) so the left hand still reaches in the aim idle. Measured offline (front camera, rifle): **~60 % of the gun visible, v8 was ~13 %**; both hands reach all 10 guns, nothing buried in the body. Used by every figure: lobby SKIN CATALOG cards, lobby squad / party stage, match intro / winner / warp cutscenes, soul reveal.
- **Adding a skin:** (1) entry in `Skins.List` with its colours (the old keys `Suit, Under, Armor, Trim, Accent, Plate, Cloth, Glove, Brand, White` must stay, menus read them); (2) `DRESS.<ID> = function(B, Z, C, opt)`: `K.head`, then torso blocks, then loop `{"Left","Right"}` for arms (`K.core` + details + `K.hand` unless `opt.noHands`) and legs (`K.core` + details + `K.boot`), then `K.fillers`; (3) offline preview (below); (4) owner screenshots from Studio.
- **Offline preview (AI sandbox, no Studio):** a small Roblox maths stand-in runs the real `Skins` code, dumps every block of the dressed rest pose, and Blender renders front / 3-4 / back / face views. It shows shape + colour, not Roblox lighting; the Studio screenshot is the final check.
- Old saves that say `VANDAL` / `SALVAGER` are fine (`Skins.Is` says no -> `OPERATOR`). Equipped skin = profile key `Skin` (default `OPERATOR`), mirrored to the Player attribute `Skin`; `AvatarService` (both games) dresses your character. Lobby skins: hanger button / TAB opens the SKIN CATALOG (2 skins); click = equip (`SetSkin`).
- **Figure idle moves (unchanged):** only with `opts.idle = true` (lobby squad stage).

### 5.4d SKIN QUALITY STANDARD (v5.4, owner Nov 4: "all skins made are on a certain level of quality so they can be sold in passes and stuff, and similar-ish to each other build wise")
- **v11 UPDATE (Nov 7):** the budget is now **70 to 180 parts including hands** (hard limit 180; v11: 160 / 154 / 118) because the owner asked for real detail, but "designed, not cluttered" still rules: strap-to-something, pouch pattern (body + flap + snap), symmetric pairs, piping where materials meet, snaps >= 0.034 studs, nothing under 0.03, sideways overhang <= 0.35, 45 degree turns only. **Cylinders (`B.cyl`) are allowed for ROBOT skins (pistons, joints, pipes) and hero props.** Neon: at most 2 glow SOURCES per skin (a source can be two matching eye parts).
Every skin - free, shop, battle pass, bundle - must pass this list before it ships. OPERATOR + SOULBOUND v9 are the reference skins: a new skin should look like it came from the same studio.
- **A. Same body, same kit.** Built only from the block kit (`K.head`, `K.core` with `K.BEVEL`, `K.band`, `K.hand`, `K.boot`, `K.fillers`) on `Skins.Proportions`. Never change body proportions, hit boxes or the slim glove size. **Fair play:** a skin may not add more than about 0.35 studs of bulk outside the body (helmets, hoods, pauldrons, packs), may not hide the body outline, and may not be near-invisible against the maps (overall brightness of the outfit must stay readable; no all-black / all-foliage outfits).
- **B. One identity, readable small.** A one-line identity (e.g. "a clean tactical operator", "a pallbearer"). One clear **read on the head** (helmet / hood / mask), **one on the torso** (carrier / coat / bandolier) and **one on the legs** (holster / tails / wraps). Test: as a black silhouette and as a 128 px thumbnail the skin is still recognisable next to the others.
- **C. Shape rules.** (1) Big / medium / small: about 60 % big shapes (body, plates, coat), 30 % medium (pouches, pads, pauldrons), 10 % small (tabs, buttons, laces); no clusters of tiny bits, nothing smaller than 0.03 studs. (2) Detail thickness only in steps: seam **0.03**, strap **0.06**, plate / pouch **0.12** studs (x `Z.u`). (3) Every detail sits **flush** on the surface under it: touching, never floating, never sunk more than half its thickness. (4) **Rotations:** axis aligned or 45 degrees. Allowed extras: ONE diagonal strap angle per skin (all diagonal straps share it) and ONE "flow tilt" of at most 12 degrees for hanging cloth / armour (coat tails, pauldron lames). No random tilts. (5) Blocks + wedges only on the body (no balls / cylinders), hero props may use cylinders. (6) Symmetric base + **1 to 3 deliberate asymmetric features** (OPERATOR: arm patch left, holster right, radio left; SOULBOUND: pauldron left, lantern right).
- **D. Colour + material.** (1) At most **3 main colours + 1 accent** + skin / bone neutral + black for straps; each main colour gets exactly one darker shade, nothing in between (no near-miss shades that look like a mistake). (2) Every main colour appears in **at least two body zones** (head / torso / legs) so the outfit ties together. (3) The accent appears in **at most 3 places**, always on purpose (brand marks, eyes, a hero detail). Breakline red is for the Breakline brand only. (4) Materials: Fabric cloth, Leather belts / holsters, Metal hardware, SmoothPlastic skin / plates / boots, Glass lenses; **Neon only for soul / premium glow, at most 2 glow sources, never team colours** (team = outlines).
- **E. Gun + hands.** The menu figure must hold **all 10 guns** with 0 hand gap in every idle pose, nothing through chest or head, and **at least 50 % of a rifle visible** from the front menu camera (offline check). Outfit pieces on the arms / hands must not cover the gun (no big gauntlets over the gloves).
- **F. Technical budget + QC.** **v10: 70 to 160 parts including hands (hard limit 180)** - fewer is fine, simple and clean beats busy (the floor was 110 before; the three v10 skins are 91 / 95 / 73). Offline QC (AI sandbox) must pass: one connected group (nothing floating), no visible coplanar faces in two colours (flicker), feet on the ground, figure check with every gun. Then Studio screenshots: front / back / 3-4, first person with a gun, lobby skin catalog card, squad / party stage, in-match third person. **Owner sign-off** before it goes on sale. **v10 offline check:** `tools/skinqc` runs the real `Skins` code against a small Roblox maths stand-in and prints per skin: part count, connected groups (must be 1), parts thinner than 0.03 studs (must be 0), neon parts, sideways overhang beyond the body (must be at most 0.35 studs).
- **G. Shop data.** Name = one word in caps, a short sub-line, a description of at most about 110 characters, card colour, the full colour table (old keys `Suit, Under, Armor, Trim, Accent, Plate, Cloth, Glove, Brand, White` included).
- **H. Tiers (PROPOSAL, not approved):** Standard = recolour / variant of an existing outfit; Rare = new outfit pieces on an existing silhouette; Epic = new head + torso silhouette + a hero prop; Legendary / Soul = everything in Epic + a glow (rule D4) + a unique hero prop. Prices and pass placement **TBD**.

### 5.4c Team + party outlines (v4.9, `TeamOutline` LocalScript, both games)
- Your **teammates** (same Team in a Breakline match) get a **blue outline** (`TEAM_COLOR`), your **party members** a **gold outline** (`PARTY_COLOR`, wins over blue), **through walls** (`Highlight`, DepthMode AlwaysOnTop, no fill except a 92 %-transparent tint). Enemies and you are never outlined; dead players lose it. Made on each client only (nobody else sees your outlines).
- Party members: Lobby = the `PartyMembers` attribute (PartyService). Breakline = **teleport data**: `LobbyServer.teleportGroup` now puts `{ party = { [userId] = { member ids } } }` into the TeleportOptions (cosmetic only, never trusted for gameplay); `TeamOutline` reads it with `GetLocalPlayerTeleportData`. Someone who rejoins Breakline without teleport data still sees team outlines.

### 5.4b Player level + XP (v3.8)
- Saved in the shared profile as `XP`; `ProfileStore` derives the Player attribute `Level` from it (`ReplicatedStorage.Levels`: level n needs `100 + 40*(n-1)` XP, max level 100).
- **Earned at the end of a Breakline match** (`MatchConfig.XP`, computed in `MatchServer.buildReward`, shown by `MatchFlow`): Base 40 + outcome (Win 60 / Draw 30 / Loss 20) + 12 per kill + 8 per box plugged + 10 per box destroyed + 2 per 100 damage. The breakdown is not shown, only the total on the XP bar.
- **Lobby:** a nameplate above every player's head (yours too): `[LV n] NAME` (`LobbyNameplates`).

### 5.5 Look & feel of the UI (v3.4)
- **Lobby = "CLEAN"** (replaces v3.2 POP, which read as childish): flat, sharp and dark, built like a big-shooter front end (CoD / Valorant / Apex lobby structure).
  - **Colors:** near-black panels, 1 px grey hairlines, white text, **Breakline red as the only loud color** (selected slot, leader, PLAY / START, edge light). Souls keep their pale blue. All in the `C` table at the top of `LobbyClient`.
  - **Type:** Oswald (condensed bold) for headings, names and numbers; Gotham SSm for body copy; Roboto Mono for small tactical labels (PRIMARY, GAME MODE, LEADER...). `FONTS` table.
  - **No** drifting shapes, floating cubes, bobbing / swaying, springy hovers, rounded pills or name bubbles. Corners are square; the only round shapes are the two icon buttons (skins, invite) next to PLAY.
  - **Hover (v3.4):** every button grows a few percent around its centre with a smooth 0.2 s Quint ease (`grow()` in `LobbyClient`; 1.03-1.08 depending on size) plus the color shift. No bounce.
  - **Logo (v3.7, `UIKit.Logo`, same everywhere: lobby top left, lobby loading screen, Breakline loading screen):** BREAK / LINE in **slim, widely spaced** condensed type (Oswald Light, letter by letter), a **thin red slash** between the words with a soft glow, and a **light that flows down the slash** every 2.4 s (bright streak + a gradient pass). No explosions.
  - **Souls:** faceted crystal icon (outlined blue diamond + bright core) + the number, top right. No pill, no W/L line. **v5.9:** Essence sits right of it (5.1); big with names on the home screen, compact while a menu is open.
  - **BACK / CLOSE buttons (v5.9, `UIKit.CloseButton`, used by the play screen, every overlay page, the Card Index + its inspect / rules panels, the shop and the Breakline big loadout picker):** a 152 x 42 dark plate with a light frame, a white **X**, the word (BACK / CLOSE) in bold, a small **ESC** key cap on the right; hover = red plate. Replaces the faint "ESC  BACK" text hints.
  - **Microcopy rule (v5.9, owner):** no small explainer lines where the screen already explains itself (removed: under SHOP, under the DECK panel, under GAME MODE, under INVITE, under the movement / gadget / weapon / skin pickers, under CHOOSE YOUR CARD, UNLOCK GEAR on the shop button, HOW THE CARD SYSTEM WORKS). Real game info stays (reserve = J while dead, right-click tip, starter pick line).
- **v5.11 PLAY SCREEN (replaces the layout below where they differ):** **left half = PLAYER CARDS** (5.8): yours always on top, two party slots under it (empty = INVITE, opens the invite screen); your card has **EDIT CARD** (cosmetics locker, PLAYER CARD tab) and, in a party, **LEAVE PARTY**. **The GAME MODES tab and the 3 squad slots above PLAY are gone.** The **podiums moved right** (`STAGE_CX` 1385: the right podium ends ~20 px from the right margin, above PLAY / INVITE); a party leader sees **PROMOTE / KICK on the front of each teammate's podium**; names above heads show PARTY LEADER / READY. The **loadout dock** sits bottom left under the cards. **PLAY opens the GAME MODES screen** (full screen over the stage): BREAKLINE and 1V1 as big cards (picture, name, tag, description, facts) each with a **READY UP** button, the locked placeholder modes as a row of small cards under them, `PARTY n / m READY` top right, BACK / ESC closes it. **READY UP:** solo = starts searching that mode at once (the button turns into CANCEL + the search timer); in a party every member readies up, the **leader's mode is the party's mode** (members see `LEADER PICKED <mode>` on the others), and the party searches as soon as everybody is ready for it; UNREADY / CANCEL undo it, any member can cancel a running search; 1v1 shows `SOLO ONLY` in a party. While searching, the PLAY block says CANCEL (click = stop). **INVITE (the + buttons on the home HUD and the play screen) opens its own INVITE screen** (LOBBY / FRIENDS tabs, search, player list on the left, YOUR PARTY on the right), not a page inside the play screen. Text removed: "BREAKLINE // 3V3" + "CLICK FOR LOADOUT" on the PLAY blocks, OPEN SLOT, MODE INFO, SQUAD.
- **v5.14 PLAY-SCREEN CARD INDEX BUTTON (`cardIndexButton(..., { big = true })` in `LobbyClient`):** the three-card fan sat low and poked out to the left of its space (its maths hung the cards 0.62 x size BELOW the middle); now it is centred in its own space, further right (x 58, was 34) and higher; **CARD INDEX** + the **DECK n / 32** line moved right with it (x 112, was 66) and the DECK line is **bigger (about 18 px Oswald, was 11 px mono) and brighter**. The home-screen button is unchanged.
- **v5.16 HOME-SCREEN CARD INDEX BUTTON (above PLAY, `cardIndexButton` without `big`):** the three-card fan is now **centred on the button's height** (it used the old maths that hung the cards 0.62 x size below the middle = too low) and sits a bit further right (x 34 -> 44); **CARD INDEX + DECK n / 32 moved right** (x 66 -> 92) to make room for it.
- **v5.12 LOBBY POLISH (owner Nov 7, replaces the lines below where they differ):** (1) **Home-screen currency plate is styled** (it was a plain black box): dark top-to-bottom gradient, a light outer frame + a fine inner frame, a **red accent bar on the left**, a hairline highlight along the top, a soft vertical **divider between SOULS and ESSENCE**; the plate sizes itself to its content (`currencyWidget` in `LobbyClient`). (2) **COSMETICS / INVITE labels under the round buttons on the home screen too** (only the play screen had them). (3) **The small line under PLAY is back:** home PLAY = `<last readied mode>  //  3V3` (default `BREAKLINE  //  3V3`, `lastMode` in `LobbyClient`); the play screen PLAY = `SELECT GAMEMODE`; the searching / ready texts are unchanged. (4) **GAME MODES screen: the READY UP text is centred in its button and bigger (36 px)** (one line = in the middle, two lines = stacked around the middle; the chevron stays right), and **clicking READY UP closes the game modes screen** (the PLAY block then shows READY / CANCEL + the search). (5) **CARD INDEX on the play screen moved from the top bar to the bottom row: right of the RESERVE tiles, between the loadout dock and the COSMETICS | PLAY | INVITE buttons** (x 980 - 1354, the PLAY row height minus 16). The home-screen CARD INDEX above PLAY is unchanged. (6) **Player cards:** new layout + no EDIT CARD button (5.8); LEAVE PARTY sits bottom left on your card. (7) **Card Index (?) DECK RULES button:** just the ring (the grey fill is gone), the ? is bigger (28 px) and centred on its own label; hover fills it red. (8) **Ability HUD:** the 3 dash charge pips sit 12 px lower (y 96 -> 108 in `AbilityClient`, both games) so they no longer cover the word DASH under the box.
- **Play screen layout (1920x1080 canvas, 56 px margins):**
  - **Top bar:** logo left; CARD INDEX, souls + Essence (compact), BACK button right; thin rule under it. No nav tabs.
  - **Stage (the whole background):** black space with stars (a few breathe very slowly), a faint horizon haze, a **slowly turning spiral galaxy** right behind the middle player (two arms, warm core, a few red specks, inner stars turn faster than outer ones) that now covers most of the screen (huge faint halo, long arms, many more stars; the ringed planet was removed in v3.6). The squad stands on **three dark stone pillars** that fall away into the void, centred in the middle area. **You always stand on the middle pillar** (red edge light), 1 teammate = left, 2 = left and right. **Names above heads are plain text** (small red PARTY LEADER line above the leader).
  - **Left: GAME MODES tab** (full height, **v4.2: a scrolling list**, `ScrollingFrame` in `LobbyClient`). Every mode is a **small card** showing just its name and tag. **Exactly one card is big** (map picture, name, tag, MODE INFO); **BREAKLINE is the big one by default**. **Click a small card** and it becomes the big card (the previous one shrinks), and the full mode page opens to the right with the picture, title, description, facts and SELECT. Click the open mode again to close the page. The SELECTED badge shows on whichever card is the selected mode, small or big. Live modes: **BREAKLINE** and **1V1**. Then the **locked placeholder modes** (hatched, padlock, COMING SOON, not selectable, same small size): RANKED, OVERLOAD, DEATHMATCH, SIEGE, FIRING RANGE. These names are **placeholders only (TBD)**; edit `LOCKED_MODES` in `LobbyClient`. Sizes: `LIVE_H` 178 (big), `SMALL_H` 78 (small), `LOCK_GAP` 10; the counter at the top reads live / total modes. The old fixed stack let the locked modes cover the 1v1 card; the scroll fixes that for any number of modes.
  - **Bottom middle: LOADOUT dock**, all square tiles of one size: **MOVE** | gap | **PRIMARY** | gap | **GADGETS** x3 (keys 2-4) | gap + slim line + gap | **RESERVE** x3 (R1-R3). Click a tile = the picker opens over the stage (red outline on the active tile); ESC or click again closes it.
  - **Right cluster:** **3 party slots** in a row (leader always leftmost, red top line; PROMOTE / KICK for the leader, LEAVE PARTY for members; open slots = INVITE). Under them: round **hanger** button (skin catalog) | **PLAY** block (red = queue, white = cancel + search timer, grey = waiting / deploying; was called START) | round **+** button (invite page). The round buttons get a red ring while their page is open.
  - Pages (mode info, invite, pickers, skin catalog) open over the middle area and stop above the party row.
- **Lobby loading screen (v3.7, `LobbyLoading` in ReplicatedFirst):** black; BREAK flies in from the left and LINE from the right, they meet and the red slash draws in between them; under it one line says what is loading right now (CONNECTING TO THE LOBBY, LOADING PROFILE, LOADING LOADOUT, SUITING UP, LOADING LOBBY, BUILDING MENUS, RESTORING YOUR SQUAD, READY) with a step counter (n / 8) and a thin red progress line. Every wait has a time limit. **Exit:** the slash stretches into a seam across the whole screen, the words fly apart, and the black splits along the seam like two doors sliding open. Shown at least 2.6 s. **v5.10: the progress bar is CENTRED under the logo** - the red fill used to grow from the LEFT over an almost invisible dark track, so the lit part looked shifted left; now the track is visible (3 px, light grey) and the fill grows from the MIDDLE outwards, and once the words have flown in the logo is nudged so the middle of the words BREAK / LINE (not the slash) is on the screen centre, right above the bar + the status line (the door seam moves with it).
- **Invite popup fix (v3.7):** the PARTY INVITE box used to be parked at y = -150 of the 1920x1080 canvas, which is still on screen whenever the window is taller than 16:9 (letterboxed canvas), so an empty invite box sat at the top all the time. It is now hidden (`Visible = false`) whenever no invite is open, and a repeated invite with the same id no longer re-pops it.
- **Home HUD** (walking around the lobby): same static logo + souls + Essence (big, with names); bottom right = the same hanger | PLAY | + cluster; **v5.9:** CARD INDEX sits right above PLAY (20 px gap, was 54), the searching text moved above CARD INDEX. Icons: the **hanger hook is one connected curl** (it floated before); the shop button shows a **shopping cart** (the old bag read as a padlock); SKINS / INVITE labels under the round buttons are 15 px bold white (were 10 px dark grey).
- **Match HUD = Finals layout, polished:** same placement, but rounded segments, a white damage trail on the health bar, low-health pulse (red HP + glow line),
  team-colored tick next to names, popping ammo number, rounded selected gun slot, soft corner shading for readability. Top bar (`MatchClient`) is a rounded plate now.
- **Match HUD v5.9 (`MatchHUD` v3.5, `MatchClient` v2.1):** the dark corner shades behind the squad + item row and the dark fade behind the top display are **gone** (only the elements are left). Under the ammo count: the **weapon name** (the fire mode / sight line is gone, and the big name left of the ammo moved there). **Reload = a small ring around the crosshair** (54 px, fills clockwise from the top with `HUDReloadU`; two half-clips with rotating gradients) instead of the thin bar above the gun picture. Gun picture in the slot + every gun / gadget picture in the loadout docks, pickers and shop are framed with **`UIKit.FitViewport`** (fits the real silhouette into the frame's shape, centred, 10-12 % margin; before, long guns were cropped and gadgets off centre). Loadout dock captions (MOVEMENT / WEAPON / GADGETS / RESERVE in Breakline, MOVE / PRIMARY / GADGETS / RESERVE in the lobby) are bold white Oswald (were small grey mono).

### 5.6 Cosmetics: shop, locker, gun skins (v5.10, scripts `CosmeticsClient`, `CosmeticsServer`, modules `CosmeticsCatalog`, `GunSkins`)
- **What is sold:** player skin EDITIONS (5.4) and GUN SKINS. Free currency = **souls**, premium = **essence**. Prices are in the data modules (`Skins`, `GunSkins`); the server reads them, the client only displays them.
- **GUN SKINS (`GunSkins`, both games):** every gun is built from a small shared palette (`GunModels.Colors`), so a skin is a THEME that repaints all parts of ANY gun: body / metal / steel / wood roles + material + shine (+ `glow` = the steel parts turn Neon). 8 themes: souls - **EMBER** 800, **GLACIER** 800, **VERDANT** 900, **BONEYARD** 1,000; essence - **CRIMSON** 200, **ABYSS** 250 (glow), **GILT** 400, **VOIDLIGHT** 450 (glow). **Each of the 10 guns gets 4** (2 souls + 2 essence, table `GUN_THEMES`) = 40 skins, id `<gun>_<theme>` (e.g. `VX9_GILT`), and a skin belongs to ONE gun (`GunSkins.ById[id].gun`). Tested offline: every skin repaints 70-100 % of its gun. `GunModels.Describe / BuildViewmodel / BuildWorld` take the skin id as their LAST argument; `GunClient` (first person) and `ThirdPersonServer` (what others see) pass `GunSkins.Equipped(player, gunId)`; `Skins.NewFigure` takes `opts.gunSkin`. Adding a skin: put a theme id in `GUN_THEMES[gun]` (new look = new entry in `Themes`).
- **Saved data (`ProfileStore` v2.2, both games):** `SkinsOwned` (edition ids), `GunSkinsOwned`, `GunSkinEquipped` (`{ [gunId] = skinId }`), `Skin` (worn), `Streak`. Mirrored as attributes `SkinsOwnedJson`, `GunSkinsOwnedJson`, `GunSkinsJson`, `Skin`, `Streak`.
- **Server (`CosmeticsServer`, remotes `CosmeticsRemotes.Buy(kind, id)` / `.Equip(kind, id, gunId)`):** `Buy` checks a real item with a price, not owned, enough of ITS currency; souls via `Profiles.Increment`, **essence only via `Profiles.SpendEssence`** (saved to the DataStore before it returns true), then the item is added and saved at once; one purchase at a time per player. `Equip` needs ownership (the three base skins are always owned); `Equip("gun", "", gunId)` = back to stock. `LobbyServer`'s `SetSkin` now also requires ownership. On join, an equipped skin the player does not own is cleaned away.
- **COSMETICS SHOP (`CosmeticsClient`):** the **COSMETICS button sits on the left, right UNDER the SHOP button** (same look, gem icon). Tabs **PLAYER SKINS / GUN SKINS**. Every tab is **split in two halves**: the **ESSENCE SHOP** (premium, purple header + PREMIUM tag, the **big half on the LEFT = seen first**, 3 cards per row) and the **SOULS SHOP** (blue header + EARN BY PLAYING tag, right half, 2 per row) - both always on screen so the souls shop is easy to find. The GUN SKINS tab has a **gun filter row** (ALL GUNS + the 10 guns, starts on the gun you play with). Card pictures fill in one per frame (no hitch). **Click a card = the big PREVIEW** (a 960 x 832 3D viewer): **drag with the mouse / a finger to turn it every way** (yaw + pitch), **scroll to zoom**, it spins slowly until you grab it; right side: name, kind, description, which gun it goes on, the price + how many you have, **BUY (asks twice: BUY -> CONFIRM PURCHASE)** or, when owned, **EQUIP** (EQUIP ON M4A1 ...). The shop, preview and locker block walking (`LobbyMenuOpen`). ESC / BACK / click outside closes.
- **COSMETICS LOCKER (the hanger button next to PLAY, or TAB):** its **own full menu, not a page inside the play screen**.
