Two tabs: **PLAYER SKINS** (every skin you own: 3 base + editions; click = preview on the right, EQUIP; EQUIPPED tag) and **GUN SKINS** (every gun skin you own, **grouped by gun**: a header with the gun name + `ON THIS GUN NOW: <skin>`, a **STOCK card** to take the skin off, then your skins; each card says **FOR THE M4A1**, an equipped one says **EQUIPPED ON M4A1**; the big button says **EQUIP ON M4A1** / **BACK TO STOCK M4A1**). Nothing owned = a message + the **GET MORE IN THE SHOP** button. The play-screen label under the hanger now reads COSMETICS; the old skin picker page is unreachable (left in `LobbyClient`).
- **v5.11 changes (owner):** (1) **drag-to-turn works:** the viewer listened to `holder.InputBegan`, but the info panel frame was layered over the viewer and the lobby's first-person camera can keep the mouse locked; now every click is hit-tested against the viewer rectangle (`UserInputService`), the turn comes from how far the pointer moved (`Delta` as a fallback) and a **Modal button frees the cursor** while a cosmetics screen is open (scroll-zoom is hit-tested the same way). (2) The COSMETICS button only says **COSMETICS** (the "SKINS // SHOP" line is gone). (3) **No more made-up text:** skin taglines + descriptions and the gun "paint job" paragraph are not shown any more; cards say only facts (`BASE SKIN`, `OPERATOR COLOURWAY`, `FOR THE M4A1`); the PREVIEW label on shop cards is gone. (4) **COLOURWAYS:** the **locker's PLAYER SKINS tab lists only the 3 base skins** (`n / m COLOURWAYS` under each); select one and a **swatch row** under the 3D preview shows ORIGINAL + every colourway of that skin (owned = click to preview + EQUIP, not owned = LOCKED + price, click opens it in the shop). The **shop still sells every colourway as its own item** (names stay: ARCTIC, NIGHTFALL ...). (5) New locker tab **PLAYER CARD** and shop tab **PLAYER CARDS** (5.8). (6) `CosmeticsShown` Player attribute while a cosmetics screen is open (LobbyClient ignores ESC / TAB then); `CosmeticsOpen = "card"` opens the locker on the PLAYER CARD tab, `"shop"` opens the shop.
- **v5.12 changes (owner, `CosmeticsClient` v3):** (1) **One shop at a time, full screen:** under EVERY tab the open shop fills the screen (5 cards per row, 316 x 348) and the other shop is a **slim tab (92 px) on its side**: while the ESSENCE SHOP is open the SOULS SHOP is a tab on the RIGHT edge; click it and the souls shop covers the screen (short slide) while the essence shop becomes a tab on the LEFT edge; click that to go back. **The shop always opens on the ESSENCE SHOP.** The tab shows the currency icon, the shop name running down it and a chevron. (2) **BUG FIX - the preview closed after turning a model:** the press started on the viewer and the release landed on the dim backdrop (a button behind the panel), which counted as "click outside". The panel is now `Active` and the preview only closes when the press AND the release are both outside the panel and nothing was being dragged. (3) **No more blinking:** every page was rebuilt after each click (equip, buy, select ...) and every model picture was rebuilt one per frame, so all models on screen blinked out and back in. Now every figure / gun picture is built ONCE as a template and **cloned** after that (same frame, `thumbTpl`), the locker viewer is **kept** between refreshes (it only swaps its model when another item is selected, building the new model before removing the old one), and the player cards (`PlayerCards.MakeView` template cache) are swapped in the same frame. (4) **Showcase picker = three tabs:** click a showcase slot and the left side shows the slot header, then **PLAYER SKINS / GUN SKINS / CARDS tabs** (only the open tab's items, was one long list of all three) + an **EMPTY THIS SLOT** button. (5) The PLAYER CARD tab is now a **card editor** (5.8).
- **v5.13 changes (owner, `CosmeticsClient` v3.1):** (1) **BUG FIX - the card editor's resize corners and turn handle only moved the item:** the handles were found by maths on the mouse position against the card's `AbsolutePosition`, and the two do not share the same screen origin (Roblox top-bar inset), so a press on a small handle landed off it and counted as a press on the (big) item = move. Now the card, every item and every handle are real invisible buttons (Roblox decides what was pressed; handles sit on top, the topmost item next), the handle buttons are bigger than they look (28 / 32 card px to press), they are not children of the turned box (their spots are turned by hand), and a drag only uses how far the pointer MOVED (`anchor + movement / zoom`), never an absolute screen position. Mouse-wheel resize works while the pointer is over the card or an item. (2) **REPOSITION** button under every showcase slot (5.8).
- **v5.16 changes (owner Nov 8, `CosmeticsClient` v4, `CosmeticsServer` v4, `CosmeticsCatalog` v3):** (1) **PLAYER SKINS are called COSTUMES** in all UI text (shop + locker tabs, YOUR COSTUMES, BASE COSTUME, `COSTUME // ...`, the showcase picker tab, server messages). (2) **Gun skin wording:** under every gun skin card it says **`<GUN> SKIN`** (was `FOR THE <GUN>`), and the preview / locker line says **`<GUN> SKIN`** (was `GOES ON: <GUN>`); the big EQUIP ON <GUN> buttons are unchanged. (3) **Shop costume preview:** the empty space under the name says **`COLORWAY FOR <BASE COSTUME>`**. (4) **Locker GUN SKINS = one small TAB per gun side by side** (all 10 guns in a grid row like the shop's gun row, each with how many skins you own for it; starts on the gun you play with or the first gun you own a skin for); the selected gun shows `<GUN> SKINS // ON THIS GUN NOW: <skin>`, then STOCK + your skins (was: every gun stacked under each other). (5) New tabs **POSES** (5.10) and **TITLES** (5.11) in both the shop and the locker: shop tabs COSTUMES / GUN SKINS / POSES / TITLES / PLAYER CARDS, locker tabs COSTUMES / GUN SKINS / POSES / TITLES / PLAYER CARD (GET MORE IN THE SHOP moved right to x 1556).
- **v5.17 (owner Nov 8):** the shop costume preview says **`COLORWAY FOR: <BASE COSTUME>`** (one space, a colon right after FOR; `CosmeticsClient` v4.1). **SHOP, GADGETS + MOVEMENT tabs (`ShopClient` v1.3):** the small line under every item name (EXPLOSIVE, 2 CHARGES / AIR JUMP ...) is gone and the name is bigger (32 px) to fill that space; in the big preview the red `GADGET // ...` / `MOVEMENT // ...` line under the name is gone and **WHAT IT DOES** / **STATS** are 22 px bright headings (guns keep their sub line + `GUN // ...`). **SMOKE is now SMOKE GRENADE** (`LobbyCatalog` + `GadgetConfig`, both games; the id stays `SMOKE`).
- **Not built yet:** buying Essence with Robux (5.1), a rotating daily shop, gun skins on gadgets, the crown on the squad stage.

### 5.8 Player cards (v5.11, `PlayerCards` module, `LobbyClient`, `CosmeticsClient`, `CosmeticsServer`, `ProfileStore` v2.3)
- **What:** a wide card (840 x 236 on the 1920 canvas) per player on the lobby play screen: **background** (a banner) over the whole card, a dark fade on the left for readability, the **Roblox avatar headshot**, **display name** + @username, **LV n**, **PARTY LEADER** (red, red border) / **READY** (green) tags, and **3 SHOWCASE slots** on the right, each with a tiny version of something the player owns: a **player skin** (figure), a **gun skin** (the gun, turned to fill the slot) or a **power-up card** (the baked / drawn card). Other players see your card (attributes replicate); party members on another lobby server show the default card without a level.
- **Saved:** profile `PlayerCard = { bg = <banner id>, show = { 3 x "skin:<id>" / "gun:<id>" / "card:<id>" / "" } }` (mirrored as `PlayerCardJson`), bought backgrounds in `BannersOwned` (`BannersOwnedJson`). `CosmeticsServer` checks everything: `Equip("card", bannerId)` (free or owned), `Equip("show", slot, value)` (the skin / gun skin / card must be owned; the same item never shows twice), `Buy("banner", id)` (souls via Increment, essence only via `SpendEssence`). On join a background that is no longer owned falls back to SLATE.
- **Backgrounds (`PlayerCards.Banners`, all drawn from GUI frames in code, no uploads; animated ones use looping tweens, no per-frame code):** free - **SLATE** (default), **BREAKLINE** (red slash), **STARFIELD** (twinkling, animated); souls - **WALL PUSH** 600, **WATERCOLOUR** 800, **GALAXY** 900 (slowly turning), **SOULFIRE** 1,200 (ghost souls rising); essence - **EMBER** 150 (rising embers), **GILDED** 250 (gold bands + a shine sweep), **VOID RIFT** 350 (pulsing rift + floating gems). Prices are first guesses.
- **Editing:** cosmetics locker **PLAYER CARD** tab: your card big on the right, the 3 slots under it; with no slot selected the left side lists the **BACKGROUNDS** (owned = click to set, locked = price, click opens it in the shop); click a slot and the left side lists everything you can show in it (EMPTY SLOT, your PLAYER SKINS incl. colourways, GUN SKINS, POWER-UP CARDS). The shop's **PLAYER CARDS** tab sells the backgrounds (preview = your own card with that background).

- **v5.12 NEW LAYOUT + FREE SHOWCASE (owner Nov 7, `PlayerCards` v2, `CosmeticsClient` v3, `CosmeticsServer` v3):**
  - **Layout (840 x 236):** the background fills the card; a **small avatar (85 x 85, half the old size) top right**; **left of it the DISPLAY NAME (38 px) with a small `LV n` right next to it** and the **real Roblox `@username` right under the name** (right-aligned to the avatar); PARTY LEADER / READY tags **top left**; a soft dark shade in the top-right corner (behind the items) keeps the name readable on bright backgrounds. **No EDIT CARD button** (edit it in the cosmetics, PLAYER CARD tab). **No dark boxes behind the showcase items** any more.
  - **Free showcase:** each of the 3 items has a placement `{ x, y, s, r }` (centre on the card in native pixels, size 0.35 - 2.5 where 1 = the old 112 x 196 slot, turn in degrees clockwise). **Default = the three old slot spots** (centres x 520 / 642 / 764, y 118), so nothing moves until the player moves it. **The info always sits on top:** an item dragged under the name / avatar is drawn behind them. Slot 3 is drawn over slot 2 over slot 1.
  - **How it is drawn:** a power-up card turns with the GUI `Rotation`; a ViewportFrame cannot be rotated, so a skin / gun skin sits in a square viewport as big as its box's diagonal and the **camera rolls** instead (same look). Items are fitted with `PlayerCards.MakeView` (one template per item, cloned after that = no blinking).
  - **Saved:** `PlayerCard.pos = { 3 x { x, y, s, r } }` (inside the same profile key, so ProfileStore did not change). `CosmeticsServer`: new `Equip("place", slot, { x, y, s, r })`, clamped + rounded by `PlayerCards.CleanPlace` (x 0 - 840, y 0 - 236, s 0.35 - 2.5, r -180 - 180); its own 0.12 s rate limit so moving never blocks an equip; on join every placement is checked (odd values -> default spot).
  - **EDITOR (cosmetics locker, PLAYER CARD tab, right side):** your card at 690 px wide. **Click an item on the card** = it is selected (red box with 4 white corner squares and a round turn handle above it). **Drag the item = move**, **drag a corner = resize**, **drag the round handle = turn** (snaps to 0 / 45 / 90 ... within 5 degrees), **mouse wheel over the card = resize**. Click an empty spot of the card = deselect. **RESET SPOT** (selected item back to its default spot) and **RESET ALL**. A placement is sent when you let go (one request every 0.2 s, newest wins, retried on "Slow down"); until the server's copy comes back the card shows your local copy, so nothing jumps back. Picking a new item for a slot selects it on the card right away. The SHOWCASE slot buttons and the BACKGROUNDS list work as before (picker tabs: 5.6).

- **v5.13 REPOSITION + ANGLE (owner Nov 7, `PlayerCards` v3, `CosmeticsClient` v3.1, `CosmeticsServer` v3.1):**
  - **REPOSITION** button under each of the 3 SHOWCASE slots (PLAYER CARD tab; greyed out on an empty slot). It opens a big full-screen view of that item: **skins + gun skins** sit in a box with the shape of a showcase slot and are **turned ANY way by dragging** (the same feel and limits as the shop preview: all the way round, tipped up to `PC.PITCH_MAX` 72 degrees; the model is re-fitted to the box at every angle, so what you see is how it sits on your card). A skin is **just the skin** (no disc / pad - the showcase pictures never had one, only the shop viewer adds it). **Power-up cards** cannot turn like that, so they **lean a little** left / right / up / down (`PC.CARD_TILT` 15 degrees each way). **DONE** (bottom middle) saves the angle, **RESET ANGLE** (left of it) = straight, **CANCEL** (right) / ESC = leave without saving. The spot / size / turn from the card editor stay as they are.
  - **Saved:** two more fields in each placement: `{ x, y, s, r, yaw, pitch }` (degrees). Missing = 0 = the old look, so old saves are fine and `ProfileStore` did not change. `PC.CleanPlace(t, i, kind)` checks them (yaw wrapped to -180..180, pitch clamped to 72, cards to 15). `CosmeticsServer`: `Equip("place")` now keeps yaw / pitch (limit by the kind of item in that slot), and **putting a NEW item into a slot resets that slot's angle to straight** (the old angle belonged to the old item).
  - **How it is drawn:** a skin / gun skin = the same cloned template picture, the camera looks from the saved angle (`PC.ViewCam`, re-fitted only when the angle changes) and still rolls for the card-editor turn. A leaning card = `PC.TiltCard`: the card's **baked sheet picture** (`CardArt.SHEETS`) cut into an **8 x 8 grid of ImageLabels**, each piece placed where a perspective camera sees it (far side a touch darker), with the card's own live name / description / rarity text moved + scaled with the surface; a straight card stays the cheap baked card. A card that is not baked stays straight (the REPOSITION view says so). `CardArt` itself did not change, so **no card rebake**.
  - **Layout:** **LV** + the level number next to the name are a bit bigger (LV 14 -> 17 px, number 20 -> 25 px).

- **v5.19 NAME NEXT TO THE AVATAR + BIGGER TITLE (owner, `PlayerCards` v4.1):** the **LV n** now sits **LEFT of the display name** (the row is right-aligned: avatar, then the NAME right next to it, then LV n further left; before, LV sat between the name and the avatar and pushed the name left). The **title** above the name is **24 px** (was 17; the name row and the @username move down 6 px to make room).
- **v5.16 POSES + TITLE ON THE CARD (`PlayerCards` v4):** (1) a **COSTUME** in a showcase slot can **hold a pose**: cosmetics locker, PLAYER CARD tab, an **ADD POSE** button sits under REPOSITION for every slot that holds a costume (it reads `POSE: <name>` once one is set); it opens the poses you own on the left (+ NO POSE), each shown on that costume; pick one. Saved as `PlayerCard.poses = { 3 x pose id / "" }` (`Equip("cardpose", slot, id)`), cleared when a new item goes into the slot; an animated pose is shown at its picture moment. The REPOSITION view keeps the pose. (2) The **title** you wear sits **above your name** on your card (17 px, tier colour) while `PlayerCard.showTitle` is on (default on): **TITLE ON CARD: ON / OFF** next to RESET ALL, or SHOW ON MY PLAYER CARD in the TITLES tab (`Equip("cardtitle", bool)`). Both live inside the existing `PlayerCard` key.

### 5.10 POSES (emotes) (v5.16: `Poses`, `PoseServer`, `PoseClient` - both games; `CosmeticsClient` / `CosmeticsServer` / `CosmeticsCatalog` - Lobby)
- **What:** a pose is a set of R15 joint angles (`Poses` module, the same joint space as `Motor6D.Transform` on the real characters and the menu figures in `Skins.NewFigure`): **held still** (a real pose) or **animated** (a looping function of time). No animation assets.
- **The 10 test poses:** free - **WAVE** (animated), **VICTORY**, **ARMS CROSSED**; souls - **SALUTE** 300, **SHRUG** 300 (animated), **KNEEL** 400, **SIT BACK** 500; essence - **DAB** 120, **FLEX** 150 (animated), **HYPE** 200 (animated, jumping). Prices are first guesses. The angles were checked offline with a NumPy forward-kinematics script (salute hand at the brow, crossed hands past the middle, flex forearms straight up ...).
- **Cosmetics locker, POSES tab:** your poses on the left (ANIMATED / STILL, `SLOT n` if on the wheel); the right side shows the **selected pose playing on the costume you wear** (drag to turn) and the **POSE WHEEL** (6 slots in a circle, slot 1 at the top, clockwise, the same layout as in game); **click a slot to put the selected pose there** (a pose already on the wheel swaps with that slot), TAKE OFF THE WHEEL. **Shop, POSES tab:** buy poses (souls / essence); an owned pose has PUT ON YOUR WHEEL (first empty slot, else slot 6).
- **In game + lobby: T opens the wheel** (centre of the screen, dim behind it, each slot shows the pose on your costume + its number). **The direction of the mouse from the middle picks the slot**; click it, or press **1 - 6**. T / ESC / clicking the middle closes it. Breakline: only while the round is Playing and you are alive (the wheel sets `GunMenuOpen`, so guns / gadgets ignore input meanwhile).
- **Holding a pose:** the character holds it (or loops it) until you **move in any way** (walk, jump, crouch, slide, an ability) or **try to pull something out** (1 - 4, Q, E, F, R, G, C, Ctrl, a mouse click): the pose ends. While you pose your **gun / gadget is put away** (`GunClient`, `GadgetClient`, `ThirdPersonClient` read the character attribute `Pose`) and **the camera swings out to a third-person view in front of you** (move the mouse to look around you; walls pull it in), back to first person when it ends.
- **Network:** `PoseRemotes.Play(slot)` / `Stop()` -> `PoseServer` checks the pose is on YOUR saved wheel and owned, and (Breakline) that the round is Playing, you are alive, not carrying a box / soul, not reviving / climbing / wall-running / revealing / sliding; it sets the character attributes **`Pose`** (id) + **`PoseAt`** (server time). The server also ends a pose itself when the character really moves (> 4.5 studs/s flat or falling / jumping, after 0.35 s) or the rules stop holding. **`PoseClient` animates every posing character** on every client (blend in 0.2 s, hold, blend out 0.2 s to the rest pose); `MoveAnim` stands down while a character poses.
- **Saved:** `PosesOwned` (bought), `PoseWheel` ({ 6 x id / "" }; empty = `Poses.DefaultWheel` = WAVE / VICTORY / ARMS CROSSED) in `ProfileStore` v2.4, mirrored as `PosesOwnedJson` / `PoseWheelJson` (Breakline reads the wheel from the same profile). `CosmeticsServer`: `Buy("pose", id)`, `Equip("wheel", slot, id | "")`; on join the wheel is cleaned (owned only, no duplicates).
- **Adding a pose:** add a line to `Poses.List` (id, name, anim, price, `joints`, optional `fn(t, J)` + `still` = the picture moment) in BOTH copies of `Poses`.

- **v5.17 FIXES (owner Nov 8, `PoseClient` v1.1 + `ThirdPersonClient`, both games):** (1) **Lobby: the cursor stayed locked in the middle after a pose** - the pose camera set `MouseBehavior = LockCenter` every frame and never gave it back. Now it is locked only while you really look around (not while any menu / wheel / settings is open), freed the moment a menu opens and set back to `Default` the moment the pose ends. (2) **Match: your body turned its back to the camera whenever you swung the camera round** - Roblox first-person lock auto-rotates the body towards the camera; `Humanoid.AutoRotate` is now OFF while you pose (restored after), so you can orbit to the front. (3) **Match: your own hands were "crossed almost"** (the VICTORY pose looked right in the preview and for other players): `ThirdPersonClient.restore()` returned at once in first person, so the gun IK (created with Weight 1) stayed ON for your own character and dragged both hands to the gun grips as soon as the pose camera showed your body; `restore()` now always sets the IK weights to 0. (4) **Lobby: the pose did not show on the character** - the default Animator tracks are now stopped on a posing character every frame and the joints are written in `Stepped` AND again at render time once the pose is fully blended in; the Output prints one line per pose (`[PoseClient] NAME holds pose X: n joints found, m default tracks stopped`) - if the lobby pose still does nothing, send that line.
- **v5.18 FIX - LOBBY POSES STILL FROZE (owner: the camera swung out but nothing moved; Output `[PoseClient] ... holds pose victory: 0 joints found, 2 default tracks stopped`):** the places run with **`StarterPlayer.AvatarJointUpgrade`**, so a character's joints are **`AnimationConstraint`s**, not `Motor6D`s, and `PoseClient` only accepted Motor6Ds (0 joints). `PoseClient` v1.2 (both games) takes either class (both have `Transform`) and also searches the whole rig by name. **Check:** the line should now say `15 joints found`. (`MoveAnim` / `AbilityPose` never filtered by class, so they already worked.)
### 5.11 TITLES (v5.16: `Titles` module, `LobbyNameplates`, `PlayerCards`, `CosmeticsClient` / `CosmeticsServer` - Lobby)
- A short line of text in its tier colour that sits **right above your name** in the lobby (`LobbyNameplates`: the nameplate is taller, the name stays where it was) and on your **player card** (5.8).
- **v5.17 (owner: "make the title larger and longer, easier to see"):** the lobby title is now **32 px** (was 18) in a **560 x 130 px** nameplate (was 320 x 84; the name row did not move: it still sits in the exact middle), with a **long thin line in the title's tier colour on each side** that fades outward (`LobbyNameplates` v2.3).
- **List (`Titles.List`, first guesses):** free - ROOKIE, FIRST BLOOD; souls - BOX THIEF 400, WALL BREAKER 600, SOUL COLLECTOR 800, SHARPSHOOTER 1,000; essence - THE LINE 150, VOIDWALKER 250, UNBREAKABLE 400 (gold). Tier colours: free light grey, souls soul blue, essence purple, gold.
- **Shop, TITLES tab** (the preview shows the title over your LV + name the way it looks in the lobby). **Locker, TITLES tab:** your titles on the left, the same preview on the right, EQUIP / EQUIPPED, REMOVE TITLE, SHOW ON MY PLAYER CARD: ON / OFF.
- **Saved:** `Title` (equipped id, "" = none, attribute `Title`) and `TitlesOwned` (attribute `TitlesOwnedJson`) in `ProfileStore` v2.4. `CosmeticsServer`: `Buy("title", id)`, `Equip("title", id | "")`; a title that is no longer owned is taken off on join.

### 5.12 SETTINGS (v5.17: `Settings` module, `SettingsServer`, `SettingsClient` - BOTH games, identical copies)
- **Button:** a round **gear** (44 px, dark, red on hover, turns a little) in the **top left, right of the Roblox chat / microphone buttons**. **v5.18 (owner: it sat right on top of the Roblox buttons):** `SettingsClient.ICON_X` 176 -> **200** px from the left edge and **`ICON_DOWN` = 14** px below the middle of the top bar (bigger = lower); change those two numbers if it still overlaps on your screen. While the FPS + ping option is on, the readout sits right of it. Clicking it (or the dim area / CLOSE / ESC) opens the panel.
- **Panel (v5.18: TALL, owner: "more vertical than horizontal"):** a 660 px wide column in the middle of the screen (height follows the rows, ~960 on the 1920 x 1080 canvas) with the three sections **stacked** (CONTROLS, then AUDIO + VISUALS, then INTERFACE, a thin divider between them). (v5.17 had a 1560 x 580 strip with three columns side by side.) drag sliders / click switches, applied at once, saved after 0.5 s (a small SAVED shows), **RESET TO DEFAULTS** bottom left:
  | Column | Setting | Range / default | What reads it |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | CONTROLS | **MOUSE SENSITIVITY** (the sensitivity bar) | 0.10 - 2.00, default 0.55 (= `MatchConfig.MOUSE_SENS`); a small tick marks the default | `GunClient` (matches + training zone), `SettingsClient` (lobby, only once you changed it) - it multiplies your own Roblox sensitivity |
  | CONTROLS | **AIM SENSITIVITY** | 0.20 - 1.50x, default 1.00 | `GunClient` while aiming |
  | CONTROLS | **FIELD OF VIEW** | 60 - 110, default 75 | `GunClient` hip view (sprint / slide kicks still add on top; aiming keeps the sight zoom) |
  | AUDIO + VISUALS | **MASTER VOLUME** | 0 - 100 %, default 100 | `Sfx` - every sound it plays goes through the `SoundService.BreaklineMaster` SoundGroup |
  | AUDIO + VISUALS | **SCREEN SHAKE** | 0 - 150 %, default 100 | `GunClient` (the slide rumble) |
  | INTERFACE | **DAMAGE NUMBERS** | on / off, default on | `HitFX:Number` |
  | INTERFACE | **SHOW FPS + PING** | on / off, default off | `SettingsClient` |
- **Saved:** profile key `Settings` (only the values the player changed; `ProfileStore` v2.5, attribute `SettingsJson`), written by `SettingsServer` (`SettingsRemotes.Save(table)`, cleaned + clamped by `Settings.Clean`, one save per 0.4 s). Both places read the same numbers. `Settings.Get(player)` fills the defaults (`Sens` stays nil until touched, so the lobby keeps the normal Roblox feel).
- While the panel is open the mouse is free (Modal), `SettingsOpen` is set (`LobbyClient` ignores ESC / TAB, `PoseClient` frees the cursor), and in a match / the training zone `GunMenuOpen` is set so guns and gadgets ignore clicks.

### 5.13 TRAINING ZONE (v5.17: LOBBY only - `TrainingServer`, `TrainingClient`, plus a port of Breakline's gun / gadget stack)
- **What (owner):** a big area in the lobby. Any player physically inside gets access to everything they normally do in a match, can damage the other players inside and the things they place, **but only the effects and numbers show: nobody is hurt, there are no health bars.** They are in first person, the lobby UI is replaced by the in-round UI of what they have equipped right now, a button at the top leaves the zone, a second button picks any card they own to test.
- **The zone:** any BasePart in Workspace named **`TrainingZone`** (make it big; invisible + CanCollide off is fine; several parts work). `TrainingServer` checks each character every 0.15 s: inside = Player attribute **`InTraining = true`** (0.5 s outside before it clears, so jumping over the edge does not flicker). Leaving by walking out, the LEAVE button, or dying clears `InTraining`, `MatchCard`, `HotBarrelUntil` and the gadget in hand.
- **Leave button + entrance:** `TrainingClient` shows **LEAVE THE TRAINING ZONE** at the top centre; `TrainingRemotes.Leave` teleports the player on top of the Workspace part **`EntranceTrainingZone`** (put it OUTSIDE the zone; there is a 3 s grace so an entrance inside the zone cannot pull the player back in; without that part it warns in the Output and uses `LobbySpawn`).
- **Choose your card:** the second button (under the first) opens a picker with **every card you own** (the starter cards + packs / daily shop, not only the 32 of your deck): click one = it is tested (`TrainingRemotes.PickCard(id)`; the server checks you own it and stores it as the Player attribute `MatchCard`, which is what `CardEffects`, `GunServer`, `GadgetServer`, `GunClient` and `CardEffectsClient` already read in a match). Click it again or NO CARD to remove it. Cards that do nothing yet say NO EFFECT YET. **It does not change the saved deck and has no effect on a real match** (the in-round card roll still draws from the 32-card deck). GRAVE CANDLE needs souls, which only exist in a match.
- **Cursor:** first person locks the mouse, so **HOLD LEFT ALT** to get the cursor for the two buttons (the picker frees it by itself; a hint under the buttons says so).
- **How the match stack runs in the lobby (all the same code as Breakline, patched to be inert outside the zone):**
  | Piece | Lobby behaviour |
  |---|---|
  | `GunServer` (lobby copy) | works only while the shooter has `InTraining`; only players inside the zone, sentries / bots and models with the attribute `TrainingTarget` can be hit (NPCs cannot); the gun follows the loadout equipped right now (`LoadoutJson`); creates `BoxDamage` / `GadgetDamage` itself |
  | `GadgetServer` (lobby copy) | gadgets work only with `InTraining`; explosions / sentries / mines only affect players inside the zone; **placed shields only flash, they take no real damage** |
  | `Vitals` (lobby stub) | `Damage` only returns the number (+ glass cannon); a **virtual health pool** (250, +10 THICK SKIN, 4 s to refill; sentries use their own) lets `Vitals.Killed` report a **virtual kill** (kill banner, hit effects, HOT BARREL) - real Humanoid health never changes |
  | `ThirdPersonServer` (lobby copy) | the world gun (`TPGun`) exists only for players inside the zone (added / removed as `InTraining` changes) |
  | `GunClient` | stored in `ReplicatedStorage > TrainingClients` (does not run there); `TrainingClient` clones it into your character on entry and destroys it on exit (it cleans up after itself), then puts the camera mode, mouse, FOV, body and HUD back |
  | `MatchHUD`, `GadgetClient`, `ThirdPersonClient`, `CardEffectsClient` | run all the time in the lobby but only act in the zone (`MatchHUD`'s gui is enabled only while `InTraining`; `GadgetClient.blockedNow()` is true outside; the other two only touch characters with a world gun / a match card) |
  | `LobbyMotion` | stands down inside the zone (GunClient owns speed + camera) |
  | `TrainingClient` | hides `LobbyUI`, `ShopGui`, `CosmeticsGui`, `CardIndex` while you are inside |
  | new lobby copies | `MatchConfig`, `CardEffects`, `GunMath`, `ReloadAnims`, `Spring`, `HitFX`, `GadgetConfig` (now Breakline's) in ReplicatedStorage |
- **v5.18 FIXES + ADDITIONS (owner Nov 9, `TrainingClient` v1.1, `TrainingServer` v1.1, lobby `GunServer`, `GunClient`, `ThirdPersonClient`):**
  - **C0 spam on leaving:** `ThirdPersonClient` (both games) printed `Unable to assign property C0. Property is read only` hundreds of times the moment you walked out: when your world gun was removed it tried to reset the waist / neck / root joints, but with `AvatarJointUpgrade` they are AnimationConstraints whose `C0` is a read-only alias of `Attachment0.CFrame`; the error stopped it before it cleared its state, so it ran (and errored) again every frame. Now every joint write goes through `setC0` (Motor6D -> `C0`, AnimationConstraint -> `Attachment0.CFrame`) and the state is cleared first.
  - **Floating hands + gun:** `TrainingClient` destroyed the cloned `GunClient`, but Roblox stops a script that is being destroyed before its own `Destroying` handler can run, so the viewmodel was never removed. Now `TrainingClient` sets the attribute **`Stop`** on the script (GunClient cleans up on it), waits a frame, destroys it, and removes every leftover viewmodel (`Viewmodel` / `GadgetVM` / anything with an `Owner` value) from the camera, twice.
  - **First person + locked mouse after leaving:** switching LockFirstPerson -> Classic kept the zoom at ~0.5 studs, which Roblox still treats as first person (mouse locked in the middle). On leaving, the camera is pushed out to **12 studs** (`THIRD_PERSON_ZOOM`, via a short `CameraMinZoomDistance`), then the place's own zoom limits come back; mouse + cursor set back to normal again 0.1 s later.
  - **250 health:** inside the zone your Humanoid has the **match health** (`Vitals.MaxFor`: 250, **260 with THICK SKIN**), kept full (nobody is hurt in here); leaving puts the lobby **100** back (`TrainingServer.setMatchHealth`).
  - **The card is EQUIPPED:** the button is now **CHANGE CARD**; the card you click shows on the **HUD bottom right above the item row** (YOUR CARD / name / rarity, the same place + look as Breakline's `CardHUD`), the line under the buttons says `CARD EQUIPPED: <name>`, and the gun gets a **fresh life** (client gun restarted + `GunServer` state reset on `TrainingCard` / entering the zone), so DEEP POCKETS loads its extra magazine every time you test it. Cards that really work in the zone: THICK SKIN (260 health), DEEP POCKETS, HOT BARREL (needs a virtual kill), HOLLOW POINT + GLASS CANNON (bigger damage numbers), SCOUT'S EYE (red outline on players you hit inside the zone). EMBER HEART only changes the regen delay (nobody takes damage in the zone, so nothing to see) and GRAVE CANDLE needs souls (match only).
  - **CHANGE LOADOUT** button next to CHANGE CARD: a panel with the 8 slots (MOVE, PRIMARY, GADGET 1-3, RESERVE 1-3; click a slot, then an item you own). It saves your **real** loadout through `LobbyRemotes.SetLoadout` (the same rules as the lobby: no item twice in a row, a gadget is in a gadget slot OR the reserve, a gun is the primary OR the reserve; the server cleans it with `LoadoutRules`) and the zone follows at once (gun, gadget charges, movement ability).
- **v5.19 CROSSHAIR FIX (owner: "when I come out of the training area I still see the crosshair"):** every time the gun script restarted in the zone (walking in, CHANGE CARD, CHANGE LOADOUT, a respawn) it made a NEW `GunHUD` ScreenGui and the old one stayed (it is only removed on respawn), and leaving removed just the first one it found. Now `GunClient` destroys its own HUD when it cleans up (both copies), `TrainingClient` v1.2 removes every `GunHUD` when the gun stops and when you leave (twice). **Also:** the repo's lobby `ThirdPersonClient` was still the pre-v5.18 copy (without the `setC0` fix for the AnimationConstraint joints) - the new copy is Breakline's file + the lobby header line.
- **Known limits (untested in Studio):** the sentry only shoots players who are inside the zone; mobile gets the on-screen gadget buttons in the whole lobby (the keys / DPad / touch buttons are inert outside); Souls / revive / boxes do not exist in the lobby; the ALT cursor is a hold, not a toggle.

### 5.7 Win streak crown (v5.10, `Crown`, `LobbyNameplates`, `MatchServer`)
- **Streak = wins in a row in ANY mode** (Breakline 3v3 and 1v1): `MatchServer.endRound` does `Streak += 1` on a win and `Streak = 0` on a loss; **a draw leaves it**. Saved in the profile, mirrored as the Player attribute `Streak` (also sent in the result table as `streak`).
- **Lobby:** a **gold crown floats (bobs + turns) 4.5 studs above the head - clear of the nameplate - for everybody with `Streak >= 1`**, with the streak (`x2`, `x5` ... from 2 up) in small gold text at its **upper right, tilted -14 degrees** (a billboard offset in camera space, so it is always off to the top-right side). The crown is client-made on every player (`LobbyNameplates`), so everybody sees everybody's. **One loss and it is gone** (the attribute drops to 0). Gem tier: 1-2 wins ruby, 3-4 sapphire, 5+ amethyst + sparkles.
- **v5.12 (owner: "move the crown down, close to the head; the multiplier text is in the crown"):** the lobby crown sits **right above the head** (`CROWN_Y` 4.5 -> **1.45** studs over the head centre, just over a helmet / hood); while a crown shows, the **nameplate moves up to 2.95** (was 2.5) so it never covers the crown. The **xN text is sized in STUDS** (`BillboardGui` size 1.6 x 0.75 studs, `TextScaled`, offset 1.65 right / 0.62 up): its left edge is always ~0.85 studs right of the crown centre, outside the crown (in pixels it slid into the crown from far away). The Breakline win line-up crown is unchanged.
- **`Crown` (both games):** `Crown.Build(parent, { tier, anchored, scale })` = an 8-sided gold band, 8 spikes (4 tall with a gem), 4 band gems; `Crown.TierFor(streak)`. The same crown floats over the winners in the Result line-up (2.1b).


## 6. Weapon Modification (Gunsmith)
- A lobby area to modify guns with **attachments** — scopes/sights and similar (full attachment list TBD).
- **No weapon skins or charms yet** (later).
- Existing groundwork: sights are already modular attachments (see 7.3) — each gun lists which sights it accepts,
  optics mount on the gun's rail and hide the folding iron sights. The temporary Armory picker already toggles them.

## 7. Weapons

### 7.1 System (data-driven)
- **`WeaponConfig`** (ReplicatedStorage) — every gun's stats. Shared defaults in `W.Defaults`; each gun only lists what differs.
  Supports `FireMode` = Auto / Semi / Burst, `Pellets` (shotguns), `Cycle` = Bolt / Pump / Slide (animated + blocks firing),
  per-gun recoil pattern, spread, ADS speed/zoom, move-speed multiplier, equip time, shell look, fire-sound pitch/volume.
  Helpers: `W.Get(id)`, `W.TTK(cfg, head)`, `W.AvgInterval(cfg)`.
- **`GunModels`** (ReplicatedStorage) — every gun's model is built from parts in code, plus the **sights**. **v3.7:** every gun (with every sight it accepts) is one connected piece - checked offline with a box-overlap test (fixed: WASP grip + mag floated under the receiver, AK / ANVIL / LONGSHOT / HALO-50 / BREACHER stocks and guards had gaps, M4 buffer tube + charging handle, ANVIL bipod + rear sight, 8x scope rings). FAMAS is slimmer and shorter. Sizes: first-person `VM_SCALE` 0.78 (`GunClient`), world gun `GUN_SCALE` 0.8 (`ThirdPersonServer`), lobby figure `GUN_S` 1.25 (`Skins`). One description
  builds both the first-person viewmodel and the third-person world gun. *(Replaces the old GunModelBuilder + RifleModel.)*
- **GunModels v3 (v4.8, full remodel):** every gun was rebuilt. **No glowing parts any more** (the team-coloured Neon stripes are gone; `SetAccent` stays as a no-op). **Every gun has a real hand rest right where the left palm sits** (first-person `Points.Palm`, set with `k.foreAt(x, palmY, z)` instead of `k.fore`): M4A1 angled foregrip on a quad-rail handguard, AK wooden slab foregrip, FAMAS moulded finger-groove grip, WASP fold-out vertical grip, ANVIL tubular grip, LONGSHOT vertical grip, HALO-50 deep flat forend, BREACHER big ridged pump forend that wraps the tube, V9 / .44 a shelf under the grip for the support hand. (The old viewmodel dropped the left hand 0.4 studs below `Fore` - `DROP` = 0.513 gun units - which put the hand under every handguard with nothing in it; an offline check now fails the build if the palm is not on geometry.) **FAMAS** is its own gun now: one-piece olive bullpup body (stock block, receiver block, forend), carry-handle channel with bridges, finger guard, folded bipod legs along the forend, slotted flash hider. Detail on every gun (rails + teeth, port covers, selectors, pins, rivets, vents, barrel bands, muzzle slots, pad ridges, mag ribs). **Nothing floats:** an offline box-overlap test checks every gun with every sight (Iron, Red Dot, Holo, 3x, 8x): 0 loose parts (it found and fixed: V9 slide, FAMAS mag release, HALO-50 muzzle brake, the 8x scope bell, optics on the pistols). Aim points, mag / muzzle / eject points, groups (`mag`, `cycle`, `cover`, `aux`) and aux counts are unchanged, so every reload animation still works. **Hands (v5.6):** `GunModels.HandParts` = one plain mitten block per hand + a wrist block (the right hand covers the pistol grip, the left hand sits under the hand rest). The viewmodel sleeves are blocks (`Cyl` false).
- **`GunClient`** — one script runs every gun (fire modes, pellets, bolt/pump anims, optics, sniper scope overlay). Rebuilds instantly on swap.
- **GUN FEEL v3.4 (Nov 5, owner: "closer to Roblox Rivals ... only one weapon in their game has true recoil, lean into that")** - `WeaponConfig` + `GunClient` + `HitFX`. *(The "only one Rivals weapon has true recoil" line is the owner's, from an interview he heard; not verified by the AI. The design below does not depend on it - flip a gun with one word.)*
  - **Two recoil modes, one field `Recoil`:** `"Kick"` (default, 9 guns) = **the aim never moves**. The bullet goes exactly where the crosshair was when you pressed the trigger; the shot only kicks the viewmodel (`ViewKick` 1.3x, alternating left / right roll, long sprays kick a bit harder), flinches the camera (`Flinch` 1.6x) and pulses the FOV + the crosshair. Accuracy on these guns comes from **spread, bloom, moving and jumping** (unchanged numbers), not from fighting recoil. `"Climb"` (**the AK-47 only**, `KR47`) = true recoil: a fixed, learnable `RecoilPattern` (16 steps: climbs hard for ~6 shots, swings left, then right, settles; jitter 0.03) moves the real aim, you pull down to hold it.
  - **How the aim stays put:** `GunClient` keeps `visPitch / visYaw` = the purely visual part of the camera rotation (camera flinch, being hit, slide rumble). `aimLook()` takes that part back out of the camera look vector before every shot, so a flinch can never throw a shot. Only `climbOffset` (real recoil) is left in. Being damaged no longer moves your aim either. Server validation is untouched.
  - **Single-shot guns** (HALO-50, BREACHER, .44, LONGSHOT) used to climb 1.1-2.5 degrees per shot; they are `Kick` now (big flinch, no aim loss).
  - **Draw animations (`EquipStyle`): REMOVED in v5.6** (owner: do not use them). Guns now just appear in the hold pose (`EquipStyle` is kept in the config but nothing reads it).
  - **Other animation accents (all small impulses through the viewmodel springs):** sprint in = gun swings up into the carry, sprint out = drops back to the hip; ADS in = the sight settles onto the eye (+ a 3.5 degree roll arc on the way in / out); reload start = the gun dips and rolls, reload end = it snaps back up; crosshair gap pulses 3 px per shot.
  - **Hitmarker (already existed, v3.4 only softened it):** four thin 45 degree ticks at the crosshair (white body 2 x 7 px, gold head 2 x 9, red kill 3 x 12), pop in (1.35x / 1.7x for 0.08 s) and fade over the last 65 % of 0.11 / 0.16 / 0.4 s; computed per frame in `HitFX:_update`. Still predicted on the client at the moment of the hit (instant); the damage number + kill banner still come from the server's `HitConfirm`.
  - **Tuning:** `Flinch`, `ViewKick`, `EquipStyle`, `Recoil` live in `WeaponConfig.Defaults` and per gun. `GunClient` is the only script that reads them.
- **RELOADS v5.6 (Nov 5, owner round 3)** - `ReloadAnims`, `WeaponConfig`, `GunClient`, `GunServer`, `GunModels`:
  - **The mag is really held.** Every key can set `hm` (0..1: the left hand is **glued to the magazine**, the fist closes on the centre of the mag parts, measured in `GunClient` as `magCenter`) and `hf` (1 = the key's hand position is final). Before, the hand was dropped 0.4 studs under the handguard (`HAND_DROP`) while the mag followed the *un-dropped* key, so the mag floated in front of the hand. Now every reload key is a final position, the resting hand pose (`FO` lowered by `HAND_DROP`) is the first + last key (`ReloadAnims.HAND_DROP`), so nothing jumps when a reload starts / ends. Rounds / shells ride **in the hand** too (`HOLD` 0.1 above the fist).
  - **Mag guns are slower** (owner: "comically fast"): `ReloadTime` M4A1 2.9 s, AK-47 3.3, FAMAS 3.0, WASP 2.7, ANVIL 5.6, LONGSHOT 3.4, V9 2.1 (was 1.9 / 2.3 / 2.0 / 1.8 / 3.8 / 2.4 / 1.3). A mag swap always plays in full whatever is left in the mag (a mag gun is **not** proportional).
  - **Round-fed guns load only what is missing, one round at a time** (`RoundReload = { Open, Per, Close }` in `WeaponConfig`; time = `Open + missing * Per + Close`, `W.ReloadDuration(cfg, ammo)`, `W.RoundsToLoad`): HALO-50 0.8 + 0.55 / round + 0.6 (1 round = 1.95 s, empty = 4.15 s), BREACHER 0.5 + 0.5 / shell + 0.8 (1 shell = 1.8 s, empty = 4.3 s), .44 Magnum 1.0 + 0.42 / round + 0.5 (1 round = 1.9 s, empty = 3.9 s). `ReloadTime` of those guns = the empty-gun time (shop bar, third person). `GunClient.startReload` rebuilds the animation with the real round count; `GunServer` uses the same duration and ignores a reload when the gun is full.
  - **HALO-50:** bolt opens, each missing round is carried to a real **loading port** on the right of the receiver (`LoadPort` + a bigger `EjectPort`) and sinks into it, bolt closes. **BREACHER:** each shell goes up into the loading port under the receiver; the **pump forend is much smaller** (0.26 x 0.4, was a 0.5 x 0.5 slab); pump racks at the end. **.44 Magnum:** the cylinder swings out on its crane, only the **spent casings (= rounds fired)** are punched out, then **one round per chamber, one by one**, the cylinder flicks shut. No more six rounds in the hand at once.
  - **ANVIL (M249) feed cover:** the sight rail used to be part of the cover, so the cover swung up through the scope. The plate + rail now stay on the gun and only a **small right-hand flap** (hinged on the outer edge, opens to the side, 150 degrees) lifts: nothing mounted on top is in its way.
  - **Equip swing removed** (owner: "butt"): every gun appears at its hip pose with no swing / flip / twirl / heavy draw (`equipCF` stays identity). `EquipStyle` in `WeaponConfig` is now unused.
  - **ADS (owner round 1 + 3):** while aiming the viewmodel kick is cut right down (`ADS_KICK_SIDE`, `ADS_KICK_BACK`, `ADS_KICK_ROT`, `ADS_CAM_PUNCH`, `ADS_FOV_PUNCH` at the top of `GunClient`; the AK-47 climb is unchanged). **Pistols (V9, .44 Magnum): only the ADS pose changed** - `k.view.AdsEyeMult` 1.3 (gun further from the eye = smaller on screen) and `k.view.AdsDrop` 0.07 (gun lower on screen); the sight sits a little below screen centre and shots still leave through it (`adsDropAng` is added to `shotShift`). The **hip pose is back to the original** (`X 0.45, Y -0.8, Z -1.3`). Lower `AdsDrop` / raise `AdsEyeMult` per gun in `GunModels`.
  - **Not tested in Studio.** The animation maths was run offline in a Luau stand-in (no errors, hand + round positions sampled); the feel is a guess. Tune: `Open` / `Per` / `Close` and `ReloadTime`, `HOLD`, `TILT` (0.45) in `ReloadAnims`.
- **`GunServer`** — validates every shot per weapon (fire rate incl. bursts, ammo, pellets, range, line of sight). Shotgun pellets
  are summed into **one** damage number per target. Owns the equipped gun as Player attributes `Weapon` + `Sight` (remote `SetLoadout`).
- **`ThirdPersonServer/Client`** — world gun rebuilds when the player swaps; IK hands follow the new grip points.
- Health = 100. Hitscan for all guns (for now).

- **v5.18 FIRST-PERSON HANDS + ARMS (owner Nov 9, `GunModels` v3.2, `GunClient`, `Skins.ViewArms`, both games):** each hand is **ONE block** (`GunModels.HAND_SIZE` 0.3 x 0.3 x 0.36; the extra wrist block that looked like a thumb is gone), **both hands are the same size** (the right one used to be about twice the left: 0.42 x 0.4 x 0.44), the forearms are **thicker** (0.32 -> 0.42, cuffs 0.46; they looked like sticks at FOV 110), and hands + sleeves + cuffs use the **colours of the costume you wear** (`Skins.ViewArms(skinId)`: OPERATOR glove / jacket, SOULBOUND wraps / coat, CHASSIS graphite / steel, colourways included; the viewmodel is rebuilt when your costume changes). `GunModels.BuildViewmodel(..., skinId, arms)` takes the colours as a new last argument.

### 7.2 Adding a new gun (checklist)
1. `WeaponConfig`: add `W.Weapons.MYGUN = { Name, Class, Inspiration, Sights, DefaultSight, ...stats }` and add `"MYGUN"` to `W.Order`.
2. `GunModels`: add `Models.MYGUN = function(k) ... end` using the kit (`k.box`, `k.cylZ`, `k.ring`, `k.inlay`, `k.teeth`…). It **must** call
   `k.grip`, `k.foreAt` (v4.8, replaces `k.fore`: the left palm point, the model must have a hand rest there), `k.magwell`, `k.muzzle`, `k.eject`, `k.mount`, `k.irons`. Iron-sight parts go after `k.tag = "Iron"`.
   Mag parts after `k.group = "mag"`, bolt/pump/slide after `k.group = "cycle"`. Or reuse a model: `Model = "VX9"` in the config.
3. Done — it shows up in the Armory picker automatically with its stats and TTK.

### 7.3 Sights
| Sight | Zoom | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Iron | gun's ADS FOV | Built into each gun (unique per gun) |
| Red Dot | gun's ADS FOV | Small tube, dot reticle |
| Holo | gun's ADS FOV | Open window, ring + dot reticle |
| 3x (Ranger) | 3x | Short scope, chevron reticle; aim sensitivity scales with zoom |
| 8x (Sniper) | 8x | Full-screen black scope overlay; gun model hides while scoped |
- Aiming fully hides the hip crosshair (it is gone by ~1/3 of the way into ADS).
- **ADS fit (v3.7):** irons / red dot / holo sit closer to the eye (`ADS_EYE_MULT` 0.7, min `ADS_EYE_MIN` 0.22 studs, top of `GunClient`), so you look down the gun instead of seeing its whole length out in front of you. 3x / 8x keep their eye distance. Lower `ADS_EYE_MULT` = gun closer to your face. **v5.6:** per-gun `k.view.AdsEyeMult` / `k.view.AdsDrop` (see 7.1 RELOADS v5.6): the pistols use 1.3 / 0.07 so they stay small and low while aiming.

### 7.4 Roster v1 (all tuning is a first pass — to be balanced in playtests)
Each gun is inspired by a real gun + its counterparts in **The Finals** and **Rivals** (researched Oct 24, 2026).
Reference points: Finals AKM 21 dmg/617 RPM, FCAR ~530 RPM, XP-54 16 dmg/880 RPM, LH1 44 dmg semi, SR-84 118/236 dmg,
M26 Matter 11 pellets, V9S 38 dmg; Rivals AR, Burst Rifle (3-round), Uzi, Shotgun (10 pellets), Sniper (3x headshot), Revolver.

| ID | Name | Class | Real / Finals / Rivals | Dmg | Rate | Mag | Reload | TTK body / head | Sights |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| VX9 | M4A1 | AR (auto) | M4 / FCAR, ARN-220 / Assault Rifle | 18 (1.6x) | 650 | 30 | 2.9s | 0.46s / 0.28s | Iron, Red Dot, Holo, 3x |
| KR47 | AK-47 | AR (auto) | AKM / AKM / AR (heavier) | 22 (1.5x) | 540 | 30 | 3.3s | 0.44s / 0.33s | Iron, Red Dot, Holo |
| HX3 | FAMAS | Burst rifle | FAMAS / FAMAS / Burst Rifle | 22 (1.55x) | 3-burst | 24 | 3.0s | 0.50s / 0.13s | Iron, Red Dot, Holo, 3x |
| WASP | WASP | SMG (auto) | MP7, Vector / XP-54, M11 / Uzi | 14 (1.4x) | 900 | 40 | 2.7s | 0.47s / 0.33s | Iron, Red Dot, Holo |
| ANVIL | ANVIL | LMG (auto) | M249, M60 / M60, Lewis / Minigun role | 17 (1.4x) | 620 | 80 | 5.6s | 0.48s / 0.39s | Iron, Red Dot, Holo, 3x |
| LONGSHOT | LONGSHOT | Marksman (semi) | M14 EBR / LH1 / — | 38 (1.75x) | 260 | 15 | 3.4s | 0.46s / 0.23s | Iron, Red Dot, 3x |
| HALO50 | HALO-50 | Sniper (bolt) | AWM / SR-84 / Sniper | 85 (2x) | 48 | 5 | 4.15s empty (0.8 + 0.55 / round + 0.6) | 1.25s / one-shot head | 8x, 3x, Iron |
| BREACHER | BREACHER | Shotgun (pump) | Remington 870 / M26 Matter / Shotgun | 9x10 | 80 | 6 | 4.3s empty (0.5 + 0.5 / shell + 0.8) | one-shot point blank | Iron, Red Dot |
| V9 | V9 | Pistol (semi) | Glock 17 / V9S / Handgun | 24 (1.6x) | 420 | 15 | 2.1s | 0.57s / 0.29s | Iron, Red Dot |
| MAGNUM | .44 Magnum | Revolver (semi) | S&W M29 / R.357 / Revolver | 45 (1.6x) | 140 | 6 | 3.9s empty (1.0 + 0.42 / round + 0.5) | 0.86s / 0.43s | Iron, Red Dot |

Balance notes: autos cluster around **~0.45s body TTK** (matches the ~0.5s TTK proposal); trade-offs come from range falloff,
**Recoil column (v3.4):** every gun is `Kick` (aim never moves) **except the AK-47 = `Climb` (true recoil)**. With no climb to fight, the 9 Kick guns are laser-straight at the crosshair, so their spread / bloom / movement numbers are now the whole accuracy model: watch for SMG / M4 sprays being too easy at range in playtests (levers: `BloomPerShot`, `BloomMax`, `FalloffStart`, `FalloffMin`).
mobility, handling, mag size and recoil. Damage falloff makes SMG/shotgun weak at range; DMR/sniper/burst reward headshots.
Weapon move-speed: SMG 1.06, pistols ~1.05, LMG/sniper 0.9.

### 7.5 Weapon choice (v3.1)
- The gun you play with is **only** the one picked in the lobby loadout (WEAPON slot). The temporary GUNS [G] picker (`GunPicker`) and
  the open `SetLoadout` remote in `GunServer` are **removed**. Sight = the gun's `DefaultSight` until the Gunsmith exists (section 6).

### 7.6 Hit flash (v5.7, `HitFX`)
Every bullet that lands on an enemy makes the target **pop white** (white fill + white outline, `HIT_FLASH.WhiteTime` 0.05 s) and then **wash red** (red fill starting at transparency 0.55, `RedTime` 0.2 s fade; headshots start stronger at 0.4). One `Highlight` per target (DepthMode Occluded, so it does not show through walls), a token cancels an older fade when a new hit lands (900 rpm guns stay crisp). It runs on the **shooter's** screen only (it is driven by `HitFX:Hit`, called from the client hit loop). Tune the numbers in `HIT_FLASH` at the top of `HitFX:_hitFlash`. The kill flash (red) is unchanged.

## 8. Movement (base kit, everyone has it)
Goal: fast-paced and fluid. All speeds are multiplied by the gun's move-speed (7.4).
- **Walk 17 / sprint 32 / crouch 9** (v5.18: 16 / 30 -> 17 / 32, owner: "a bit faster"; sprint was 24 before v3). Speeds live at the top of `GunClient` (matches + training zone) and `LobbyMotion` (lobby).
- **v5.18 (owner: "make jumps carry you a bit further and make slides actually gain momentum", `Movement` v3.4, both games):** sprint-jump boost **3 -> 5** (`SPRINT_JUMP_BOOST`), fall gravity **1.25 -> 1.1** (`FALL_GRAVITY_MULT`, a little more hang time; jump height stays 4.6 so mantle heights do not change); slides: start boost **6 -> 8**, start cap **40 -> 44**, and on flat ground a slide now **speeds up by 24 studs/s per second for its first 0.45 s** (`SLIDE_FLAT_GAIN` / `SLIDE_GAIN_TIME`, up to `SLIDE_GAIN_CAP` 50; downhill still goes to 58), flat slide time **0.95 -> 1.1 s**, slide-jump keeps **100 %** (was 95 %).
- **Sprint:** hold Shift. **Mobile:** "Sprint" button above the jump button; **gamepad:** click the left stick. Both are tap-to-toggle
  (button turns accent-colored while on) and switch off when you stop moving. Sent to `Movement` as the `SprintToggle` attribute.
- **Jump (momentum):** low and snappy, not springy — jump height 4.6 (Roblox default 7.2) and 1.4× gravity on the way down only.
  You keep your run speed in the air, so a sprint-jump goes far and flat (+2.5 forward speed when jumping at ≥ 24 speed).
  Air control: steer slowly (turn rate 3.2 rad/s), pressing backwards brakes, no input = keep drifting. Hitting a wall drops the
  momentum into it. Walking off a ledge works the same way. All in `CFG` at the top of `Movement` (JUMP_HEIGHT, FALL_GRAVITY_MULT, AIR_*).
- **Crouch:** C / LeftCtrl / gamepad B / mobile button while **not** sprinting. Hold to crouch (`CROUCH_TOGGLE` in `Movement` switches
  to tap-toggle). Lowers hitbox + camera, can't sprint while crouched, pressing sprint stands you up. Stays crouched under low ceilings.
  Replicated as the `Crouching` character attribute (third person shows bent legs + lean).
- **Slide:** same key while sprinting (speed ≥ 20). Small start boost (+6), steering, lowered hitbox + camera, buffered input
  (press just before landing or while still speeding up). **Momentum rules:**
  - Flat ground (under ~3.5°): keeps **all** momentum for ~0.95s, then blends smoothly back into a run (or crouch if C is still held).
  - Downhill: **gains** speed (scaled by steepness, cap 58) and the slide timer pauses, so long ramps = long fast slides.
  - Uphill: **loses** speed slowly (scaled by steepness); ends early if it drops below 14.
  - Sliding off a ledge keeps the momentum into the air.
- **Slide-jump:** keeps 95% of momentum (also works in the short blend right after a slide ends).
- **Mantle:** jump into a ledge (1.4–7.5 studs). With the lower jump, mantle is how you get onto anything above ~4.5 studs.
- **First person:** your own body + gear are always hidden locally (fixes seeing your body while falling).
- **Speed feel (subtle, tunable in `SPEEDFX` at the top of `GunClient`):** sprint FOV 82 (was 80), slide FOV +7, extra FOV when going
  faster than a sprint (downhill slides); quick FOV kick + dip when a slide starts; light camera rumble while sliding; faint speed lines
  at the screen edges — start at ~17 speed, about 60% strength at a normal sprint, full at ~38 (slides) — plus a soft dark edge
  vignette at speed; all hidden while aiming. Optional looping wind sound (`Wind` in SoundBank — empty = silent).

- **Animations (v4.1, `MoveAnim`, new LocalScript in both games):** real **walk, run (sprint), crouch (idle + shuffle) and slide** animations for every player, driven procedurally on the R15 joints after the Animator (no animation assets). Walk = small stride + arm swing; run = big stride with high knees, forward lean, pumping bent arms, steady head; crouch = deep knee bend, hunched torso, low shuffle; **slide = leaning back, front leg out straight, back leg folded under, arms out, head kept level, tiny speed vibration** (the old slide leaned the whole body forward and looked broken - `ThirdPersonClient` now leans back and its old leg poses are disabled). While holding a gun the arms stay with the gun IK; with no gun (lobby), a gadget, a soul, a revive or a box the arms are animated here. Your own crouch / slide / sprint is sent with the new remote `AbilityRemotes.Move` (`AbilityServer`) as character attributes `MvCrouch` / `MvSlide` / `MvSprint` so other clients see it.
- **v5.18 animations (`MoveAnim` v3.4, both games):** **run arms** (owner: "the hands move really really quick") - longer strides at a sprint (0.70 -> 0.56 rad per stud, legs + arms cycle slower at the same speed), and the arms have their own swing clock that runs **40 % slower than the stride** at a sprint (locked to the legs when walking), with a smaller arc and a calmer elbow pump. **Jump** (owner: "looks like something Mario would do to hit a pipe") - both knees gently tucked (the lead knee a bit higher), a slight lean, **arms low and out to the sides for balance** (no fist thrown over the head any more); on the way down the legs open and reach for the floor and the arms spread a little.
- **v5.19 STABLE BODY (owner: "the walk and run animation makes your character bob / bounce / tilt left and right every step, don't do that, have the player stable"; `MoveAnim` v3.5 + `ThirdPersonClient`, both games):** the body no longer bounces on every foot strike, rolls side to side or twists the shoulders against the hips (`BODY_BOB` / `BODY_ROLL` / `BODY_TWIST` = 0 in `MoveAnim`; `ThirdPersonClient` `BOB` 0.03 -> 0 and `STRIDE_TWIST_DEG` 3.2 -> 0 for the gun-holding body). The forward lean, the legs and the arm swing are unchanged.
- **Movement abilities (v3.8):** see section 3.3. `Movement` stands down (releases the slide / mantle / crouch) while `AbilityMode` is set; the abilities are only active when the matching one is in your MOVE slot.

## 9. Tech Principles
- **Data-driven:** stats in config modules (WeaponConfig, etc.).
- **Server-authoritative** for damage, ammo, fire rate; client does visuals and instant feedback.
- Damage numbers are shown from the **server-confirmed** value (HitConfirm).
- Parts players and bullets should pass through (e.g. the middle wall) need **CanCollide = false AND CanQuery = false**.
- **Wall Push map:** you build the map; `MatchServer` builds the gameplay pieces into `workspace.WallPush` at server start, fitted to the **`MapBounds`** part of the map folder that was picked (anchored, transparent, bottom = ground; maps = `ServerStorage > breaklineMaps / 1v1Maps > <name>_map`, see 2.1). Tuning for fitting is in `MatchConfig.MAP`.
- **Box look:** `BoxModel` (ReplicatedStorage) builds the box and animates the faces from box attributes (`State`, `Health`, `MaxHealth`, `PlugTeam`, `Draining`); `MatchClient` runs it.
- **Carrying:** Player attribute `Carrying` (bool). Any new gun, gadget or ability script **must refuse to work while it is true** (GunClient + GunServer already do).
- **Box damage:** `GunServer` validates a shot on a plugged box and fires the `ServerStorage.BoxDamage` BindableEvent (part, damage, shooter); `MatchServer` applies it.

### Sound bank (v5.7: `SoundBank` v2, `Sfx`, `MoveSounds`)
- `SoundBank` is split into sections (Global, Combat, Body, Movement, Abilities, Gadgets, Boxes, Death, Match, UI, Cards, Guns, Fallbacks). Every entry has a comment that says **when it plays** and **what kind of sound fits**; `[LIVE]` = a code hook plays it today.
- **Guns:** each of the 10 guns has its own block that lists only the slots the gun plays, in play order, with the real timing (reload times come from `ReloadAnims` x `ReloadTime`) and a character note (fire rate, how long a shot may be, what to do with `FirePitch` / `FireVolume`, how many variations to use). New per-gun slots: `DryFire` (trigger on an empty mag during the reload), `AimIn` / `AimOut` (sights).
- **New hooks (v5.7):** Footstep / Jump / Land / LandHard / SoulPickup / SoulDrop / ReviveStart / ReviveStop / Respawn (`MoveSounds`, both games); DoubleJump / MegaCharge / MegaLaunch / ClimbGrab / ClimbRelease / WallExhausted (`AbilityClient`); GadEquip / GadPutAway / GadThrow / GadPlaceTry / GadPlace (+ `_FRAG` ... per-gadget variants) (`GadgetClient`); SmokePop / SentryDeploy / SentryShot / MineArm / MineBeep / MineTrigger (new `GadgetServer` FX events -> `GadgetClient`); ReserveOpen / ReserveClose / ReserveSwap (`DeathScreen`); MenuOpen / LoadoutPick / UIHover / UIClick (`MatchFlow`, `LoadoutPicker`); CardTick / CardLand / CardRare / CardPick are now listed (CardRoll already played them). `Fallbacks` keep every new slot audible with an old sound where that makes sense (e.g. MenuOpen -> Notify) and silent otherwise.
- `Sfx.SECTIONS` lists the section names; a new section needs its name there. Roblox's built-in footstep sound is not removed (see `MoveSounds` header).

### Code layout (Rojo-style)
- `src/ReplicatedStorage/` — shared modules: WeaponConfig, GunModels, GunMath, Spring, Sfx, SoundBank, HitFX, **MatchConfig, BoxModel, BoxFX, DeathFX**
- `src/ServerScriptService/` — GunServer, ThirdPersonServer, AvatarService, **MatchServer**
- `src/StarterPlayer/StarterPlayerScripts/` — ThirdPersonClient, **MatchClient**, **MatchHUD** (v3.1; GunPicker removed), **MatchFlow (v3.6)**
- `src/ReplicatedStorage/TimeScale` (v3.6, ModuleScript: the slow-motion clock)
- `src/ReplicatedFirst/` — **LoadingScreen (v3.5, LocalScript)**; `src/ReplicatedStorage/UIKit` (v3.5, ModuleScript: shared colors/fonts, soul icon, the logo; **v3.7: in both games**)
- `src/StarterPlayer/StarterCharacterScripts/` — GunClient, Movement

- File endings: `.server.lua` = Script, `.client.lua` = LocalScript, `.lua` = ModuleScript

## 9.1 Two-game setup (Lobby + Breakline)
- One **experience** (Universe), two **places**: Lobby = start place, Breakline = second place. Same Universe so teleport + MemoryStore + DataStore are shared.
- Flow: Lobby PLAY -> MemoryStore queue -> matchmaker packs 3v3 -> `ReserveServer` -> roster in MemoryStore (`Matches`) -> `TeleportAsync` -> Breakline `MatchRoster` -> match -> `TeleportAsync` back to Lobby.
- Roster is trusted (server-written MemoryStore), never teleport data (clients can forge it).
- Lobby map/art, lobby spawn and shop UI are owner/TBD. Lobby is the future home of loadout, cards and shop (phase 6).

## 10. Roadmap
1. **Gun feel + weapon system** — built (10 guns, sights, test picker), not playtested
2. **Movement** — built (incl. crouch + momentum slide + speed effects), not playtested
3. Gray-box map + wall/box server logic — **built v1 (not playtested)**, section 2.1
4a. **Lobby, parties, queue, shared profile — built v2.8 (not playtested)**
4. Teams, rounds, respawn, match HUD — **basic version built** (teams, rounds, respawn, HUD); match timer/overtime, scoreboard, team select still to do
5. Polish, mobile, playtests
6. **Meta systems:** loadout + reserve, card deck + slot roll (**v4.3: built with placeholder cards, abilities not wired**), coins/missions, shop, gunsmith, special movement abilities
   *(order within this phase TBD; 1v1/2v2 modes can come with phase 4)*

**Changelog**
- **Nov 10 (v5.19):** **METEOR SOUL DROP + FIXES (files: code-layout line 'v5.19 changed'). luau-compile OK on every changed file, luau-analyze finds no unknown names (only Roblox built-ins); NOT tested in Studio (the AI cannot run Roblox).** Owner asked for: (1) the crosshair stays on screen after leaving the training zone -> 5.13 (old GunHUDs were never removed); (2) sentry / prox mine / bubble shield could not be placed -> 3.4 (the v5.18 cook change broke the click); (3) place distance 60 -> 20 studs, the see-through preview also only reaches 20 -> 3.4; (4) the 20 s loadout screen back to a black space background -> 2.1b; (5) a bigger loadout dock at the bottom -> 2.1b; (6) no bob / bounce / side tilt while walking and running -> 8; (7) 3,000 souls for new players after their starter pick -> 5.1; (8) player card: LV left of the name so the name sits right next to the picture, a bigger title -> 5.8; (9) replace the painting / fly-through / flash with a soul that drops from space above the spawn, burning like a re-entering rocket, anime-drawn look, landing on the spawn, then the respawn animation, card roll when the camera wooshes in -> 2.1b Warp. **Cards unchanged -> NO card rebake.** **Check first:** (a) the drop on your map: does the space shot show the map below (`SD.HEIGHT` 720 - lower it if the map is too small / the sky box looks odd up there), is the chase readable (`back` / `side` numbers in the chase camera), does the camera stop in the right spot before the impact; (b) the ink outlines on the fire (Highlights: Roblox shows at most 31 at once - 2 per dropping player + 1 per impact); (c) the anime layer strength (speed line count / transparency in `drawArt`, `SD.ANIME_FPS` 12; 24 = smoother, 8 = choppier); (d) the impact word shows (`SD.IMPACT_TEXT`; boxes = change it to "BOOM!"); (e) the respawn reveal ends as the card roll starts - if the card roll starts too early / late, change `SD.REVEAL_PAD` or `C.WARP_TIME`; (f) placing a sentry / shield / mine at 5, 15 and 21 studs (21 should be red); (g) walk + run in third person (lobby + match) for any leftover wobble; (h) leaving the training zone after CHANGE CARD a few times: no crosshair; (i) a fresh test account: 3,000 souls after the starter pick.

- **Nov 8 (v5.15):** **LOADING SCREEN FIX (files: code-layout line 'v5.15 changed'). luau-compile OK, luau-analyze: `t0` was the ONLY unknown non-Roblox global in all of `src/` + `lobby/src/` and it is gone; the old and new `LoadingScreen` were run in an offline Roblox stand-in (virtual clock, the server's real Waiting -> LOAD_HOLD -> AllReady -> Pregame timeline): the OLD file errors with `LoadingScreen:253: attempt to perform arithmetic (sub) on number and nil` at the switch to Pregame and the screen is never removed (= the owner's bug); the NEW file removes it 1.2 s into Pregame, the watchdog removes it when the `ClientReady` remote is missing, and a forced error in the wait loop keeps the picture until Pregame and then removes it; NOT tested in Studio (the AI cannot run Roblox).** Owner: "when loading into a game the loading screen never goes away, it just stays there even after the 15 seconds have passed and everyone's loaded in, the game goes on without you". Cause: the v5.10 `MIN_SHOW` check used `t0`, which only existed inside the logo fade task (2.1b). Fix: `shownAt`, pcall around the loop, watchdog (`FAILSAFE` 3 s), time limit on `ClientReady`. Server (`MatchServer`) unchanged - it was already moving on correctly. **Cards unchanged -> NO card rebake.** **Check first:** the screen fades ~1.2 s after the Output / clients enter Pregame; if you ever see `[Breakline] LoadingScreen watchdog closed the loading screen` or `LoadingScreen wait loop error` in the client Output, send it to the AI (the screen still closes, but it means something else went wrong).
- **Nov 7 (v5.14):** **CARD SHOP + STARTER CARDS + 8 WORKING CARDS + UI FIXES (files: code-layout line 'v5.14 new / changed'). luau-compile OK on every changed file, luau-analyze finds no unknown names (only Roblox built-ins); the CARDS page, the EYE view and two full pack openings (one with SKIP ROLL) ran without errors in an offline Roblox GUI stand-in; pack odds (20,000 test rolls each), the daily pick (1,000 days: always 3 different cards, the same for the same day) and the starter deck (32 cards, valid) checked in plain Luau; (2) a **CARDS tab** in the shop with its own layout: **DAILY SHOP** (3 decently sized cards, every 24 h, only from the cards in the packs on sale, picked by rarity) + **CARD PACKS** (3 per row, own artwork, BUY + an EYE button with every card and its chance), **souls only** -> 5.9; (3) three packs: **FIRST STEPS** (beginner, cheapest), **LEVEL UP** (advanced, most expensive), **SOUL PACK** (every rarity, middle price), 12 cards each, placeholders for now -> 5.9; (4) the **opening animation** (dark + spotlight, anime flash rips the top, circular roll left -> right, lands, big card, CLAIM only, SKIP ROLL while rolling) -> 5.9; (5) **bigger DECK text** above 32 / 32 in the Card Index -> 4.1; (6) the **pre-game loadout menu kicked you out every countdown second** -> fixed (3.1); (7) the **play-screen CARD INDEX button**: card drawing further right + up, text moved over, bigger DECK line -> 5.5. **Not changed on purpose:** Scout's Eye stays **Uncommon** (the owner listed it as rare; moving it changes its baked card - ask if wanted). **NO card rebake:** the 32 baked cards did not change (only the Deep Pockets text, and text is live on top of the sheets); the new PLACEHOLDER card is drawn live like any card that is not on the sheets. **Untested guesses to check first:** (a) the opening's CanvasGroups (the pack top + body) look sharp on your screen; (b) the circular roll speed / length (`DUR` 5.4, `TURNS` 4, `RX` 640 in `CardShopUI.openPack`); (c) the spotlight strength (strip transparency ~0.93, pool 0.78 - 0.9); (d) the daily timer counts to 00:00 UTC (that is 7 PM / 8 PM New York time); (e) Deep Pockets: the HUD shows e.g. 60 / 30 until the first reload - and if the very first shot after the card pick is refused, tell the AI (client + server both hand out the extra magazine); (f) Thick Skin: MatchHUD / OverheadBars already follow `MaxHealth`; (g) Grave Candle: the candle ball rides exactly on the soul part (the visible soul is drawn by SoulClient around it); (h) Scout's Eye uses one Highlight per marked enemy (Roblox shows at most 31 Highlights at once, with the team outlines); (i) existing players who showed a now-unowned card on their player card keep it shown until they change it.

(6) **wall climb: the stomach must stay on whatever wall / ceiling you are on**, also when switching to a wall at another angle or onto a ceiling (3.3). **Cards unchanged -> NO card rebake** (the leaning card is cut from the existing baked sheets at run time). **Untested guesses to check first:** (a) the 8 x 8 leaning card: look for thin seams between the pieces (they overlap by 0.8 px; raise that or `TILT_N` in `PlayerCards` if you see lines) and for the text sitting right on its plates at full lean; (b) the perspective strength (`TILT_D` 3.2: smaller = stronger); (c) the item hit buttons follow a turned item with Roblox's own (rotated) hit area - if a turned item is hard to grab near its corners, tell the AI; (d) the REPOSITION drag speed (cards 0.12 degrees / px, models 0.63 / 0.46 like the shop); (e) climbing: `WC_HOLD` 1.3 - if the chest / knees push into the wall and jitter, raise it to 1.45; if you still float, lower to 1.15; (f) corner feel: `WC_CORNER_BLEND` 2.6 (bigger = a wider, earlier curve); (g) coming down from a ceiling you arrive head first and roll upright on the wall (`WC_TURN_WALL` 3.2 rad/s) - tell the AI if you would rather stay head-down; (h) going round the side edge of a wall now wraps you onto the side face - check it on thin walls / pillars; (i) the icons at the small HUD size (the figure limbs are at least 2 px).

the three outfits + 8 editions were run offline through a Roblox maths stand-in (one connected group, no part under 0.03 studs, overhang <= 0.35, 45 degree turns only); every gun skin was run offline through the real `GunModels.Describe` (70-100 % of the parts repainted); - **Nov 3 (c):** **v4.7 - shop, starter pick, dash, 250 health, nicer guns (Breakline + Lobby). Syntax-checked with luau-analyze only, NOT playtested in Studio.** (1) **Health:** the shield is gone. Everybody has **250 white health** (`Vitals` v2: `MAX_HEALTH` 250). Health does not come back until the player went **12 s without taking damage** (`REGEN_DELAY`), then it refills at 20 / s (`REGEN_PER_SEC`); Roblox's default 1 % regen `Health` script is removed. `MatchHUD` v3.4 (shield bar removed) and `OverheadBars` v2: the bar over a shot player now stays **10 s** (`SHOW_FOR`) and shows health only. (2) **Crouch / slide clipping:** `MoveAnim` v3.1 measures the lowest part of the posed body every frame against the floor under the root and lifts the body until nothing sinks in (integrator, no jitter). **Slide wobble:** the sine shake on the hips / right arm and the 5.5 Hz body sway are removed, and `ThirdPersonClient` damps the gun's turn-lag while sliding (it swung the right hand when steering). (3) **DASH** (new movement ability, `AbilityClient` v2.1 + `UIKit` icon + `Movement` v3.3): press **F** (gamepad Y) to dash in the direction you move (look direction when standing still). 3 charges, every used charge needs **10 s** to come back, one after the other (`DASH_CHARGES`, `DASH_RECHARGE`, `DASH_SPEED` 92, `DASH_TIME` 0.24, `DASH_EXIT` 34). HUD: icon + 3 charge pips + count. `Movement` stands down while the character attribute `Dashing` is set and carries the speed into the air afterwards. (4) **SHOP (Lobby):** new `ShopServer` (Script), `ShopClient` (LocalScript), `LoadoutRules` (ModuleScript, both places). Currency = **Souls**. A **SHOP button** sits on the left of the screen (halfway down) and a **shop NPC** opens it too: place a Model named `ShopNPC` (or tag it `ShopNPC`) in the Lobby workspace, `ShopServer` adds the E prompt by itself (use your own `ProximityPrompt` named `ShopPrompt` inside the model to override). Tabs **GUNS / GADGETS / MOVEMENT**; click an item and the right panel shows real gun stats (from `WeaponConfig`) or what a gadget / movement ability does + its numbers (`GadgetConfig`, `LobbyCatalog.MoveStats`). BUY asks twice (BUY, CONFIRM). Ownership is saved in the profile (`Owned`, mirrored as the Player attribute `OwnedJson`); the loadout pickers (lobby `LobbyClient` v5.5 and Breakline's pre-game `MatchFlow`) only list owned items, and `LoadoutRules.Clean` enforces it on the server (lobby SetLoadout and Breakline pre-game loadout). **Prices live in `LobbyCatalog.Price`:** guns V9 500, WASP 800, M4A1 900, AK-47 1000, FAMAS 1100, .44 1200, BREACHER 1300, LONGSHOT 1500, ANVIL 1600, HALO-50 2200; gadgets Frag / Smoke / Flash 300, Mine 500, Shield 700, Sentry 800; movement Double Jump 700, Dash 900, Mega Jump 900, Wall Run 1100, Wall Climb 1100 (a match pays roughly 75 - 200 souls). (5) **New players:** a profile with `StarterDone = false` gets a full-screen pick: **WEAPON** (M4A1 / V9 / AK-47) and **MOVEMENT** (Double Jump / Wall Climb / Dash). They also always start with **Frag Grenade, Flashbang and Sentry** (no choice, `LobbyCatalog.Starter`). The two picks + the three gadgets become their owned items and their loadout. Queuing is blocked until the pick is made. Existing saves have no `Owned` list, so they also see the pick once (until then everything counts as owned). (6) **Gun models:** `GunModels` v2 adds a DETAIL pass to every gun (rails, vents, ribs, pins, levers, barrel bands, grain, rubber pads, slide serrations, mag ribs) and re-tones materials. Base sizes, proportions and every hand / mag / muzzle point are unchanged (bounding boxes grew by at most 0.03 studs), mag details live in the `mag` group and slide / bolt / pump / feed-cover details in `cycle` / `cover`, so the reload animations are untouched. **Files:** Lobby: LobbyCatalog (both), ProfileStore (both), LoadoutRules (NEW, both), ShopServer (NEW), ShopClient (NEW), LobbyServer, LobbyClient, WeaponConfig + GadgetConfig (copies of Breakline's, NEW in Lobby), GunModels (both), AbilityClient (both), Movement (both), MoveAnim (both), UIKit (both). Breakline: Vitals, MatchHUD, OverheadBars, MatchServer, MatchFlow, ThirdPersonClient.
- **Nov 3 (b):** **v4.4 - owner feedback on v4.3 (Breakline + Lobby). Syntax-checked with luau-compile / luau-analyze only, NOT playtested.** (1) **Card Index (lobby `CardIndex`):** the DECK panel keeps the 32 / 32 counter, white bar, rarity mix and AUTO FILL / UNDO CHANGES / CLEAR, but the middle list is now a **grid of mini cards** (5 per row, one per distinct card, red `xN` count pill, `-` button top right, click = inspect, right click = take one out). Collection cards lose the `x1` badge; under each card the red in-deck boxes stay and the text is only **OWNED n**, bigger; the `+` / `-` squares are 28 px (was 32). (2) **`CardArt` (both games):** the white shine sweep on RARE and up is removed; the name and description now use a measured fixed `TextSize` inside the card's `UIScale` (the old `TextScaled` + `UITextSizeConstraint` worked in real pixels, so the text never grew with the inspect card). (3) **Spectating:** new `SpectateHide` (StarterPlayerScripts) hides `MatchHUD2`, `CardHUD`, `AbilityHUD`, `GunHUD` while `IsDead`. (4) **Death time `DEAD_TIME` 25 s** (was 15; team wipe stays 10 s, 1v1 stays 3 s); revive was already 8 s (`REZ_TIME`). (5) **Sentry:** damage 7 -> 3.5, rpm 540 -> 480; it now really dies - its Humanoid has the state machine off so `Humanoid.Died` never fired; `GadgetServer` watches `HealthChanged` and checks health every frame. (6) **Health 200 + SHIELD 100:** new ServerScriptService ModuleScript `Vitals` (damage goes into the shield first; `GunServer` and `GadgetServer` call `Vitals.Damage`), character attributes `Shield` / `MaxShield` / `LastShieldHit` / `LastHit`. `MatchHUD`: thin blue shield bar above the health bar, both bars flash + shake when they lose points (the white ghost trail stays). New `OverheadBars` (LocalScript): slim shield + health bar over other players, shown ~3.5 s after they are hit, pops + flashes on damage, replaces Roblox's bar. No shield regeneration yet. (7) **`MoveAnim` v3:** new sprint-leap AIR pose (lead leg forward, trailing leg back, forward lean, arms pumping, legs reach down when falling) and a **tactical half-kneel crouch** (right foot forward, left knee down, front hand on the knee; low stagger while moving). The crouch body height is fitted to the real UpperLeg / LowerLeg lengths and the measured hip height so the feet no longer sink into the floor. (8) **`Movement` v3.2:** `AIR_DRAG` 0 and the take-off speed is kept in the air (steering never lowers it, only braking backwards or hitting a wall), `SPRINT_JUMP_BOOST` 3, `FALL_GRAVITY_MULT` 1.25, `CROUCH_HIP_FACTOR` 0.52. (9) **Sprint FOV** 82 -> 86 (`GunClient`). **Files:** Lobby: CardIndex, CardArt, MoveAnim, Movement. Breakline: CardArt, MatchConfig, GadgetConfig, GadgetServer, GunServer, DeathServer, Vitals (NEW), DeathScreen, MatchHUD, OverheadBars (NEW), SpectateHide (NEW), GunClient, MoveAnim (file `MoveAnime`), Movement.
